interface ParsedArgs { flags: Record<string, string | true>; positionals: string[]; }

function parseArgs(argv: string[]): ParsedArgs {
  const flags: Record<string, string | true> = {};
  const positionals: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    const a = argv[i];
    if (a === '--') { positionals.push(...argv.slice(i + 1)); break; }
    if (a === '-h') flags.help = true;
    else if (a === '-v') flags.version = true;
    else if (a.startsWith('--')) {
      const eq = a.indexOf('=');
      if (eq >= 0) flags[a.slice(2, eq)] = a.slice(eq + 1);
      else {
        const next = argv[i + 1];
        if (next !== undefined && !next.startsWith('--')) { flags[a.slice(2)] = next; i++; }
        else flags[a.slice(2)] = true;
      }
    } else positionals.push(a);
  }
  return { flags: flags, positionals: positionals };
}

function die(msg: string): never {
  process.stderr.write('tred: ' + msg + '\n');
  process.stderr.write('运行 tred --help 查看用法\n');
  process.exit(1);
}

function requireStr(flags: Record<string, string | true>, key: string): string {
  const v = flags[key];
  if (typeof v !== 'string') die('缺少或非法 --' + key);
  return v;
}

let __stdinOverride: string | undefined = undefined;

/** @internal 测试用：覆盖 stdin 内容，避免 readFileSync(0) 阻塞 */
export function _setStdinOverride(s: string | undefined): void {
  __stdinOverride = s;
}

function readContent(flags: Record<string, string | true>): string {
  const has = function (k: string): boolean { return flags[k] !== undefined; };
  const count = (has('content') ? 1 : 0) + (has('content-file') ? 1 : 0) + (has('content-stdin') ? 1 : 0);
  if (count === 0) die('必须指定 --content / --content-file / --content-stdin 之一');
  if (count > 1) die('--content / --content-file / --content-stdin 只能选一个');
  if (has('content')) return requireStr(flags, 'content');
  if (has('content-file')) return readFileSync(requireStr(flags, 'content-file'), 'utf-8');
  if (__stdinOverride !== undefined) return __stdinOverride;
  return readFileSync(0, 'utf-8');
}

async function buildFormatter(flags: Record<string, string | true>): Promise<Formatter | undefined> {
  if (!flags.prettier) return undefined;
  let prettier: any;
  try {
    const spec = 'prettier';
    prettier = await import(spec);
  } catch { die('--prettier 需要先安装 prettier: npm i -D prettier'); }
  return function (src: string, file: string): Promise<string> {
    return prettier.format(src, { filepath: file });
  };
}

function resolveFingerprint(flags: Record<string, string | true>, anchor: string): string {
  const hasFp = typeof flags.fp === 'string';
  const hasGen = typeof flags['fp-source'] === 'string' && typeof flags['fp-version'] === 'string';
  if (hasFp && hasGen) die('--fp 与 --fp-source/--fp-version 不能同时使用');
  if (hasFp) {
    const fp = flags.fp as string;
    if (!/^[a-f0-9]{8}$/.test(fp)) die('--fp 必须是 8 位小写十六进制，如 a1b2c3d4');
    return fp;
  }
  if (hasGen) {
    return makeFingerprint({
      anchor: anchor,
      source: flags['fp-source'] as string,
      version: flags['fp-version'] as string,
    });
  }
  die('必须提供 --fp，或 --fp-source + --fp-version 自动生成指纹');
}

async function cmdImpact(flags: Record<string, string | true | string[]>, positionals: string[]): Promise<number> {
  const target = positionals[1];
  if (!target) die('用法: tred impact <targetName> [--dir <path>]');
  const dir = typeof flags.dir === 'string' ? flags.dir : '.';
  const report = analyzeImpact(dir, target);
  console.log(report);
  return 0;
}

async function cmdSplit(flagsRaw: Record<string, string | true | string[]>, positionals: string[]): Promise<number> {
  const flags: Record<string, string | true> = {};
  for (const k of Object.keys(flagsRaw)) {
    const v = flagsRaw[k];
    flags[k] = Array.isArray(v) ? v[v.length - 1] : v;
  }
  const file = requireStr(flags, 'file');
  const name = requireStr(flags, 'name');
  const to = requireStr(flags, 'to');
  const targets = name.split(',').map(s => s.trim());
  const targetClass = typeof flags['class'] === 'string' ? flags['class'] : undefined;
  const res = splitFile(file, targets, to, targetClass);
  if (!res.ok) { console.error('❌ [tred split] ' + res.message); return 1; }
  console.log('✅ [tred split] ' + res.message);
  return 0;
}

async function cmdMap(flags: Record<string, string | true | string[]>): Promise<number> {
  const dir = typeof flags.dir === 'string' ? flags.dir : '.';
  const map = generateMap(dir);
  process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(map, null, 2) + '\n');
  return 0;
}

async function cmdPatch(flagsRaw: Record<string, string | true | string[]>, positionals: string[]): Promise<number> {
  const flags: Record<string, string | true> = {};
  for (const k of Object.keys(flagsRaw)) {
    const v = flagsRaw[k];
    flags[k] = Array.isArray(v) ? v[v.length - 1] : v;
  }
  const file = requireStr(flags, 'file');
  let patchText = '';
  if (positionals[1]) {
    patchText = readFileSync(positionals[1], 'utf-8');
  } else {
    patchText = readFileSync(0, 'utf-8');
  }

  const fuzzy = Boolean(flags.fuzzy);
  const fuzzyThreshold = typeof flags['fuzzy-threshold'] === 'string' ? parseFloat(flags['fuzzy-threshold']) : undefined;
  const minRareTokens = typeof flags['fuzzy-min-rare'] === 'string' ? parseInt(flags['fuzzy-min-rare'], 10) : undefined;
  const autoHeal = Boolean(flags['auto-heal']);
  const html = Boolean(flags.html);
  const res = applyPatch(file, patchText, { fuzzy, fuzzyThreshold, minRareTokens, autoHeal, html });

  if (!res.ok) {
    console.error('❌ [tred patch] ' + res.message);
    if (res.suggestion) {
      console.log('\n💡 [tred suggest] 发现第 ' + res.suggestion.line + ' 行有 ' + (res.suggestion.score * 100).toFixed(1) + '% 相似度的代码块，' + res.suggestion.reason);
      console.log('   -----------------------------');
      res.suggestion.context.forEach((line: string, i: number) => {
        const lineNum = res.suggestion!.line - 2 + i;
        console.log('   ' + String(lineNum).padStart(4) + ' | ' + line);
      });
      console.log('   -----------------------------');
      const nextThresh = Math.max(0, res.suggestion.score - 0.05);
      console.log('   如果你确认这是正确的目标，可以尝试降低阈值：');
      console.log('   tred patch --file ' + file + ' ... --fuzzy --fuzzy-threshold ' + nextThresh.toFixed(2));
    }
    return 1;
  }

  console.log('✓ Patch 应用成功: ' + file);
  return 0;
}

async function cmdGrep(flags: Record<string, string | true | string[]>, positionals: string[]): Promise<number> {
  const pattern = positionals[1];
  if (!pattern) die('用法: tred grep <pattern> [--dir <path>] [--ast] [--html]');
  const dir = typeof flags.dir === 'string' ? flags.dir : '.';
  const useAst = Boolean(flags.ast);
  const useHtml = Boolean(flags.html);
  grepDir(dir, pattern, useAst, useHtml);
  return 0;
}

async function cmdReplace(flagsRaw: Record<string, string | true | string[]>): Promise<number> {
  const flags: Record<string, string | true> = {};
  for (const k of Object.keys(flagsRaw)) {
    const v = flagsRaw[k];
    flags[k] = Array.isArray(v) ? v[v.length - 1] : v;
  }

  const file = requireStr(flags, 'file');
  const anchor = typeof flags.anchor === 'string' ? flags.anchor : undefined;
  const content = readContent(flags);

  let fingerprint = '';
  if (anchor) {
    fingerprint = resolveFingerprint(flags, anchor);
  }

  let astSelector: AstSelector | undefined;
  let genericSelector: any;
  if (typeof flags.selector === 'string') {
    try {
      genericSelector = JSON.parse(flags.selector);
      if (['class','method','function','arrow-function','statement','variable','property'].includes(genericSelector.kind)) {
        astSelector = genericSelector as AstSelector;
      }
    } catch {
      die('--selector 必须是合法 JSON');
    }
  }
  
  const toArr = (v: any) => (Array.isArray(v) ? v : v ? [v] : undefined);
  const fuzzThresh = typeof flags['fuzzy-threshold'] === 'string' ? parseFloat(flags['fuzzy-threshold']) : undefined;
  if (fuzzThresh !== undefined && astSelector) astSelector.fuzzyThreshold = fuzzThresh;
  const occ = typeof flags.occurrence === 'string' ? parseInt(flags.occurrence, 10) : undefined;
  if (astSelector && occ) astSelector.occurrence = occ;

  const snippetSelector: SnippetSelector = {};
  if (flags['snippet-start-line']) snippetSelector.startLine = parseInt(flags['snippet-start-line'] as string, 10);
  if (flags['snippet-end-line']) snippetSelector.endLine = parseInt(flags['snippet-end-line'] as string, 10);
  if (flags['snippet-start']) snippetSelector.startSnippet = flags['snippet-start'] as string;
  if (flags['snippet-end']) snippetSelector.endSnippet = flags['snippet-end'] as string;
  if (flags['cut-to-end']) snippetSelector.cutToEnd = true;
  if (occ) snippetSelector.occurrence = occ;

  const hasSnippet = Object.keys(snippetSelector).length > 0;

  if (!anchor && !astSelector && !genericSelector && !hasSnippet) {
    die('必须提供 --anchor 或 --selector 或 --snippet-* 之一');
  }

  if (flags['auto-inject'] && (!anchor || !astSelector)) {
    die('--auto-inject 需要同时提供 --anchor 和 --selector');
  }

  const opts: ReplaceOptions = {
    file,
    anchor: anchor || '',
    content,
    fingerprint,
    dryRun: Boolean(flags['dry-run']),
    autoInjectAnchor: Boolean(flags['auto-inject']),
    fuzzy: Boolean(flags.fuzzy),
    astSelector,
    selector: genericSelector,
    snippetSelector: hasSnippet ? snippetSelector : undefined,
    assertions: {
      include: toArr(flagsRaw['assert-include']),
      exclude: toArr(flagsRaw['assert-exclude']),
      regex: toArr(flagsRaw['assert-regex']),
      hasAst: toArr(flagsRaw['assert-has-ast']),
      noAst: toArr(flagsRaw['assert-no-ast']),
    },
    format: await buildFormatter(flags),
  };
  
  const r = await replace(opts);

  if (flags.json) {
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2) + '\n');
  } else {
    process.stdout.write('[tred] ' + r.status.toUpperCase() + ' ' + r.file + '\n');
    if (r.message) process.stdout.write('        ' + r.message + '\n');
    if (r.syntaxErrors && r.syntaxErrors.length) {
      process.stdout.write('        错误:\n');
      for (const e of r.syntaxErrors) process.stdout.write('          ' + e + '\n');
    }
    if (r.status === 'failed' && r.failureContext) {
      process.stdout.write('\n        ⚠️ 原代码 (将被替换):\n');
      process.stdout.write(r.failureContext.oldCode.split('\n').map((l: string) => '          ' + l).join('\n') + '\n');
      process.stdout.write('\n        ⚠️ 你的新代码 (导致失败):\n');
      process.stdout.write(r.failureContext.newCode.split('\n').map((l: string) => '          ' + l).join('\n') + '\n');
    } else if (r.diff) {
      process.stdout.write('\n' + r.diff);
    }
  }
  return r.status === 'failed' ? 1 : 0;
}

function renderTree(nodes: OutlineNode[], prefix: string): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < nodes.length; i++) {
    const node = nodes[i];
    const last = i === nodes.length - 1;
    const branch = last ? '└─ ' : '├─ ';
    let label = branch + node.kind + ' ' + node.name + ' @' + node.line + ':' + node.column;
    if (node.selector) label += '  selector=' + JSON.stringify(node.selector);
    out.push(prefix + label);
    if (node.children && node.children.length > 0) {
      const nextPrefix = prefix + (last ? '   ' : '│  ');
      const sub = renderTree(node.children, nextPrefix);
      for (const line of sub) out.push(line);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

async function cmdAst(flags: Record<string, string | true>): Promise<number> {
  const file = requireStr(flags, 'file');
  if (!existsSync(file)) die('文件不存在: ' + file);
  const source = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
  const outline = buildOutline(file, source);

  if (flags.json || flags['json-ast']) {
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(outline, null, 2) + '\n');
    return 0;
  }

  process.stdout.write(file + '\n');
  if (outline.length === 0) {
    process.stdout.write('  (无 class / function)\n');
    return 0;
  }
  const lines = renderTree(outline, '');
  for (const line of lines) process.stdout.write(line + '\n');
  process.stdout.write('\n提示: 把 selector={...} 的内容直接传给 --selector\n');
  return 0;
}

async function cmdReplaceMany(flags: Record<string, string | true>): Promise<number> {
  const opsFile = requireStr(flags, 'ops-file');
  const raw = readFileSync(opsFile, 'utf-8');
  let ops: ReplaceOptions[];
  try { ops = JSON.parse(raw) as ReplaceOptions[]; }
  catch { die('--ops-file 必须是合法 JSON'); }
  if (!Array.isArray(ops)) die('--ops-file 必须是 JSON 数组');

  if (flags['dry-run']) {
    ops = ops.map(function (op) {
      const copy: ReplaceOptions = Object.assign({}, op);
      copy.dryRun = true;
      return copy;
    });
  }

  const r = await replaceMany(ops, {
    rollbackLog: typeof flags['rollback-log'] === 'string' ? flags['rollback-log'] : undefined,
    continueOnError: Boolean(flags['continue-on-error']),
  });

  if (flags.json) {
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2) + '\n');
  } else {
    process.stdout.write('[tred] batch ' + r.status.toUpperCase() + '\n');
    for (let i = 0; i < r.results.length; i++) {
      const res = r.results[i];
      process.stdout.write('  #' + (i + 1) + ' ' + res.status + ' ' + res.anchor + ' @ ' + res.file + '\n');
      if (res.message) process.stdout.write('     ' + res.message + '\n');
    }
    if (r.committedFiles.length > 0) process.stdout.write('  写入: ' + r.committedFiles.join(', ') + '\n');
    if (r.error) process.stdout.write('  error: ' + r.error + '\n');
    if (r.status === 'rolled-back' && typeof flags['rollback-log'] === 'string') {
      process.stdout.write('  审计日志: ' + flags['rollback-log'] + '\n');
    }
  }
  return (r.status === 'rolled-back' || r.status === 'partial') ? 1 : 0;
}

function renderVerifyHuman(r: VerifyReport): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const absDir = r.dir || '.';
  out.push('[tred] verify ' + absDir);
  out.push('扫描 ' + r.scannedFiles + ' 个文件，' + r.files.length + ' 个含锚点');
  if (r.files.length === 0 && r.issues.length === 0) {
    out.push('✅ 无锚点，无问题');
    return out.join('\n') + '\n';
  }
  out.push('');

  for (const fr of r.files) {
    const hasOrphan = fr.orphans.length > 0;
    const icon = hasOrphan ? '❌' : '✅';
    out.push(icon + ' ' + fr.file);
    for (const a of fr.anchors) {
      const fp = a.fp ? '  fp=' + a.fp : '  (无指纹)';
      out.push('   @TRED  ' + a.name + '  L' + a.line + '-L' + a.endLine + fp);
    }
    for (const o of fr.orphans) {
      out.push('   ⚠ 孤儿 ' + o.kind.toUpperCase() + '  ' + o.name + '  L' + o.line);
    }
  }

  if (r.issues.length > 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('问题 (' + r.issues.length + '):');
    for (const i of r.issues) {
      const loc = i.file ? i.file + (i.line ? ':' + i.line : '') : '';
      out.push('  [' + i.level + '] ' + i.code + (loc ? ' ' + loc : '') + '  ' + i.message);
    }
  }

  if (r.duplicatesAcross.length > 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('跨文件同名锚点 (' + r.duplicatesAcross.length + '):');
    for (const d of r.duplicatesAcross) {
      const locs = d.entries.map(function (e) { return e.file + ':' + e.line; }).join(', ');
      out.push('  "' + d.name + '"  ' + locs);
    }
  }

  if (r.fingerprintMismatches.length > 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('跨文件指纹不一致 (' + r.fingerprintMismatches.length + '):');
    for (const m of r.fingerprintMismatches) {
      const locs = m.entries.map(function (e) { return e.file + ':' + e.line + (e.fp ? '=' + e.fp : '=?'); }).join(', ');
      out.push('  "' + m.name + '"  ' + locs);
    }
  }

  out.push('');
  out.push(r.ok ? '✅ ok' : '❌ 未通过');
  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}

function renderFixHuman(r: FixReport): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('[tred] verify --fix ' + (r.dryRun ? '（dry-run，用 --apply 实际写盘）' : '（--apply）'));
  out.push('扫描 ' + r.scannedFiles + ' 个文件');

  if (r.totalActions === 0 && r.remainingOrphanStarts.length === 0) {
    out.push('✅ 无需修复');
    return out.join('\n') + '\n';
  }

  if (r.files.length > 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push(r.dryRun ? '待修复 (' + r.totalActions + ' 处):' : '修复结果:');
    for (const f of r.files) {
      const status = f.error ? '✗' : (f.applied ? '✅' : '·');
      out.push('  ' + status + ' ' + f.file);
      for (const a of f.actions) {
        out.push('      L' + a.line + '  ' + a.description);
      }
      if (f.error) out.push('      error: ' + f.error);
    }
  }

  if (r.remainingOrphanStarts.length > 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('⚠ 需手动处理 (孤儿 START 无法自动修复):');
    for (const o of r.remainingOrphanStarts) {
      out.push('  ' + o.file + ':' + o.line + '  锚点 "' + o.anchorName + '" 有 START 无 END');
    }
    out.push('  提示：手动补 END，或用 replace --auto-inject 重写该锚点');
  }

  if (r.dryRun && r.files.length > 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('--- diff ---');
    for (const f of r.files) {
      out.push(f.diff);
    }
    out.push('提示: 加 --apply 实际写盘');
  }

  out.push('');
  out.push(r.ok ? (r.dryRun ? '✅ dry-run 完成' : '✅ 修复完成') : '❌ 有文件修复失败');
  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}

function renderDiffHuman(r: {
  ok: boolean;
  anchor: string;
  left: { file: string; found: boolean; startLine: number | null; endLine: number | null };
  right: { file: string; found: boolean; startLine: number | null; endLine: number | null };
  diff: string;
  message?: string;
}): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('[tred] diff ' + r.anchor);
  const leftLoc = r.left.found ? r.left.file + ' L' + r.left.startLine + '-L' + r.left.endLine : r.left.file + ' (未找到)';
  const rightLoc = r.right.found ? r.right.file + ' L' + r.right.startLine + '-L' + r.right.endLine : r.right.file + ' (未找到)';
  out.push('  left:  ' + leftLoc);
  out.push('  right: ' + rightLoc);
  if (!r.ok) {
    out.push('  ' + (r.message || '失败'));
    return out.join("\n") + "\n";
  }
  if (!r.diff) {
    out.push('  (无差异)');
    return out.join("\n") + "\n";
  }
  out.push('');
  out.push(r.diff.trimEnd());
  return out.join("\n") + "\n";
}

function renderDiffDirHuman(r: DiffDirResult): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('[tred] diff-dir ' + r.leftDir + ' ↔ ' + r.rightDir);
  out.push('  比对: ' + r.stats.compared + ' 个锚点');
  out.push('  未变: ' + r.stats.unchanged);
  out.push('  变化: ' + r.stats.changed);
  out.push('  缺失: ' + r.stats.missing);
  out.push('');
  for (const f of r.files) {
    if (f.anchors.length === 0) continue;
    const suffix = f.leftExists && f.rightExists ? '' : ' (文件单侧存在)';
    out.push(f.file + suffix);
    for (const a of f.anchors) {
      if (a.message) {
        out.push('  ⚠ ' + a.name + ': ' + a.message);
      } else if (!a.diff) {
        out.push('  ✓ ' + a.name + ' (无变化)');
      } else {
        out.push('  ≠ ' + a.name);
        const lines = a.diff.trimEnd().split('\n');
        for (const line of lines) out.push('    ' + line);
      }
    }
    out.push('');
  }
  out.push(r.ok ? '✅ 无差异' : '❌ 有差异');
  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}

function renderDoctorHuman(r: DoctorReport): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('[tred] doctor');
  out.push('  环境: ' + r.platform + ' / node ' + r.nodeVersion);
  out.push('  目录: ' + r.dir);
  out.push('  版本: tred=' + r.tredVersion + ', package=' + r.packageVersion);
  out.push('');
  out.push('  git:');
  out.push('    ' + (r.git.isRepo ? '✓ 仓库 (branch=' + r.git.branch + ', head=' + (r.git.head ? r.git.head.slice(0, 8) : 'n/a') + ')' : '× 非 git 仓库'));
  out.push('    ' + (r.git.dirty ? '⚠ 有 ' + r.git.changedFiles + ' 个改动文件' : '✓ 工作树干净'));
  out.push('');
  out.push('  缓存:');
  out.push('    ' + (r.cache.exists ? '✓ ' + r.cache.entries + ' 条 (' + r.cache.sizeBytes + ' 字节)' : '· 未启用'));
  out.push('');
  out.push('  锚点:');
  out.push('    ' + r.anchors.files + ' 文件, ' + r.anchors.total + ' 锚点, ' + r.anchors.errors + ' error / ' + r.anchors.warnings + ' warning');
  out.push('');
  out.push('  依赖:');
  out.push('    tsx=' + (r.deps.tsx ? '✓' : '×') + ', prettier=' + (r.deps.prettier ? '✓' : '×'));
  out.push('');
  out.push('  建议:');
  for (const s of r.suggestions) out.push('    · ' + s);
  out.push('');
  out.push(r.ok ? '✅ 环境健康' : '⚠ 有问题需要处理');
  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}

async function cmdDoctor(flags: Record<string, string | true>): Promise<number> {
  const dir = typeof flags.dir === 'string' ? flags.dir : '.';
  const r = doctor({ dir });
  if (flags.json) {
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2) + '\n');
  } else {
    process.stdout.write(renderDoctorHuman(r));
  }
  return r.ok ? 0 : 1;
}

async function cmdDiff(flags: Record<string, string | true>): Promise<number> {
  if (flags['against-dir'] !== undefined) {
    const leftDir = requireStr(flags, 'dir');
    const rightDir = requireStr(flags, 'against-dir');
    const anchorFilter = typeof flags.anchor === 'string' ? flags.anchor : undefined;
    let fileList: string[] | undefined;
    if (typeof flags['since-file'] === 'string') {
      try {
        fileList = readFileSync(flags['since-file'] as string, 'utf-8').split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
      } catch (e) {
        die('--since-file 读取失败: ' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
      }
    }
    const useGit = typeof flags.since === 'string' || fileList !== undefined;
    const r = useGit
      ? diffDirGit({ leftDir: leftDir, rightDir: rightDir, anchorFilter: anchorFilter, since: typeof flags.since === 'string' ? flags.since : 'HEAD', changedFiles: fileList } as any)
      : diffDir({ leftDir: leftDir, rightDir: rightDir, anchorFilter: anchorFilter });
    if (flags.json) {
      process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2) + '\n');
    } else if (flags.format === 'markdown') {
      process.stdout.write(renderDiffDirMarkdown(r as any));
    } else {
      process.stdout.write(renderDiffDirHuman(r));
    }
    // v0.17 修正：目录模式 ok=false 只代表有差异/缺失，不是错误；--exit-code 才断言
    if (flags['exit-code'] && (r.stats.changed > 0 || r.stats.missing > 0)) return 1;
    return 0;
  }

  const anchor = requireStr(flags, 'anchor');
  const left = requireStr(flags, 'file');
  const right = requireStr(flags, 'against');
  const r = diffAnchor({ anchor: anchor, left: left, right: right });
  if (flags.json) {
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2) + '\n');
  } else {
    process.stdout.write(renderDiffHuman(r));
  }
  if (!r.ok) return 1;
  if (flags['exit-code'] && r.diff !== '') return 1;
  return 0;
}



function renderAnnotateHuman(r: AnnotateResult): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('[tred] annotate ' + r.file + (r.applied ? ' (已写盘)' : ' (dry-run)'));
  if (!r.ok) {
    out.push('  ❌ 失败');
    if (r.syntaxErrors) for (const e of r.syntaxErrors) out.push('    ' + e);
    return out.join('\n') + '\n';
  }
  if (r.actions.length === 0) {
    out.push('  无新锚点（全部已存在或无可匹配节点）');
    return out.join('\n') + '\n';
  }
  out.push('  新增 ' + r.actions.length + ' 条锚点注释');
  for (const a of r.actions) {
    out.push('    L' + a.line + '  ' + a.kind.toUpperCase() + '  ' + a.name + '  (' + a.nodeKind + ')');
  }
  if (r.diff) {
    out.push('');
    out.push(r.diff.trimEnd());
  }
  if (!r.applied) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('提示: 加 --apply 写盘');
  }
  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}

function renderAnnotateDirHuman(r: AnnotateDirResult): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('[tred] annotate --dir ' + r.dir + (r.apply ? ' (已写盘)' : ' (dry-run)'));
  out.push('  涉及文件: ' + r.annotatedFiles + ' 个');
  out.push('  新增锚点: ' + r.totalActions + ' 条');
  if (r.failedFiles > 0) out.push('  失败文件: ' + r.failedFiles + ' 个');
  if (r.files.length === 0) {
    out.push('  (无可标注节点)');
    return out.join('\n') + '\n';
  }
  out.push('');
  for (const f of r.files) {
    const icon = f.ok ? (f.applied ? '✅' : '·') : '❌';
    out.push(icon + ' ' + f.file + '  (' + f.actions.length + ' 条' + (f.ok ? '' : '，失败') + ')');
    if (!f.ok && f.syntaxErrors) {
      for (const e of f.syntaxErrors) out.push('    ' + e);
    }
  }
  if (!r.apply) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('提示: 加 --apply 写盘');
  }
  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}

async function cmdAnnotate(flags: Record<string, string | true>): Promise<number> {
  const all = Boolean(flags.all);
  const classes = all || Boolean(flags.classes);
  const methods = all || Boolean(flags.methods);
  const functions = all || Boolean(flags.functions);
  if (!classes && !methods && !functions) {
    die('至少指定 --classes / --methods / --functions 之一，或用 --all');
  }

  const apply = Boolean(flags.apply);
  const useDir = typeof flags.dir === 'string';
  const useFile = typeof flags.file === 'string';
  if (useDir && useFile) die('--file 和 --dir 不能同时使用');
  if (!useDir && !useFile) die('必须指定 --file 或 --dir');

  if (useDir) {
    const r = annotateDir({
      dir: flags.dir as string,
      classes: classes,
      methods: methods,
      functions: functions,
      apply: apply,
      order: (typeof flags.order === 'string' ? flags.order : 'source') as any,
      since: typeof flags.since === 'string' ? flags.since : undefined,
    sinceFile: typeof flags['since-file'] === 'string' ? flags['since-file'] : undefined,
      exclude: typeof flags.exclude === 'string' ? [flags.exclude as string] : undefined,
    } as any);
    if (flags.json) {
      process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2) + '\n');
    } else if (flags.format === 'markdown') {
      process.stdout.write(renderAnnotateDirMarkdown(r as any));
    } else {
      process.stdout.write(renderAnnotateDirHuman(r));
    }
    return r.ok ? 0 : 1;
  }

  const r = annotateFile({
    file: flags.file as string,
    classes: classes,
    methods: methods,
    functions: functions,
    apply: apply,
  });
  if (flags.json) {
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2) + '\n');
  } else {
    process.stdout.write(renderAnnotateHuman(r));
  }
  return r.ok ? 0 : 1;
}


async function cmdCheck(flags: Record<string, string | true>): Promise<number> {
  const dir = typeof flags.dir === 'string' ? flags.dir : '.';
  const preCommit = Boolean(flags['pre-commit']);
  const strict = Boolean(flags.strict) || !preCommit;

  let baselineFile: string | undefined;
  if (preCommit && typeof flags.baseline === 'string') {
    baselineFile = flags.baseline;
  } else if (preCommit) {
    const auto = join(process.cwd(), '.tred-baseline.json');
    if (existsSync(auto)) baselineFile = auto;
  }

  let report = verifyDir({ dir: dir, strict: strict });
  if (baselineFile) {
    const bl = loadBaseline(baselineFile);
    if (bl) {
      report = filterIssuesByBaseline(report, bl, strict);
    } else {
      console.error('[tred] baseline 文件无效: ' + baselineFile);
    }
  }

  if (flags.json) {
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n');
  } else if (flags.format === 'markdown') {
    process.stdout.write(renderVerifyMarkdown(report));
  } else {
    process.stdout.write(renderVerifyHuman(report));
  }
  return report.ok ? 0 : 1;
}

async function cmdInit(flags: Record<string, string | true>): Promise<number> {
  const dir = typeof flags.dir === 'string' ? flags.dir : '.';
  const withHook = Boolean(flags['with-hook']);
  const r = init({ dir: dir, withHook: withHook });
  if (flags.json) {
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(r, null, 2) + '\n');
  } else {
    process.stdout.write('[tred] init ' + dir + '\n');
    for (const f of r.created) process.stdout.write('  ✓ 创建: ' + f + '\n');
    for (const f of r.skipped) process.stdout.write('  · 已存在: ' + f + '\n');
    for (const f of r.errors) process.stdout.write('  ❌ 失败: ' + f + '\n');
    if (r.created.length === 0 && r.skipped.length === 0) {
      process.stdout.write('  (无文件变化)\n');
    }
    process.stdout.write('\n下一步建议:\n');
    process.stdout.write('  1. 编辑 .tred/config.json 调整 dir / exclude\n');
    process.stdout.write('  2. tred verify --dir src --save-baseline .tred-baseline.json  （冻结现存问题）\n');
    process.stdout.write('  3. tred check --pre-commit  （只报新增）\n');
  }
  return r.ok ? 0 : 1;
}

async function cmdVerify(flags: Record<string, string | true>): Promise<number> {
  const dir = typeof flags.dir === 'string' ? flags.dir : '.';
  if (!existsSync(dir)) die('目录不存在: ' + dir);

  const format = typeof flags.format === 'string' ? flags.format : 'text';
  const VALID = ['text', 'markdown', 'github-annotations', 'sarif', 'html'];
  if (VALID.indexOf(format) < 0) {
    die('--format 只支持 ' + VALID.join(' | ') + '（json 请用 --json）');
  }
  const useMarkdown = format === 'markdown';
  const useGithub = format === 'github-annotations';
  const useSarif = format === 'sarif';
  const useHtml = format === 'html';

  if (flags.fix) {
    if (useSarif || useHtml) {
      die('--fix 不支持 --format ' + format + '（仅支持 text | markdown | github-annotations）');
    }
    const fixReport = fixDir({
      dir: dir,
      apply: Boolean(flags.apply),
      fixOrphanStart: Boolean(flags['fix-orphan-start']),
      cache: Boolean(flags.cache),
      cacheDir: typeof flags['cache-dir'] === 'string' ? flags['cache-dir'] : undefined,
    });
    if (flags.json) {
      process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(fixReport, null, 2) + '\n');
    } else if (useMarkdown) {
      process.stdout.write(renderFixMarkdown(fixReport));
    } else if (useGithub) {
      process.stdout.write(renderFixGithubAnnotations(fixReport));
    } else {
      process.stdout.write(renderFixHuman(fixReport));
    }
    return fixReport.ok ? 0 : 1;
  }

  let verifyReport = verifyDir({
    dir: dir,
    strict: Boolean(flags.strict),
    cache: Boolean(flags.cache),
    cacheDir: typeof flags['cache-dir'] === 'string' ? flags['cache-dir'] : undefined,
    since: typeof flags.since === 'string' ? flags.since : undefined,
    sinceFile: typeof flags['since-file'] === 'string' ? flags['since-file'] : undefined,
    exclude: Array.isArray(flags.exclude) ? (flags.exclude as any) : (typeof flags.exclude === 'string' ? [flags.exclude as string] : undefined),
  } as any);

  const saveBaselineFile = typeof flags['save-baseline'] === 'string' ? flags['save-baseline'] : undefined;
  const baselineFile = typeof flags.baseline === 'string' ? flags.baseline : undefined;

  if (saveBaselineFile) {
    const bl = buildBaseline(verifyReport);
    try {
      saveBaseline(saveBaselineFile, bl);
      process.stdout.write('[tred] 基线已保存: ' + saveBaselineFile + ' (' + bl.issues.length + ' 个 issues)\n');
    } catch (e) {
      die('保存基线失败: ' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
    }
  }

  let finalReport = verifyReport;
  if (baselineFile) {
    const bl = loadBaseline(baselineFile);
    if (!bl) {
      die('基线文件不存在或损坏: ' + baselineFile);
    }
    finalReport = filterIssuesByBaseline(verifyReport, bl, Boolean(flags.strict));
  }

  if (typeof flags['diff-from'] === 'string') {
    const diffMap = getDiffLines(flags['diff-from'] as string);
    if (diffMap.size === 0) {
      process.stdout.write('[tred] --diff-from ' + flags['diff-from'] + ': 无改动行\n');
    } else {
      const dirPrefix = (function () {
        if (dir === '.' || dir === './') return '';
        let d = dir.replace(/\/+$/, '');
        if (d.indexOf('./') === 0) d = d.slice(2);
        return d;
      })();
      const kept = finalReport.issues.filter((issue) => {
        if (!issue.file || !issue.line) return false;
        const key = dirPrefix ? dirPrefix + '/' + issue.file : issue.file;
        const lines = diffMap.get(key);
        if (!lines) return false;
        return lines.has(issue.line);
      });
      const hasError = kept.some((i) => i.level === 'error');
      const hasWarning = kept.some((i) => i.level === 'warning');
      const strict = Boolean(flags.strict);
      const ok = strict ? (!hasError && !hasWarning) : !hasError;
      finalReport = Object.assign({}, finalReport, { issues: kept, ok: ok });
    }
  }

  const pkgVersion = readPkgVersion();
  const informationUri = readPkgField('homepage') || 'https://github.com/chinartcn/tred';

  if (flags.json) {
    process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(finalReport, null, 2) + '\n');
  } else if (useMarkdown) {
    process.stdout.write(renderVerifyMarkdown(finalReport));
  } else if (useGithub) {
    process.stdout.write(renderGithubAnnotations(finalReport));
  } else if (useSarif) {
    process.stdout.write(renderSarif(finalReport, { version: pkgVersion, informationUri: informationUri }));
  } else if (useHtml) {
    process.stdout.write(renderHtml(finalReport, { version: pkgVersion }));
  } else {
    process.stdout.write(renderVerifyHuman(finalReport));
  }
  return finalReport.ok ? 0 : 1;
}

function readPkgVersion(): string {
  try {
    const raw = readFileSync(join(process.cwd(), 'package.json'), 'utf-8');
    const pkg = JSON.parse(raw) as { version?: string };
    return pkg.version || '0.0.0';
  } catch {
    try {
      const here = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
      const pkgPath = join(_dirname(here), '..', 'package.json');
      const raw = readFileSync(pkgPath, 'utf-8');
      const pkg = JSON.parse(raw) as { version?: string };
      return pkg.version || '0.0.0';
    } catch {
      return '0.0.0';
    }
  }
}

function readPkgField(field: string): string | null {
  try {
    const here = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
    const pkgPath = join(_dirname(here), '..', 'package.json');
    const raw = readFileSync(pkgPath, 'utf-8');
    const pkg = JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string, unknown>;
    const v = pkg[field];
    return typeof v === 'string' ? v : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function cmdWatch(flags: Record<string, string | true>): Promise<number> {
  const dir = typeof flags.dir === 'string' ? flags.dir : '.';
  if (!existsSync(dir)) die('目录不存在: ' + dir);

  const strict = Boolean(flags.strict);
  const format = typeof flags.format === 'string' ? flags.format : 'text';
  if (format !== 'text' && format !== 'markdown' && format !== 'github-annotations') {
    die('--format 只支持 text | markdown | github-annotations');
  }

  function runOnce(): void {
    let report;
    try {
      report = verifyDir({ dir: dir, strict: strict });
    } catch (e) {
      process.stdout.write('[tred] verify 失败: ' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)) + '\n');
      return;
    }
    const ts = new Date().toISOString().slice(11, 19);
    process.stdout.write('\n[' + ts + '] verify ' + dir + '\n');
    if (format === 'markdown') {
      process.stdout.write(renderVerifyMarkdown(report));
    } else if (format === 'github-annotations') {
      process.stdout.write(renderGithubAnnotations(report));
    } else {
      process.stdout.write(renderVerifyHuman(report));
    }
  }

  runOnce();
  process.stdout.write('\n[tred] watch 已启动（Ctrl+C 退出）\n');

  const handle = watchDir(dir, runOnce);

  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
    const onSigint = (): void => {
      process.stdout.write('\n[tred] watch 已停止\n');
      handle.close();
      process.removeListener('SIGINT', onSigint);
      resolve();
    };
    process.on('SIGINT', onSigint);
  });
  return 0;
}

async function cmdServe(flags: Record<string, string | true>): Promise<number> {
  const dir = typeof flags.dir === 'string' ? flags.dir : '.';
  if (!existsSync(dir)) die('目录不存在: ' + dir);

  const portRaw = typeof flags.port === 'string' ? flags.port : '8080';
  const port = parseInt(portRaw, 10);
  if (isNaN(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) die('非法 --port: ' + portRaw);

  const shouldOpen = !Boolean(flags['no-open']);
  const watch = Boolean(flags.watch);
  const strict = Boolean(flags.strict);

  const pkgVersion = readPkgVersion();

  function regenerate(): string {
    return renderHtml(verifyDir({ dir: dir, strict: strict }), { version: pkgVersion });
  }

  let cached = '';
  try {
    cached = regenerate();
  } catch (e) {
    die('首次生成报告失败: ' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
  }

  let dirty = false;
  function getHtml(): string {
    if (dirty) {
      try { cached = regenerate(); }
      catch { /* keep old */ }
      dirty = false;
    }
    return cached;
  }

  let handle: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof serveHtml>>;
  try {
    handle = await serveHtml(getHtml, { port: port });
  } catch (e) {
    const code = (e as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code;
    if (code === 'EADDRINUSE') {
      die('端口 ' + port + ' 已被占用，请用 --port <other> 换一个');
    }
    die('启动 HTTP 服务失败: ' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
  }

  process.stdout.write('[tred] serve ' + handle.url + ' (dir: ' + dir + ')' + String.fromCharCode(10));
  process.stdout.write('[tred] Ctrl+C 停止' + String.fromCharCode(10));

  if (shouldOpen) {
    const ok = tryOpenBrowser(handle.url);
    if (ok) process.stdout.write('[tred] 已尝试打开浏览器' + String.fromCharCode(10));
    else process.stdout.write('[tred] 请手动打开上面的 URL' + String.fromCharCode(10));
  }

  let watchHandle: ReturnType<typeof watchDir> | null = null;
  if (watch) {
    watchHandle = watchDir(dir, () => {
      dirty = true;
      process.stdout.write('[tred] 文件变化，刷新浏览器查看最新' + String.fromCharCode(10));
    });
    process.stdout.write('[tred] watch 已启用' + String.fromCharCode(10));
  }

  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
    const onSig = (): void => {
      process.stdout.write(String.fromCharCode(10) + '[tred] 已停止' + String.fromCharCode(10));
      if (watchHandle) watchHandle.close();
      handle.close();
      process.removeListener('SIGINT', onSig);
      resolve();
    };
    process.on('SIGINT', onSig);
  });

  return 0;
}


export async function cliMain(argv: string[]): Promise<void> {
  const parsed = parseArgs(argv);
  const flags = parsed.flags;
  const positionals = parsed.positionals;

  if (flags.help || positionals[0] === 'help') { process.stdout.write(HELP); return; }
  if (flags.version || positionals[0] === 'version') { process.stdout.write('tred v' + VERSION + '\n'); return; }

  const cmd = positionals[0];
  if (cmd === 'grep')         { process.exit(await cmdGrep(flags, positionals)); }
  if (cmd === 'map')          { process.exit(await cmdMap(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'impact')       { process.exit(await cmdImpact(flags, positionals)); }
  if (cmd === 'split')        { process.exit(await cmdSplit(flags, positionals)); }
  if (cmd === 'patch')        { process.exit(await cmdPatch(flags, positionals)); }
  if (cmd === 'replace')      { process.exit(await cmdReplace(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'replace-many') { process.exit(await cmdReplaceMany(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'ast')          { process.exit(await cmdAst(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'verify')       { process.exit(await cmdVerify(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'diff')         { process.exit(await cmdDiff(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'doctor')       { process.exit(await cmdDoctor(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'check')        { process.exit(await cmdCheck(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'init')         { process.exit(await cmdInit(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'watch')        { process.exit(await cmdWatch(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'serve')        { process.exit(await cmdServe(flags)); }
  
  // v1.3.0: ops 声明式执行
  if (cmd === 'commit')       { process.exit(await cmdCommit(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'strip')        { process.exit(await cmdStrip(flags)); }
  if (cmd === 'ops' || (cmd && cmd.endsWith('.json'))) {
    const opsFile = cmd.endsWith('.json') ? cmd : positionals[1];
    if (!opsFile) die('用法: tred ops <ops.json>');
    process.exit(await runOps(opsFile, flags));
  }
  
  if (!cmd) { process.stderr.write(HELP); process.exit(1); }
  die('未知命令: ' + cmd);
}

// 只有作为 CLI 入口（cli.ts / cli.js）时才自动执行；被 import 时静默
const __argv1 = process.argv[1] ?? '';
const __isCliEntry = __argv1.endsWith('cli.ts') || __argv1.endsWith('cli.js');
if (__isCliEntry) {
  cliMain(process.argv.slice(2)).catch(function (e: unknown) {
    process.stderr.write('tred: ' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)) + '\n');
    process.exit(1);
  });
}


async function cmdCommit(flags: Record<string, string | true | string[]>): Promise<number> {
  const msg = typeof flags.message === 'string' ? flags.message : 'tred: auto commit';
  console.log('[tred commit] 执行前置验证...');
  
  // 1. 跑 verify
  const v = await cmdVerify({ ...flags, strict: true });
  if (v !== 0) {
    console.error('[tred commit] ❌ 验证失败，已中止提交');
    return 1;
  }

  // 2. 跑 git add
  console.log('[tred commit] git add -A');
  const add = spawnSync('git', ['add', '-A'], { stdio: 'inherit' });
  if (add.status !== 0) return 1;

  // 3. 跑 git commit
  console.log('[tred commit] git commit -m "' + msg + '"');
  const commit = spawnSync('git', ['commit', '-m', msg], { stdio: 'inherit' });
  if (commit.status !== 0) {
    // 如果没有文件变更，git 会返回非 0
    return 1;
  }

  console.log('[tred commit] ✅ 提交成功！');
  return 0;
}

async function cmdStrip(flags: Record<string, string | true | string[]>): Promise<number> {
  const dir = typeof flags.dir === 'string' ? flags.dir : '.';
  const apply = Boolean(flags.apply);
  
  console.log('[tred strip] 扫描目录: ' + dir + (apply ? ' (写入模式)' : ' (dry-run)'));
  const count = stripDir(dir, apply);
  
  if (count === 0) {
    console.log('  ✓ 没有需要清理的锚点。');
  } else {
    if (apply) {
      console.log('  ✓ 已从 ' + count + ' 个文件中剥离了内部锚点。');
    } else {
      console.log('  ℹ 发现 ' + count + ' 个文件包含内部锚点。加 --apply 执行清理。');
    }
  }
  return 0;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/diff-anchor.ts
================================================================================
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { findAnchor } from './anchor.js';
import { unifiedDiff } from './diff.js';
import type {
  DiffAnchorOptions,
  DiffAnchorResult,
  DiffAnchorSide,
} from './types.js';

function extractAnchorSide(file: string, anchor: string): DiffAnchorSide {
  if (!existsSync(file)) {
    return { file: file, found: false, content: null, startLine: null, endLine: null };
  }
  const source = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
  const block = findAnchor(source, anchor);
  if (!block) {
    return { file: file, found: false, content: null, startLine: null, endLine: null };
  }
  const lines = source.split('\n');
  // 提取 START 行之后到 END 行之前的内容（不含两条注释）
  const inner = lines.slice(block.startLine + 1, block.endLine);
  return {
    file: file,
    found: true,
    content: inner.join('\n'),
    startLine: block.startLine + 2,
    endLine: block.endLine,
  };
}

export function diffAnchor(opts: DiffAnchorOptions): DiffAnchorResult {
  const left = extractAnchorSide(opts.left, opts.anchor);
  const right = extractAnchorSide(opts.right, opts.anchor);

  if (!left.found && !right.found) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      anchor: opts.anchor,
      left: left,
      right: right,
      diff: '',
      message: '两侧均未找到锚点 "' + opts.anchor + '"',
    };
  }
  if (!left.found) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      anchor: opts.anchor,
      left: left,
      right: right,
      diff: '',
      message: '左侧文件未找到锚点: ' + opts.left,
    };
  }
  if (!right.found) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      anchor: opts.anchor,
      left: left,
      right: right,
      diff: '',
      message: '右侧文件未找到锚点: ' + opts.right,
    };
  }

  const a = left.content ?? '';
  const b = right.content ?? '';
  if (a === b) {
    return { ok: true, anchor: opts.anchor, left: left, right: right, diff: '' };
  }

  const diff = unifiedDiff(a, b, 'a/' + opts.left, 'b/' + opts.right);
  return { ok: true, anchor: opts.anchor, left: left, right: right, diff: diff };
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/diff-dir.ts
================================================================================
import { copyFileSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname } from 'node:path';
import os from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { verifyDir } from './verify.js';
import { getChangedFiles, isGitRepo } from './git.js';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { diffAnchor } from './diff-anchor.js';
import type {
  DiffDirAnchorResult,
  DiffDirFileResult,
  DiffDirOptions,
  DiffDirResult,
  VerifyFileReport,
} from './types.js';

export function diffDir(opts: DiffDirOptions): DiffDirResult {
  const leftReport = verifyDir({ dir: opts.leftDir, extensions: opts.extensions, ignore: opts.ignore });
  const rightReport = verifyDir({ dir: opts.rightDir, extensions: opts.extensions, ignore: opts.ignore });

  const leftMap = new Map<string, VerifyFileReport>();
  for (const f of leftReport.files) leftMap.set(f.file, f);
  const rightMap = new Map<string, VerifyFileReport>();
  for (const f of rightReport.files) rightMap.set(f.file, f);

  const allFiles = new Set<string>();
  for (const f of leftMap.keys()) allFiles.add(f);
  for (const f of rightMap.keys()) allFiles.add(f);

  const files: DiffDirFileResult[] = [];
  let compared = 0;
  let unchanged = 0;
  let changed = 0;
  let missing = 0;

  const sortedFiles = Array.from(allFiles).sort();
  for (const rel of sortedFiles) {
    const lf = leftMap.get(rel);
    const rf = rightMap.get(rel);
    const leftPath = join(opts.leftDir, rel);
    const rightPath = join(opts.rightDir, rel);
    const leftExists = existsSync(leftPath);
    const rightExists = existsSync(rightPath);

    if (!leftExists || !rightExists) {
      const side = lf || rf;
      const anchors: DiffDirAnchorResult[] = [];
      if (side) {
        for (const a of side.anchors) {
          if (opts.anchorFilter && a.name !== opts.anchorFilter) continue;
          anchors.push({
            name: a.name,
            leftFound: !!lf && lf.anchors.some((x) => x.name === a.name),
            rightFound: !!rf && rf.anchors.some((x) => x.name === a.name),
            diff: '',
            message: leftExists ? '右侧文件缺失' : '左侧文件缺失',
          });
          missing++;
        }
      }
      if (anchors.length > 0) {
        files.push({ file: rel, leftExists: leftExists, rightExists: rightExists, anchors: anchors });
      }
      continue;
    }

    const names = new Set<string>();
    if (lf) for (const a of lf.anchors) names.add(a.name);
    if (rf) for (const a of rf.anchors) names.add(a.name);

    const anchorResults: DiffDirAnchorResult[] = [];
    const sortedNames = Array.from(names).sort();
    for (const name of sortedNames) {
      if (opts.anchorFilter && name !== opts.anchorFilter) continue;
      compared++;
      const dr = diffAnchor({ anchor: name, left: leftPath, right: rightPath });
      if (!dr.ok) {
        missing++;
        anchorResults.push({
          name: name,
          leftFound: dr.left.found,
          rightFound: dr.right.found,
          diff: '',
          message: dr.message,
        });
      } else if (dr.diff === '') {
        unchanged++;
        anchorResults.push({ name: name, leftFound: true, rightFound: true, diff: '' });
      } else {
        changed++;
        anchorResults.push({ name: name, leftFound: true, rightFound: true, diff: dr.diff });
      }
    }

    if (anchorResults.length > 0) {
      files.push({ file: rel, leftExists: leftExists, rightExists: rightExists, anchors: anchorResults });
    }
  }

  return {
    ok: changed === 0 && missing === 0,
    leftDir: opts.leftDir,
    rightDir: opts.rightDir,
    files: files,
    stats: { compared: compared, unchanged: unchanged, changed: changed, missing: missing },
  };
}

/**
 * diffDirGit：从 git ref 提取历史内容，与当前目录对比。
 * 只处理 git 改动过的文件（增量验证）。
 */
export function diffDirGit(opts: DiffDirOptions & { since?: string; changedFiles?: string[] }): DiffDirResult {
  const since = opts.since || (opts.changedFiles ? 'HEAD' : undefined);
  if (!since) {
    return diffDir(opts as DiffDirOptions);
  }
  if (!isGitRepo()) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      leftDir: opts.leftDir,
      rightDir: opts.rightDir,
      files: [],
      stats: { compared: 0, unchanged: 0, changed: 0, missing: 0 },
    };
  }

  const tmp = mkdtempSync(join(os.tmpdir(), 'tred-git-'));
  const leftDir = join(tmp, 'ref');
  const rightDir = join(tmp, 'current');
  mkdirSync(leftDir, { recursive: true });
  mkdirSync(rightDir, { recursive: true });

  try {
    const changed = opts.changedFiles ?? getChangedFiles(since);
    const cwd = process.cwd();
    const dirPrefix = opts.leftDir === '.' ? '' : opts.leftDir.replace(/^\.\//, '') + '/';

    for (const rel of changed) {
      if (dirPrefix && !rel.startsWith(dirPrefix)) continue;
      const relUnderDir = dirPrefix ? rel.slice(dirPrefix.length) : rel;
      const extMatch = (opts.extensions ?? ['.ts', '.tsx', '.js', '.jsx', '.mjs', '.cjs'])
        .some((e) => relUnderDir.endsWith(e));
      if (!extMatch) continue;

      const srcPath = join(cwd, rel);
      if (!existsSync(srcPath)) continue;

      const leftOut = join(leftDir, relUnderDir);
      mkdirSync(dirname(leftOut), { recursive: true });
      const r = spawnSync('git', ['show', since + ':' + rel], { cwd, encoding: 'utf-8' });
      if (r.status === 0) {
        writeFileSync(leftOut, r.stdout);
      }

      const rightOut = join(rightDir, relUnderDir);
      mkdirSync(dirname(rightOut), { recursive: true });
      copyFileSync(srcPath, rightOut);
    }

    return diffDir({
      leftDir,
      rightDir,
      extensions: opts.extensions,
      ignore: opts.ignore,
      anchorFilter: opts.anchorFilter,
    });
  } finally {
    rmSync(tmp, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/diff-lines.ts
================================================================================
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

/**
 * 解析 git diff --unified=0 <ref> HEAD，得到每个文件"新增/修改"的行号集合。
 * 返回 Map<相对仓库根的路径, Set<行号>>。
 * 只删除不添加的 hunk 不产生行号。
 */
export function getDiffLines(ref: string, cwd?: string): Map<string, Set<number>> {
  const r = spawnSync('git', ['diff', '--unified=0', ref, 'HEAD'], {
    cwd: cwd ?? process.cwd(),
    encoding: 'utf-8',
  });
  const map = new Map<string, Set<number>>();
  if (r.status !== 0) return map;

  let currentFile: string | null = null;
  for (const line of (r.stdout || '').split('\n')) {
    if (line.indexOf('+++ b/') === 0) {
      currentFile = line.slice(6);
      if (!map.has(currentFile)) map.set(currentFile, new Set());
      continue;
    }
    if (line.indexOf('+++ ') === 0) {
      currentFile = null;
      continue;
    }
    if (currentFile && line.indexOf('@@') === 0) {
      const m = line.match(/^@@ -\d+(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)(?:,(\d+))? @@/);
      if (m) {
        const start = parseInt(m[1], 10);
        const count = m[2] ? parseInt(m[2], 10) : 1;
        const set = map.get(currentFile);
        if (set) {
          for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
            set.add(start + i);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return map;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/diff.ts
================================================================================
interface Op { type: ' ' | '-' | '+'; line: string; }

const LCS_CELL_LIMIT = 4_000_000;

function lcsDiff(a: string[], b: string[]): Op[] {
  const m = a.length;
  const n = b.length;

  if (m * n > LCS_CELL_LIMIT) {
    const ops: Op[] = [];
    for (const line of a) ops.push({ type: '-', line });
    for (const line of b) ops.push({ type: '+', line });
    return ops;
  }

  const dp: number[][] = Array.from({ length: m + 1 }, () => new Array<number>(n + 1).fill(0));
  for (let i = 1; i <= m; i++) {
    for (let j = 1; j <= n; j++) {
      if (a[i - 1] === b[j - 1]) dp[i][j] = dp[i - 1][j - 1] + 1;
      else dp[i][j] = Math.max(dp[i - 1][j], dp[i][j - 1]);
    }
  }

  const ops: Op[] = [];
  let i = m;
  let j = n;
  while (i > 0 && j > 0) {
    if (a[i - 1] === b[j - 1]) {
      ops.push({ type: ' ', line: a[i - 1] });
      i--; j--;
    } else if (dp[i - 1][j] >= dp[i][j - 1]) {
      ops.push({ type: '-', line: a[i - 1] });
      i--;
    } else {
      ops.push({ type: '+', line: b[j - 1] });
      j--;
    }
  }
  while (i > 0) { ops.push({ type: '-', line: a[i - 1] }); i--; }
  while (j > 0) { ops.push({ type: '+', line: b[j - 1] }); j--; }
  ops.reverse();
  return ops;
}

export function unifiedDiff(
  oldText: string,
  newText: string,
  oldName: string,
  newName: string,
  contextLines: number = 3,
): string {
  if (oldText === newText) {
    return '--- ' + oldName + '\n+++ ' + newName + '\n';
  }

  const oldLines = oldText.split('\n');
  const newLines = newText.split('\n');
  const minLen = Math.min(oldLines.length, newLines.length);

  let prefix = 0;
  while (prefix < minLen && oldLines[prefix] === newLines[prefix]) prefix++;

  let suffix = 0;
  while (
    suffix < minLen - prefix &&
    oldLines[oldLines.length - 1 - suffix] === newLines[newLines.length - 1 - suffix]
  ) suffix++;

  const oldMid = oldLines.slice(prefix, oldLines.length - suffix);
  const newMid = newLines.slice(prefix, newLines.length - suffix);
  const midOps = lcsDiff(oldMid, newMid);

  const allOps: Op[] = [];
  for (const line of oldLines.slice(0, prefix)) allOps.push({ type: ' ', line });
  for (const op of midOps) allOps.push(op);
  for (const line of oldLines.slice(oldLines.length - suffix)) allOps.push({ type: ' ', line });

  // 截取局部上下文
  let startIdx = 0;
  let endIdx = allOps.length - 1;
  for (let k = 0; k < allOps.length; k++) {
    if (allOps[k].type !== ' ') { startIdx = Math.max(0, k - contextLines); break; }
  }
  for (let k = allOps.length - 1; k >= 0; k--) {
    if (allOps[k].type !== ' ') { endIdx = Math.min(allOps.length - 1, k + contextLines); break; }
  }
  
  const slicedOps = allOps.slice(startIdx, endIdx + 1);

  const header = '--- ' + oldName + '\n+++ ' + newName + '\n';
  const body = slicedOps.map((o) => o.type + o.line).join('\n');
  return body ? header + body + '\n' : header;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/doctor.ts
================================================================================
import { existsSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { getCachePath, loadCache } from './cache.js';
import { getChangedFiles, getCurrentBranch, getHeadSha, isDirty, isGitRepo } from './git.js';
import { verifyDir } from './verify.js';
import type { DoctorOptions, DoctorReport } from './types.js';

function fileSize(p: string): number {
  try { return statSync(p).size; } catch { return 0; }
}

function hasModule(name: string, rootDir: string): boolean {
  return existsSync(join(rootDir, 'node_modules', name));
}

function readVersionFile(p: string): string {
  try { return readFileSync(p, 'utf-8').trim(); } catch { return 'unknown'; }
}

export function doctor(opts?: DoctorOptions): DoctorReport {
  const o = opts || {};
  const dir = o.dir || '.';
  const absDir = resolve(dir);

  const packageVersion = (function () {
    try {
      const raw = readFileSync(join(absDir, 'package.json'), 'utf-8');
      const pkg = JSON.parse(raw) as { version?: string };
      return pkg.version || 'unknown';
    } catch { return 'unknown'; }
  })();

  const tredVersion = readVersionFile(join(absDir, '.tred-version'));

  const gitRepo = isGitRepo(absDir);
  const changedList = gitRepo ? getChangedFiles('HEAD', absDir) : [];
  const git = {
    available: gitRepo,
    isRepo: gitRepo,
    branch: gitRepo ? getCurrentBranch(absDir) : null,
    head: gitRepo ? getHeadSha(absDir) : null,
    dirty: gitRepo ? isDirty(absDir) : false,
    changedFiles: changedList.length,
  };

  const cacheDir = join(absDir, '.tred-cache');
  const cachePath = getCachePath(cacheDir);
  const cacheExists = existsSync(cachePath);
  const loadedCache = loadCache(cacheDir);
  const cache = {
    exists: cacheExists,
    entries: loadedCache ? Object.keys(loadedCache.entries).length : 0,
    sizeBytes: fileSize(cachePath),
    path: cachePath,
  };

  const verifyReport = verifyDir({ dir: absDir });
  const errors = verifyReport.issues.filter((i) => i.level === 'error').length;
  const warnings = verifyReport.issues.filter((i) => i.level === 'warning').length;
  const anchors = {
    files: verifyReport.files.length,
    total: verifyReport.files.reduce((acc, f) => acc + f.anchors.length, 0),
    issues: verifyReport.issues.length,
    errors,
    warnings,
  };

  const deps = {
    tsx: hasModule('tsx', absDir),
    prettier: hasModule('prettier', absDir),
  };

  const suggestions: string[] = [];
  if (!git.isRepo) suggestions.push('当前目录不是 git 仓库，--since 用法受限（可用 --since-file）');
  if (git.dirty) suggestions.push('git 有 ' + git.changedFiles + ' 个改动文件，可用 tred verify --since HEAD 只扫改动');
  if (errors > 0) suggestions.push(errors + ' 个 error 级锚点问题，运行 tred verify --strict 查看');
  if (warnings > 0) suggestions.push(warnings + ' 个 warning 级锚点问题');
  if (!cache.exists) suggestions.push('缓存未启用，tred verify --cache 可加速重复扫描');
  if (packageVersion !== 'unknown' && tredVersion !== 'unknown' && packageVersion !== tredVersion) {
    suggestions.push('版本不一致：package.json=' + packageVersion + ' vs .tred-version=' + tredVersion);
  }
  if (!deps.tsx) suggestions.push('tsx 未安装：npm i -D tsx');
  if (suggestions.length === 0) suggestions.push('环境健康，一切正常');

  return {
    ok: errors === 0,
    tredVersion,
    packageVersion,
    nodeVersion: process.version,
    platform: process.platform + '/' + process.arch,
    dir: absDir,
    git,
    cache,
    anchors,
    deps,
    suggestions,
  };
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/fingerprint.ts
================================================================================
import { createHash } from 'node:crypto';

/**
 * 基于"意图"生成 8 位指纹：
 * - anchor：被替换的锚点名
 * - source：脚本标识（比如 "graph-engine-migrator"）
 * - version：脚本版本号
 *
 * 不要传代码内容进来——Prettier/ESLint 一跑就全变了。
 */
export function makeFingerprint(parts: {
  anchor: string;
  source: string;
  version: string;
}): string {
  return createHash('sha1')
    .update(JSON.stringify(parts))
    .digest('hex')
    .slice(0, 8);
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/fix.ts
================================================================================
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import ts from 'typescript';
import type {
  FixAction,
  FixFileResult,
  FixOptions,
  FixRemainingOrphan,
  FixReport,
} from './types.js';
import { scanFileAnchors, verifyDir } from './verify.js';
import { checkSyntax } from './safety.js';
import { unifiedDiff } from './diff.js';
import { atomicWrite } from './atomic.js';
import { loadCache, saveCache, statFile } from './cache.js';

export interface FixFileOptions {
  /** 是否尝试补全孤儿 START（默认 false） */
  fixOrphanStart?: boolean;
}

export interface FixFileCompute {
  nextSource: string;
  actions: FixAction[];
  remainingOrphanStarts: FixRemainingOrphan[];
}

function computeLineStarts(lines: string[]): number[] {
  const acc: number[] = [];
  let pos = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    acc[i] = pos;
    pos += lines[i].length + 1;
  }
  return acc;
}

function lineOfPos(lineStarts: number[], pos: number): number {
  let lo = 0;
  let hi = lineStarts.length - 1;
  while (lo < hi) {
    const mid = (lo + hi + 1) >> 1;
    if (lineStarts[mid] <= pos) lo = mid; else hi = mid - 1;
  }
  return lo + 1;
}

/** 判断 pos 是否在文件顶层（不在 Block/ClassBody/ModuleBlock 内） */
function isTopLevel(sf: ts.SourceFile, pos: number): boolean {
  let node: ts.Node = sf;
  for (;;) {
    let next: ts.Node | null = null;
    ts.forEachChild(node, (c) => {
      if (next) return;
      if (c.getStart(sf) <= pos && pos <= c.getEnd()) next = c;
    });
    if (!next) return true;
    const n: ts.Node = next;
    if (
      ts.isBlock(n) ||
      ts.isModuleBlock(n) ||
      ts.isClassLike(n) ||
      ts.isInterfaceDeclaration(n)
    ) return false;
    node = n;
  }
}

/** 收集所有可作为"块内容"的节点（Statement / ClassElement），按 start 升序 */
function collectEligibleNodes(sf: ts.SourceFile): { start: number; end: number }[] {
  const nodes: { start: number; end: number }[] = [];
  const visit = (node: ts.Node): void => {
    if (ts.isStatement(node) || ts.isClassElement(node)) {
      nodes.push({ start: node.getStart(sf), end: node.getEnd() });
    }
    ts.forEachChild(node, visit);
  };
  visit(sf);
  nodes.sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start);
  return nodes;
}

interface OrphanStartFixer {
  nextSource: string;
  actions: FixAction[];
  remaining: FixRemainingOrphan[];
}

function fixOrphanStarts(source: string, orphanStarts: { line: number; name: string }[]): OrphanStartFixer {
  const lines = source.split("\n");
  const lineStarts = computeLineStarts(lines);
  const sf = ts.createSourceFile("x.ts", source, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
  const eligible = collectEligibleNodes(sf);

  // 所有 START 行（含健康 + 孤儿），用于跨锚点检测
  const allAnchorStartLines = new Set<number>();
  {
    const scan = scanFileAnchors(source);
    for (const a of scan.anchors) allAnchorStartLines.add(a.line);
    for (const o of scan.orphans) if (o.kind === "start") allAnchorStartLines.add(o.line);
  }

  const actions: FixAction[] = [];
  const remaining: FixRemainingOrphan[] = [];
  const insertions: { pos: number; text: string; line: number; name: string }[] = [];

  for (const o of orphanStarts) {
    const startLineEndPos = (lineStarts[o.line - 1] ?? 0) + (lines[o.line - 1]?.length ?? 0);
    const target = eligible.find((n) => n.start > startLineEndPos);

    if (!target) {
      if (!isTopLevel(sf, startLineEndPos)) {
        remaining.push({ file: "", line: o.line, anchorName: o.name, kind: "start" });
        continue;
      }
      const indent = (lines[o.line - 1].match(/^[\t ]*/) || [""])[0];
      const hadTrailing = source.endsWith("\n");
      insertions.push({
        pos: source.length,
        text: (hadTrailing ? "" : "\n") + indent + "// @TRED_END: " + o.name + (hadTrailing ? "\n" : ""),
        line: o.line,
        name: o.name,
      });
      actions.push({
        kind: "add-orphan-end",
        line: o.line,
        anchorName: o.name,
        description: "补孤儿 END 到文件末尾: \"" + o.name + "\" @ L" + o.line,
      });
      continue;
    }

    const targetEndLine = lineOfPos(lineStarts, target.end);
    let crossed = false;
    for (let ln = o.line + 1; ln <= targetEndLine; ln++) {
      if (allAnchorStartLines.has(ln)) {
        crossed = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (crossed) {
      remaining.push({ file: "", line: o.line, anchorName: o.name, kind: "start" });
      continue;
    }

    const indent = (lines[o.line - 1].match(/^[\t ]*/) || [""])[0];
    insertions.push({
      pos: target.end,
      text: "\n" + indent + "// @TRED_END: " + o.name,
      line: o.line,
      name: o.name,
    });
    actions.push({
      kind: "add-orphan-end",
      line: o.line,
      anchorName: o.name,
      description: "补孤儿 END 到 L" + targetEndLine + " 之后: \"" + o.name + "\" @ L" + o.line,
    });
  }

  insertions.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.pos !== b.pos) return b.pos - a.pos;
    return b.line - a.line;
  });

  let nextSource = source;
  for (const ins of insertions) {
    nextSource = nextSource.slice(0, ins.pos) + ins.text + nextSource.slice(ins.pos);
  }
  return { nextSource, actions, remaining };
}

/**
 * 修复：删孤儿 END（总是）。fixOrphanStart=true 时额外补孤儿 END（AST 定位，保守）。
 */
export function fixFileContent(source: string, options?: FixFileOptions): FixFileCompute {
  const opts = options || {};
  const scan = scanFileAnchors(source);
  const orphanEnds = scan.orphans.filter((o) => o.kind === "end");

  const actions: FixAction[] = [];
  let working = source;

  if (orphanEnds.length > 0) {
    const lines = working.split("\n");
    const kept: string[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      const lineNo = i + 1;
      const orphan = orphanEnds.find((o) => o.line === lineNo);
      if (orphan) {
        actions.push({
          kind: "delete-orphan-end",
          line: lineNo,
          anchorName: orphan.name,
          description: "删除孤儿 END: \"" + orphan.name + "\" @ L" + lineNo,
        });
        continue;
      }
      kept.push(lines[i]);
    }
    working = kept.join("\n");
  }

  let remainingOrphanStarts: FixRemainingOrphan[] = [];

  if (opts.fixOrphanStart) {
    const scan2 = scanFileAnchors(working);
    const orphanStarts = scan2.orphans.filter((o) => o.kind === "start");
    if (orphanStarts.length > 0) {
      const r = fixOrphanStarts(working, orphanStarts.map((o) => ({ line: o.line, name: o.name })));
      working = r.nextSource;
      actions.push(...r.actions);
      remainingOrphanStarts = r.remaining;
    }
  } else {
    const scan2 = scanFileAnchors(working);
    remainingOrphanStarts = scan2.orphans
      .filter((o) => o.kind === "start")
      .map((o) => ({ file: "", line: o.line, anchorName: o.name, kind: "start" as const }));
  }

  return { nextSource: working, actions, remainingOrphanStarts };
}

function updateCacheForApplied(
  cacheDir: string,
  dir: string,
  relFiles: string[],
): void {
  const cache = loadCache(cacheDir);
  if (!cache) return;
  for (const rel of relFiles) {
    const fullPath = resolve(dir, rel);
    const sig = statFile(fullPath);
    if (!sig) continue;
    const source = readFileSync(fullPath, 'utf-8');
    const scan = scanFileAnchors(source);
    const hasContent = scan.anchors.length > 0 || scan.orphans.length > 0;
    cache.entries[rel] = {
      mtimeMs: sig.mtimeMs,
      size: sig.size,
      file: hasContent ? { file: rel, anchors: scan.anchors, orphans: scan.orphans } : null,
    };
  }
  saveCache(cacheDir, cache);
}

export function fixDir(opts: FixOptions): FixReport {
  const apply = Boolean(opts.apply);
  const useCache = Boolean(opts.cache);
  const cacheDir = opts.cacheDir ?? '.tred-cache';

  const scanReport = verifyDir({
    dir: opts.dir,
    extensions: opts.extensions,
    ignore: opts.ignore,
    cache: useCache,
    cacheDir: cacheDir,
  });

  const files: FixFileResult[] = [];
  const remainingOrphanStarts: FixRemainingOrphan[] = [];
  let totalActions = 0;

  for (const fr of scanReport.files) {
    const fullPath = resolve(opts.dir, fr.file);
    let original: string;
    try {
      original = readFileSync(fullPath, 'utf-8');
    } catch {
      continue;
    }
    const computed = fixFileContent(original, { fixOrphanStart: opts.fixOrphanStart });

    for (const o of computed.remainingOrphanStarts) {
      remainingOrphanStarts.push({ file: fr.file, line: o.line, anchorName: o.anchorName, kind: 'start' });
    }

    if (computed.actions.length === 0) continue;

    const diff = unifiedDiff(original, computed.nextSource, 'a/' + fr.file, 'b/' + fr.file);
    const result: FixFileResult = {
      file: fr.file,
      actions: computed.actions,
      diff: diff,
      applied: false,
    };

    if (apply) {
      const syntaxErrors = checkSyntax(fullPath, computed.nextSource);
      if (syntaxErrors.length > 0) {
        result.error = '语法校验失败，拒绝写入：' + syntaxErrors.join('; ');
      } else {
        try {
          atomicWrite(fullPath, computed.nextSource);
          result.applied = true;
        } catch (e) {
          result.error = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
        }
      }
    }

    files.push(result);
    totalActions += computed.actions.length;
  }

  // 修复完成后主动更新缓存：仅写入本次修改过的文件（下次 verify 全 hit）
  if (useCache && apply) {
    const applied = files.filter(function (f) { return f.applied; });
    if (applied.length > 0) {
      updateCacheForApplied(
        cacheDir,
        opts.dir,
        applied.map(function (f) { return f.file; }),
      );
    }
  }

  const ok = files.every(function (f) { return !f.error; });
  return {
    ok: ok,
    dryRun: !apply,
    dir: opts.dir,
    scannedFiles: scanReport.scannedFiles,
    files: files,
    totalActions: totalActions,
    remainingOrphanStarts: remainingOrphanStarts,
  };
}

let wasmInstance: any = null;
let memory: WebAssembly.Memory | null = null;

if (WASM_BASE64) {
  try {
    const buf = Buffer.from(WASM_BASE64, 'base64');
    const mod = new WebAssembly.Module(buf);
    wasmInstance = new WebAssembly.Instance(mod, {});
    memory = wasmInstance!.exports.memory;
  } catch (e) {}
}

// 真正的零分配，直接比对 charCode
export function getSimilarity(query: string, target: string): number {
  if (!query || !target) return 0;

  const qLen = query.length;
  const tLen = target.length;

  // ── 1. 短文本极致加速（零内存分配） ──
  if (qLen < 100 && tLen < 100) {
    const qParts: { s: number, e: number }[] = [];
    let qStart = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i <= qLen; i++) {
      const c = i < qLen ? query.charCodeAt(i) : -1;
      if (c === -1 || c === 32 || c === 45 || c === 95 || (c >= 65 && c <= 90 && i > qStart)) {
        if (i > qStart) qParts.push({ s: qStart, e: i });
        qStart = i;
      }
    }

    let intersection = 0;
    let tStart = 0;
    let tCount = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i <= tLen; i++) {
      const c = i < tLen ? target.charCodeAt(i) : -1;
      if (c === -1 || c === 32 || c === 45 || c === 95 || (c >= 65 && c <= 90 && i > tStart)) {
        if (i > tStart) {
          const tWordLen = i - tStart;
          tCount++;
          // 在 qParts 里寻找相等的词
          for (let j = 0; j < qParts.length; j++) {
            const qp = qParts[j];
            if (qp.e - qp.s === tWordLen) {
              let match = true;
              for (let k = 0; k < tWordLen; k++) {
                let qc = query.charCodeAt(qp.s + k);
                let tc = target.charCodeAt(tStart + k);
                if (qc >= 65 && qc <= 90) qc += 32;
                if (tc >= 65 && tc <= 90) tc += 32;
                if (qc !== tc) { match = false; break; }
              }
              if (match) { intersection++; break; }
            }
          }
        }
        tStart = i;
      }
    }

    // 子串完全包含，直接给高分
    const qLower = query.toLowerCase();
    const tLower = target.toLowerCase();
    if (qLower.includes(tLower) || tLower.includes(qLower)) return 0.9;

    if (qParts.length === 0 || tCount === 0) return 0;
    return (2 * intersection) / (qParts.length + tCount);
  }

  // ── 2. 超大字符串走 WASM ──
  if (wasmInstance && memory && qLen >= 100) {
    try {
      const qBytes = Buffer.from(query);
      const tBytes = Buffer.from(target);
      const total = qBytes.length + tBytes.length;
      if (memory.buffer.byteLength < total + 1024) {
        memory.grow(Math.ceil((total + 1024 - memory.buffer.byteLength) / 65536));
      }
      const mem = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer);
      mem.set(qBytes, 1024);
      mem.set(tBytes, 1024 + qBytes.length);
      return wasmInstance.exports.get_similarity(1024, qBytes.length, 1024 + qBytes.length, tBytes.length);
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  return 0;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/git.ts

import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

function run(args: string[], cwd?: string): { ok: boolean; stdout: string } {
  const r = spawnSync('git', args, { cwd: cwd ?? process.cwd(), encoding: 'utf-8' });
  return { ok: r.status === 0, stdout: r.stdout || '' };
}

export function isGitRepo(cwd?: string): boolean {
  return run(['rev-parse', '--git-dir'], cwd).ok;
}

export function getCurrentBranch(cwd?: string): string | null {
  const r = run(['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'], cwd);
  return r.ok ? r.stdout.trim() : null;
}

export function getHeadSha(cwd?: string): string | null {
  const r = run(['rev-parse', 'HEAD'], cwd);
  return r.ok ? r.stdout.trim() : null;
}

export function isDirty(cwd?: string): boolean {
  const r = run(['status', '--porcelain'], cwd);
  return r.ok && r.stdout.trim().length > 0;
}

export function getMergeBase(target: string, cwd?: string): string | null {
  const r = run(['merge-base', target, 'HEAD'], cwd);
  return r.ok ? r.stdout.trim() : null;
}

export function getStagedFiles(cwd?: string): string[] {
  const r = run(['diff', '--name-only', '--diff-filter=ACMR', '--cached'], cwd);
  if (!r.ok) return [];
  return r.stdout.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}

export function getUnstagedFiles(cwd?: string): string[] {
  const r = run(['diff', '--name-only', '--diff-filter=ACMR'], cwd);
  if (!r.ok) return [];
  return r.stdout.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}

export function getUntrackedFiles(cwd?: string): string[] {
  const r = run(['ls-files', '--others', '--exclude-standard'], cwd);
  if (!r.ok) return [];
  return r.stdout.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
}

function parseRange(ref: string): string {
  const t = ref.trim();
  if (!t) return 'HEAD';
  if (t.indexOf('...') >= 0) return t;
  if (t === 'HEAD') return 'HEAD';
  return t + '...HEAD';
}

export function getChangedFiles(ref: string, cwd?: string): string[] {
  if (!isGitRepo(cwd)) return [];
  const set = new Set<string>();
  const range = parseRange(ref);
  const r = run(['diff', '--name-only', '--diff-filter=ACMR', range], cwd);
  if (r.ok) {
    for (const line of r.stdout.split('\n')) {
      const t = line.trim();
      if (t) set.add(t);
    }
  }
  for (const f of getStagedFiles(cwd)) set.add(f);
  for (const f of getUnstagedFiles(cwd)) set.add(f);
  for (const f of getUntrackedFiles(cwd)) set.add(f);
  return Array.from(set).sort();
}

export function gitShow(ref: string, path: string, cwd?: string): string | null {
  const r = run(['show', ref + ':' + path], cwd);
  return r.ok ? r.stdout : null;
}

export function globToRegex(glob: string): RegExp {
  let re = '^';
  for (let i = 0; i < glob.length; i++) {
    const ch = glob[i];
    if (ch === '*') {
      if (glob[i + 1] === '*') { re += '.*'; i++; }
      else { re += '[^/]*'; }
    } else if (ch === '?') { re += '[^/]'; }
    else if ('.+^$()|[]\\\\{}'.indexOf(ch) >= 0) { re += '\\\\' + ch; }
    else { re += ch; }
  }
  re += '$';
  return new RegExp(re);
}

export function matchesAnyGlob(relPath: string, globs: string[]): boolean {
  if (globs.length === 0) return false;
  const base = relPath.split('/').pop() ?? relPath;
  for (const g of globs) {
    const re = globToRegex(g);
    if (re.test(relPath) || re.test(base)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

import { existsSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, relative } from 'node:path';
import { buildOutline } from './ast-outline.js';

export function grepDir(dir: string, pattern: string, useAst: boolean, useHtml: boolean): void {
  const re = new RegExp(pattern, 'gi');
  const ignore = new Set(['node_modules', '.git', 'dist', '.tred-tmp']);
  const htmlResults: { file: string, context: string, line: number, text: string }[] = [];

  function walk(d: string) {
    let entries;
    try { entries = readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }); } catch { return; }
    for (const e of entries) {
      if (e.isDirectory()) {
        if (!ignore.has(e.name)) walk(join(d, e.name));
      } else if (e.isFile() && /\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx|mjs|cjs)$/.test(e.name)) {
        const full = join(d, e.name);
        const source = readFileSync(full, 'utf-8');
        const lines = source.split('\n');
        let outline: any[] = [];
        if (useAst) { try { outline = buildOutline(full, source); } catch {} }

        for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
          if (re.test(lines[i])) {
            let contextStr = '';
            if (useAst && outline.length > 0) {
              let bestNode: any = null;
              function findNode(nodes: any[]) {
                for (const n of nodes) {
                  if (n.line <= i + 1) {
                    if (!bestNode || n.line > bestNode.line) bestNode = n;
                    if (n.children) findNode(n.children);
                  }
                }
              }
              findNode(outline);
              if (bestNode) contextStr = `[in ${bestNode.kind}: ${bestNode.name}]`;
            }
            const relPath = relative(process.cwd(), full);
            re.lastIndex = 0;
            if (useHtml) htmlResults.push({ file: relPath, context: contextStr, line: i + 1, text: lines[i].trim() });
            else console.log(`${relPath}:${i + 1}:${contextStr} ${lines[i].trim()}`);
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
  walk(dir);

  if (useHtml) {
    const html = `<!DOCTYPE html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>tred grep</title><style>body{font-family:sans-serif;padding:2em;background:#f6f8fa;} .card{background:#fff;border:1px solid #d0d7de;margin-bottom:1em;padding:1em;border-radius:6px;} .ctx{color:#0969da;font-weight:bold;} .line{font-family:monospace;margin-top:0.5em;}</style></head><body><h1>🔍 tred grep: ${pattern}</h1><p>找到 ${htmlResults.length} 处匹配</p>${htmlResults.map(r => `<div class="card"><strong>${r.file}:${r.line}</strong> <span class="ctx">${r.context}</span><div class="line">${r.text.replace(/</g,'&lt;')}</div></div>`).join('')}</body></html>`;
    writeFileSync('tred-grep-report.html', html);
    console.log('  ✓ HTML 报告已生成: tred-grep-report.html');
  }
}

import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, relative } from 'node:path';
import ts from 'typescript';

export function analyzeImpact(dir: string, target: string): string {
  const ignore = new Set(['node_modules', '.git', 'dist', '.tred-tmp', '.tred']);
  const impacts: string[] = [];
  let totalRefs = 0;

  function walk(d: string) {
    let entries;
    try { entries = readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }); } catch { return; }
    for (const e of entries) {
      if (e.isDirectory()) {
        if (!ignore.has(e.name)) walk(join(d, e.name));
      } else if (e.isFile() && /\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx|mjs|cjs)$/.test(e.name)) {
        const full = join(d, e.name);
        const source = readFileSync(full, 'utf-8');
        const sf = ts.createSourceFile(full, source, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
        const fileRefs: string[] = [];

        function visit(node: ts.Node, context: string) {
          let currentContext = context;
          if (ts.isFunctionDeclaration(node) && node.name) currentContext = `function ${node.name.text}`;
          else if (ts.isClassDeclaration(node) && node.name) currentContext = `class ${node.name.text}`;
          else if (ts.isMethodDeclaration(node) && ts.isIdentifier(node.name)) currentContext = `method ${node.name.text}`;

          if (ts.isIdentifier(node) && node.text === target) {
            // 排除自身声明的行
            const isDecl = ts.isVariableDeclaration(node.parent) || ts.isFunctionDeclaration(node.parent) || ts.isClassDeclaration(node.parent);
            if (!isDecl) {
              fileRefs.push(currentContext || 'top-level');
            }
          }
          ts.forEachChild(node, (child) => visit(child, currentContext));
        }
        visit(sf, '');

        if (fileRefs.length > 0) {
          totalRefs += fileRefs.length;
          const uniqueCtx = Array.from(new Set(fileRefs));
          const rel = relative(dir, full);
          impacts.push(`  - ${rel}: 被引用于 [ ${uniqueCtx.join(', ')} ]`);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  walk(dir);

  if (totalRefs === 0) return `\n✅ 安全：没有找到任何对 "${target}" 的引用。`;
  return `\n🚨 影响分析: "${target}" 共计被引用 ${totalRefs} 次，涉及 ${impacts.length} 个文件：\n${impacts.join('\n')}`;
}
