import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { updateAnchorState, getAnchorState } from './state.js';
import { getParser } from './parsers/index.js';
import { detectIndent, findAllAnchors, formatAnchor } from './anchor.js';
import { findNodeRange } from './ast.js';
import { findSnippetRange } from './snippet.js';
import { atomicWrite } from './atomic.js';
import { checkSyntax, checkAssertions } from './safety.js';
import { unifiedDiff } from './diff.js';
import type { BatchResult, ReplaceManyOptions, ReplaceOptions, ReplaceResult, ReplaceStatus } from './types.js';
import { writeRollbackLog } from './rollback-log.js';

export { makeFingerprint } from './fingerprint.js';
export { checkSyntax } from './safety.js';
export { unifiedDiff } from './diff.js';
export { buildOutline } from './ast-outline.js';
export type {
  AstSelector,
  BatchResult,
  BatchStatus,
  Formatter,
  OutlineKind,
  OutlineNode,
  ReplaceOptions,
  ReplaceResult,
  ReplaceStatus,
} from './types.js';

export function normalizeContent(content: string): string {
  const lines = content.split('\n');
  let lastIdx = lines.length - 1;
  while (lastIdx >= 0 && lines[lastIdx].trim() === '') lastIdx--;
  if (lastIdx < 0) return content;
  const baseMatch = lines[lastIdx].match(/^[\t ]*/);
  const baseIndent = baseMatch ? baseMatch[0] : '';
  if (baseIndent.length === 0) return content;
  return lines.map(function (line) {
    let k = 0;
    while (k < baseIndent.length && k < line.length && (line[k] === ' ' || line[k] === '\t')) k++;
    return line.slice(k);
  }).join('\n');
}

function indentLines(text: string, indent: string): string {
  if (!indent) return text;
  return text.split('\n').map(function (line) { return line ? indent + line : line; }).join('\n');
}

function buildBlock(anchor: string, fp: string, content: string, indent: string): string {
  const pair = formatAnchor(anchor, fp);
  const normalized = normalizeContent(content).replace(/\n+$/, '');
  const body = indentLines(normalized, indent);
  return indent + pair.start + '\n' + body + '\n' + indent + pair.end;
}

interface ComputeOutcome {
  nextSource: string | null;
  status: ReplaceStatus;
  message?: string;
  syntaxErrors?: string[];
  diff?: string;
}

interface ComputeOutcome {
  nextSource: string | null;
  status: ReplaceStatus;
  message?: string;
  syntaxErrors?: string[];
  diff?: string;
  failureContext?: { oldCode: string; newCode: string };
}

function saveFailureCache(file: string, reason: string, context?: { oldCode: string; newCode: string }) {
  try {
    mkdirSync('.tred', { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync('.tred/last-failure.json', JSON.stringify({
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      file,
      reason,
      oldCode: context?.oldCode || null,
      newCode: context?.newCode || null
    }, null, 2));
  } catch (e) { /* 忽略 */ }
}

async function computeReplace(original: string, opts: ReplaceOptions): Promise<ComputeOutcome> {
  const file = opts.file;
  const content = opts.content;
  const fingerprint = opts.fingerprint || '';

  let range: { start: number; end: number } | null = null;
  let status: 'injected' | 'replaced' = 'replaced';
  let indent = '';

  const parser = getParser(file);

  if (opts.selector && opts.selector.kind === 'file') {
    const fs2 = opts.selector as any;
    const lines = original.split('\n');
    const lineStarts: number[] = [];
    let pos = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      lineStarts.push(pos);
      pos += lines[i].length + 1;
    }
    let startLine = 0;
    let endLine = lines.length - 1;
    if (fs2.scope === 'tail') {
      if (fs2.snippet) {
        const idx = lines.findIndex((l) => l.includes(fs2.snippet));
        if (idx < 0) return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '找不到 snippet: ' + fs2.snippet };
        startLine = idx;
      } else if (fs2.line) { startLine = fs2.line - 1; }
    } else if (fs2.scope === 'head') {
      if (fs2.snippet) {
        const idx = lines.findIndex((l) => l.includes(fs2.snippet));
        if (idx < 0) return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '找不到 snippet: ' + fs2.snippet };
        endLine = idx - 1;
      } else if (fs2.line) { endLine = fs2.line - 1; }
    }
    if (startLine < 0 || endLine >= lines.length || startLine > endLine) {
      return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '范围非法' };
    }
    const startPos = lineStarts[startLine];
    const endPos = lineStarts[endLine] + lines[endLine].length;
    range = { start: startPos, end: endPos };
    status = 'replaced';
  } else if (opts.selector && !parser) {
    range = findNodeRange(file, original, opts.selector as any);
    if (!range) return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: 'AST 找不到节点。建议：如果是局部作用域或复杂属性，请改用 kind: "file" 覆盖' };
    indent = detectIndent(original, range.start);
    status = 'replaced';
  } else if (opts.selector && parser) {
    range = parser.findNode(original, opts.selector);
    if (!range) return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: 'AST 找不到节点 (Parser: ' + file + ')' };
    status = 'replaced';
  } else if (opts.anchor && parser && opts.anchor.startsWith('auto-')) {
    const state = getAnchorState(file, opts.anchor);
    if (!state) return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '找不到外部锚点记录: ' + opts.anchor };
    const sig = JSON.parse(state.astSignature);
    range = parser.findNode(original, sig);
    if (!range) return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '根据 AST 特征未能找到节点' };
    status = 'replaced';
  } else if (opts.anchor) {
    const allAnchors = findAllAnchors(original, opts.anchor, opts.fuzzy);
    if (allAnchors.length > 1) return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '锚点名出现多次' };
    const block = allAnchors[0];
    if (block) {
      if (block.fp === fingerprint) return { nextSource: null, status: 'skipped', message: '指纹一致，跳过' };
      range = { start: block.startLineStart, end: block.endLineEnd };
      indent = detectIndent(original, block.startLineStart);
    } else {
      if (!opts.autoInjectAnchor || !opts.astSelector) {
        return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '找不到锚点且未配置 autoInjectAnchor' };
      }
      const astRange = findNodeRange(file, original, opts.astSelector);
      if (!astRange) return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: 'AST 找不到节点' };
      range = astRange;
      indent = detectIndent(original, range.start);
      status = 'injected';
    }
  } else if (opts.snippetSelector) {
    const snipRange = findSnippetRange(original, opts.snippetSelector);
    if (!snipRange) return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '找不到匹配的代码片段' };
    range = snipRange;
    indent = detectIndent(original, range.start);
  } else if (opts.astSelector) {
    const astRange = findNodeRange(file, original, opts.astSelector);
    if (!astRange) return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: 'AST 找不到节点' };
    range = astRange;
    indent = detectIndent(original, range.start);
  } else {
    return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '缺少定位条件' };
  }

  if (!range) return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '内部错误：找不到有效的范围' };

  const normalized = normalizeContent(content).replace(/\n+$/, '');
  let newBlock = '';
  if (status === 'injected') {
    newBlock = buildBlock(opts.anchor!, fingerprint, content, indent);
  } else if (opts.anchor) {
    newBlock = buildBlock(opts.anchor, fingerprint, content, indent);
  } else {
    // 核心修复：判断是否在行首，如果在行首，才统一加缩进，否则首行保持原样
    const lineStartPos = original.lastIndexOf('\n', range.start) + 1;
    const beforeNode = original.slice(lineStartPos, range.start);
    const isAtLineStart = beforeNode.trim().length === 0;
    
    if (isAtLineStart) {
      newBlock = indentLines(normalized, indent);
    } else {
      const lines = normalized.split('\n');
      newBlock = lines[0] + lines.slice(1).map((l: string) => l ? indent + l : l).join('\n');
    }
  }

  const oldCode = original.slice(range.start, range.end);
  const failureContext = { oldCode, newCode: newBlock };

  if (opts.assertions) {
    const assertErrors = checkAssertions(normalized, opts.assertions);
    if (assertErrors.length > 0) {
      saveFailureCache(file, '智能断言失败', failureContext);
      return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '智能断言失败', syntaxErrors: assertErrors, failureContext };
    }
  }

  let nextSource = '';
  if (status === 'injected') {
    const lineStart = range.start - indent.length;
    nextSource = original.slice(0, lineStart) + newBlock + original.slice(range.end);
  } else {
    nextSource = original.slice(0, range.start) + newBlock + original.slice(range.end);
  }

  if (opts.format) {
    try {
      nextSource = await opts.format(nextSource, file);
    } catch (e) {
      saveFailureCache(file, '格式化失败', failureContext);
      return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '格式化失败: ' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)), failureContext };
    }
  }

  let syntaxErrors: string[] = [];
  const isTsLike = /\.[jt]sx?$/.test(file);
  if (isTsLike) {
    syntaxErrors = checkSyntax(file, nextSource);
  } else if (parser) {
    syntaxErrors = parser.checkSyntax(nextSource);
  }

  if (syntaxErrors.length > 0) {
    saveFailureCache(file, '语法校验失败', failureContext);
    return { nextSource: null, status: 'failed', message: '语法校验失败，拒绝写入', syntaxErrors, failureContext };
  }

  const diff = unifiedDiff(original, nextSource, 'a/' + file, 'b/' + file, original.length > 5000 ? 3 : 999999);
  return { nextSource, status, diff };
}

export async function replace(opts: ReplaceOptions): Promise<ReplaceResult> {
  if (!existsSync(opts.file)) {
    return { status: 'failed', file: opts.file, anchor: opts.anchor, message: '文件不存在: ' + opts.file };
  }
  const original = readFileSync(opts.file, 'utf-8');
  const c = await computeReplace(original, opts);
  const result: ReplaceResult = {
    status: c.status, file: opts.file, anchor: opts.anchor,
    message: c.message, syntaxErrors: c.syntaxErrors,
    failureContext: c.failureContext, // 👈 补上这个，CLI 才能拿到
  };
  if (opts.dryRun) {
    result.diff = c.diff;
    if (!result.message) result.message = 'dry-run：未写盘';
    return result;
  }
  if (c.nextSource !== null) atomicWrite(opts.file, c.nextSource);
  return result;
}

export async function replaceMany(ops: ReplaceOptions[], options?: ReplaceManyOptions): Promise<BatchResult> {
  const anyDryRun = ops.some(function (o) { return Boolean(o.dryRun); });
  const continueOnError = Boolean(options?.continueOnError);
  const results: ReplaceResult[] = new Array(ops.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < ops.length; i++) {
    results[i] = { status: 'failed', file: ops[i].file, anchor: ops[i].anchor, message: '未执行' };
  }

  // 阶段 1：前置检查（文件存在性）
  const missing = new Set<string>();
  for (let i = 0; i < ops.length; i++) {
    if (!existsSync(ops[i].file)) {
      results[i] = {
        status: 'failed',
        file: ops[i].file,
        anchor: ops[i].anchor,
        message: '文件不存在: ' + ops[i].file,
      };
      missing.add(ops[i].file);
    }
  }
  if (missing.size > 0 && !continueOnError) {
    return { status: 'rolled-back', results: results, committedFiles: [] };
  }

  // 阶段 2：按文件分组
  const groups = new Map<string, { op: ReplaceOptions; index: number }[]>();
  for (let i = 0; i < ops.length; i++) {
    const op = ops[i];
    if (missing.has(op.file)) continue;
    if (!groups.has(op.file)) groups.set(op.file, []);
    groups.get(op.file)!.push({ op: op, index: i });
  }

  const originals = new Map<string, string>();
  const pending = new Map<string, string>();
  let earlyExit: BatchResult | null = null;

  // 阶段 3：文件级并发（每个文件内部 op 仍串行）
  const fileTasks = Array.from(groups.entries()).map(async ([file, items]) => {
    if (earlyExit) return;
    const original = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
    originals.set(file, original);
    let source = original;
    let dirty = false;
    let failedInThisFile = false;

    for (const item of items) {
      if (failedInThisFile) {
        results[item.index] = {
          status: 'failed',
          file: file,
          anchor: item.op.anchor,
          message: '同文件内前置 op 失败，跳过',
        };
        continue;
      }
      const c = await computeReplace(source, item.op);
      results[item.index] = {
        status: c.status,
        file: file,
        anchor: item.op.anchor,
        message: c.message,
        syntaxErrors: c.syntaxErrors,
        diff: c.diff,
      };
      if (c.status === 'failed') {
        failedInThisFile = true;
        // 回写同文件已成功 compute 的 op 状态：本文件整体 skip
        for (const prev of items) {
          if (prev.index === item.index) break;
          const pr = results[prev.index];
          if (pr.status !== 'failed') {
            results[prev.index] = {
              status: 'failed',
              file: file,
              anchor: prev.op.anchor,
              message: '同文件内后续 op 失败，本文件整体 skip',
            };
          }
        }
        if (!continueOnError) {
          earlyExit = { status: 'rolled-back', results: results, committedFiles: [] };
        }
      } else if (c.nextSource !== null) {
        source = c.nextSource;
        dirty = true;
      }
    }
    if (!failedInThisFile && dirty) pending.set(file, source);
  });

  await Promise.all(fileTasks);
  if (earlyExit) return earlyExit;

  if (anyDryRun) {
    return { status: 'dry-run', results: results, committedFiles: [] };
  }

  // 阶段 4：提交（必须串行，保证事务语义）
  const writer = options?._writer ?? atomicWrite;
  const committed: string[] = [];
  const attempted = Array.from(pending.keys());

  if (continueOnError) {
    for (const [file, nextSource] of pending) {
      try {
        writer(file, nextSource);
        committed.push(file);
      } catch (e) {
        const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
        const items = groups.get(file)!;
        for (const item of items) {
          results[item.index] = {
            status: 'failed',
            file: file,
            anchor: item.op.anchor,
            message: '提交失败: ' + msg,
          };
        }
      }
    }
    const anyFailed = results.some(function (r) { return r.status === 'failed'; });
    if (anyFailed && committed.length === 0) {
      return { status: 'rolled-back', results: results, committedFiles: [] };
    }
    if (anyFailed) {
      return { status: 'partial', results: results, committedFiles: committed };
    }
    return { status: 'committed', results: results, committedFiles: committed };
  }

  let failedFile: string | null = null;
  let commitError: string | null = null;
  const rollbackErrors: { file: string; message: string }[] = [];

  try {
    for (const [file, nextSource] of pending) {
      try {
        writer(file, nextSource);
        committed.push(file);
      } catch (e) {
        failedFile = file;
        throw e;
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    commitError = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
    for (const file of committed) {
      try { writer(file, originals.get(file)!); }
      catch (err) {
        rollbackErrors.push({ file: file, message: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });
      }
    }

    if (options?.rollbackLog) {
      try {
        writeRollbackLog(
          {
            timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
            status: 'rolled-back',
            error: commitError,
            commitPhase: {
              attempted: attempted,
              committed: committed.slice(),
              failed: failedFile,
              rollbackErrors: rollbackErrors,
            },
            ops: results.map(function (r) { return Object.assign({}, r); }),
          },
          options.rollbackLog,
        );
      } catch (logErr) {
        commitError = commitError + ' | rollback-log 写入失败: ' + (logErr instanceof Error ? logErr.message : String(logErr));
      }
    }

    return { status: 'rolled-back', results: results, committedFiles: [], error: commitError };
  }

  return { status: 'committed', results: results, committedFiles: committed };
}

import { existsSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, chmodSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import type { InitOptions, InitResult } from './types.js';

const CONFIG_PATH = join('.tred', 'config.json');
const HOOK_PATH = join('.git', 'hooks', 'pre-commit');

const CONFIG_TEMPLATE = "{\n  \"dir\": \"src\",\n  \"exclude\": [\n    \"**/*.test.ts\",\n    \"**/*.spec.ts\"\n  ],\n  \"baseline\": \".tred-baseline.json\",\n  \"cacheDir\": \".tred-cache\"\n}\n";

const HOOK_TEMPLATE = "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# 由 tred init 生成\nset -uo pipefail\nDIR=\"$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null || echo .)\"\ncd \"$DIR\"\n\nif [ -f .tred-baseline.json ]; then\n  tred verify --dir src --baseline .tred-baseline.json --strict\nelse\n  tred verify --dir src --strict\nfi\nexit $?\n";

export function init(opts?: InitOptions): InitResult {
  const dir = opts && opts.dir ? opts.dir : process.cwd();
  const withHook = Boolean(opts && opts.withHook);
  const created: string[] = [];
  const skipped: string[] = [];
  const errors: string[] = [];

  const configAbs = resolve(dir, CONFIG_PATH);
  if (existsSync(configAbs)) {
    skipped.push(CONFIG_PATH);
  } else {
    try {
      mkdirSync(resolve(dir, ".tred"), { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(configAbs, CONFIG_TEMPLATE, "utf-8");
      created.push(CONFIG_PATH);
    } catch (e) {
      errors.push(CONFIG_PATH + ": " + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
    }
  }

  let hookPath: string | null = null;
  if (withHook) {
    const hookAbs = resolve(dir, HOOK_PATH);
    if (existsSync(hookAbs)) {
      skipped.push(HOOK_PATH);
    } else {
      try {
        mkdirSync(resolve(dir, ".git/hooks"), { recursive: true });
        writeFileSync(hookAbs, HOOK_TEMPLATE, "utf-8");
        try {
          chmodSync(hookAbs, 0o755);
        } catch { /* ignore */ }
        created.push(HOOK_PATH);
        hookPath = hookAbs;
      } catch (e) {
        errors.push(HOOK_PATH + ": " + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    ok: errors.length === 0,
    configPath: configAbs,
    hookPath: hookPath,
    created: created,
    skipped: skipped,
    errors: errors,
  };
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/map.ts
================================================================================
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, relative } from 'node:path';
import { buildOutline } from './ast-outline.js';

export interface CodeMapFile {
  lines: number;
  outline: any[];
}

export interface CodeMap {
  totalFiles: number;
  totalLines: number;
  files: Record<string, CodeMapFile>;
}

export function generateMap(dir: string): CodeMap {
  const result: CodeMap = { totalFiles: 0, totalLines: 0, files: {} };
  const ignore = new Set(['node_modules', '.git', 'dist', '.tred-tmp', '.tred']);

  function walk(d: string) {
    let entries;
    try { entries = readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true }); } catch { return; }
    for (const e of entries) {
      if (e.isDirectory()) {
        if (!ignore.has(e.name)) walk(join(d, e.name));
      } else if (e.isFile() && /\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx|mjs|cjs)$/.test(e.name)) {
        const full = join(d, e.name);
        const source = readFileSync(full, 'utf-8');
        const lines = source.split('\n').length;
        let outline: any[] = [];
        try { outline = buildOutline(full, source); } catch {}

        if (outline.length > 0) {
          const rel = relative(process.cwd(), full);
          result.files[rel] = { lines, outline };
          result.totalFiles++;
          result.totalLines += lines;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  walk(dir);
  return result;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/ops.ts
================================================================================
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { replace } from './index.js';
import { annotateDir } from './annotate.js';
import { verifyDir } from './verify.js';

export interface OpsConfig {
  ops: Array<any>;
}

function normalizeReplaceOp(raw: any, index: number): { op?: any; errors: string[] } {
  const errors: string[] = [];
  const prefix = 'op[' + index + ']';

  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') return { errors: [prefix + ': 必须是一个 JSON 对象'] };

  let content: string | undefined = undefined;
  if (typeof raw.content === 'string') {
    content = raw.content;
  } else if (typeof raw.contentFile === 'string') {
    try {
      content = readFileSync(raw.contentFile, 'utf-8');
    } catch (e) {
      errors.push(prefix + ': 读取 contentFile 失败（' + raw.contentFile + '）：' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
    }
  }
  if (content === undefined) errors.push(prefix + ': 必须提供 "content" 或 "contentFile"');

  if (typeof raw.file !== 'string') errors.push(prefix + ': 缺少 "file" 字段');

  const selector = raw.selector ?? raw.astSelector;
  const snippetSelector = raw.snippetSelector;
  const anchor = raw.anchor;

  if (!anchor && !selector && !snippetSelector) errors.push(prefix + ': 必须提供 "anchor" / "selector" 之一');
  if (errors.length > 0) return { errors };

  let astSelector: any = undefined;
  let universalSelector: any = undefined;
  if (selector) {
    if (selector.kind === 'file') {
      universalSelector = selector;
    } else if (['class', 'method', 'function', 'statement', 'variable'].includes(selector.kind)) {
      astSelector = selector;
    } else {
      universalSelector = selector;
    }
  }

  const op: any = {
    file: raw.file,
    anchor,
    content,
    fingerprint: raw.fingerprint,
    dryRun: raw.dryRun,
    autoInjectAnchor: raw.autoInjectAnchor,
    fuzzy: raw.fuzzy,
    astSelector,
    selector: universalSelector,
    snippetSelector,
    assertions: raw.assertions,
    format: raw.format,
  };

  if (op.autoInjectAnchor && (!op.anchor || !op.astSelector)) {
    errors.push(prefix + ': 使用 autoInjectAnchor 时，必须提供 "anchor" 和 "selector"');
    return { errors };
  }

  return { op, errors: [] };
}

export async function runOps(filePath: string, flags: Record<string, string | true | string[]>): Promise<number> {
  if (!existsSync(filePath)) {
    console.error('❌ [tred ops] ops 文件不存在: ' + filePath);
    return 1;
  }

  let config: OpsConfig;
  try {
    config = JSON.parse(readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8'));
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('❌ [tred ops] JSON 解析失败：' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
    console.error('   建议：检查有没有多余的逗号、少写的引号');
    return 1;
  }

  if (!config.ops || !Array.isArray(config.ops)) {
    console.error('❌ [tred ops] 格式错误：顶层必须是 {"ops": [...]}');
    return 1;
  }

  let hasError = false;
  for (let i = 0; i < config.ops.length; i++) {
    const raw = config.ops[i];
    const type = raw && raw.type;
    console.log('[tred ops] [' + (i + 1) + '/' + config.ops.length + '] ' + (type || '?') + ' ...');

    try {
      if (type === 'replace') {
        const { op, errors } = normalizeReplaceOp(raw, i);
        if (errors.length > 0) {
          hasError = true;
          for (const err of errors) console.error('  ❌ ' + err);
          continue;
        }
        if (flags['dry-run']) op.dryRun = true;
        const r = await replace(op);
        if (r.status === 'failed') {
          hasError = true;
          console.error('  ❌ ' + (r.message || 'replace 失败'));
          if (r.syntaxErrors) r.syntaxErrors.forEach((e: string) => console.error('     ' + e));
          if (r.failureContext) {
            console.error('\n  ⚠️ 原代码:\n' + r.failureContext.oldCode.split('\n').map((l: string) => '     ' + l).join('\n'));
            console.error('\n  ⚠️ 你的新代码:\n' + r.failureContext.newCode.split('\n').map((l: string) => '     ' + l).join('\n'));
          }
        } else {
          console.log('  ✓ ' + r.status);
        }
      } else if (type === 'annotate') {
        const r = annotateDir(raw);
        if (!r.ok) hasError = true;
        console.log('  ' + (r.ok ? '✓' : '❌') + ' 文件: ' + r.annotatedFiles + ', 动作: ' + r.totalActions);
      } else if (type === 'verify') {
        const r = verifyDir(raw);
        if (!r.ok) hasError = true;
        console.log('  ' + (r.ok ? '✓' : '❌') + ' issues: ' + r.issues.length);
      } else {
        console.warn('  ⚠ 未知操作类型: ' + type);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      hasError = true;
      const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
      if (msg.includes('undefined') || msg.includes('null')) {
        console.error('  ❌ op[' + i + '] 字段类型不对：' + msg);
      } else {
        console.error('  ❌ op[' + i + '] 执行失败：' + msg);
      }
    }
  }
  return hasError ? 1 : 0;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/parsers/css.ts
================================================================================
import postcss from 'postcss';
import type { TredParser, AstRange } from './types.js';

export const cssParser: TredParser = {
  extensions: ['.css', '.scss', '.less'],
  checkSyntax(source: string): string[] {
    try {
      postcss.parse(source);
      return [];
    } catch (e) {
      return [(e as Error).message];
    }
  },
  findNode(source: string, selector: { selector?: string }): AstRange | null {
    if (!selector.selector) return null;
    const root = postcss.parse(source);
    let found = false;
    root.walkRules(rule => {
      if (rule.selector === selector.selector) {
        if (rule.source && rule.source.start && rule.source.end) {
          const start = rule.source.start.offset!;
          const end = rule.source.end.offset! + 1;
          (selector as any).__res = { start, end };
          found = true;
        }
      }
    });
    return found ? (selector as any).__res : null;
  }
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/parsers/html.ts
================================================================================
import { parse } from 'node-html-parser';
import type { TredParser, AstRange } from './types.js';

export const htmlParser: TredParser = {
  extensions: ['.html', '.htm'],
  checkSyntax(source: string): string[] {
    try {
      parse(source);
      return []; // node-html-parser 容错极高，仅拦截严重崩溃
    } catch (e) {
      return [(e as Error).message];
    }
  },
  findNode(source: string, selector: { cssSelector?: string }): AstRange | null {
    if (!selector.cssSelector) return null;
    const root = parse(source);
    const el = root.querySelector(selector.cssSelector);
    if (!el) return null;
    const start = el.range[0];
    const end = el.range[1];
    return { start, end };
  }
};

import { jsonParser } from './json.js';

import { htmlParser } from './html.js';

import type { TredParser } from './types.js';

const parsers: TredParser[] = [jsonParser, markdownParser, htmlParser, cssParser];

export function getParser(file: string): TredParser | null {
  const ext = '.' + file.split('.').pop();
  return parsers.find(p => p.extensions.includes(ext)) || null;
}

export * from './types.js';


================================================================================
FILE: src/parsers/json.ts
================================================================================
import type { TredParser, AstRange } from './types.js';

export const jsonParser: TredParser = {
  extensions: ['.json'],
  checkSyntax(source: string): string[] {
    try {
      JSON.parse(source);
      return [];
    } catch (e) {
      return [e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)];
    }
  },
  findNode(source: string, selector: { path?: string }): AstRange | null {
    if (!selector.path) return null;
    const keys = selector.path.split('.');
    const lastKey = keys[keys.length - 1];
    // 利用正则寻找最内层的 key: value
    const re = new RegExp('"' + lastKey + '"\\s*:\\s*(\\{[^}]*\\}|\\[[^\\]]*\\]|"[^"]*"|true|false|null|\\d+)', 'm');
    const match = source.match(re);
    if (match && match.index !== undefined) {
      const keyPrefixLen = match[0].indexOf(match[1]);
      return {
        start: match.index + keyPrefixLen,
        end: match.index + match[0].length
      };
    }
    return null;
  }
};


================================================================================
FILE: src/parsers/markdown.ts
================================================================================
import type { TredParser, AstRange } from './types.js';

export const markdownParser: TredParser = {
  extensions: ['.md', '.markdown'],
  checkSyntax(source: string): string[] {
    return []; // Markdown 容错率极高，暂不严格拦截
  },
  findNode(source: string, selector: { heading?: string, codeLang?: string }): AstRange | null {
    const lines = source.split('\n');
    let pos = 0;
    
    if (selector.heading) {
      const target = selector.heading;
      for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        if (lines[i].trim() === target || lines[i].startsWith('#' + target) || lines[i].startsWith('##' + target)) {
          const start = pos;
          const end = pos + lines[i].length;
          return { start, end };
        }
        pos += lines[i].length + 1;
      }
    }
    
    if (selector.codeLang) {
      const startTag = '```' + selector.codeLang;
      for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
        if (lines[i].startsWith(startTag)) {
          const start = pos + lines[i].length + 1;
          let j = i + 1;
          let end = start;
          while (j < lines.length && !lines[j].startsWith('```')) {
            end += lines[j].length + 1;
            j++;
          }
          return { start, end: end - 1 };
        }
        pos += lines[i].length + 1;
      }
    }
    return null;
  }
};

export interface AstRange { start: number; end: number; }

export interface TredParser {
  extensions: string[];
  findNode(source: string, selector: any): AstRange | null;
  /** v2.1.0: 每种语言负责自己的语法校验 */
  checkSyntax(source: string): string[];
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/patch.ts
================================================================================
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { checkSyntax } from './safety.js';
import { getSimilarity } from './fuzzy.js';

export interface PatchResult {
  ok: boolean;
  message?: string;
  suggestion?: {
    line: number;
    score: number;
    context: string[];
    reason: string;
  };
}

export function extractTokens(line: string): string[] {
  if (!line) return [];
  const normalized = line.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g, '$1 $2').replace(/[-_]/g, ' ');
  const tokens = normalized.match(/[a-zA-Z0-9]{4,}|[\u4e00-\u9fa5]{2,}/g) || [];
  return tokens.map(t => t.toLowerCase());
}

function genHtmlReport(file: string, line: number, current: string[], target: string[], score: number): string {
  const esc = (s: string) => s.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;').replace(/>/g, '&gt;');

  const currentHtml = current.map((l, i) => {
    const num = line - 2 + i;
    return '<div class="line"><span class="num">' + num + '</span>' + esc(l) + '</div>';
  }).join('');

  const targetHtml = target.map((l, i) => {
    const num = line - 2 + i;
    return '<div class="line"><span class="num">' + num + '</span>' + esc(l) + '</div>';
  }).join('');

  return [
    '<!DOCTYPE html>',
    '<html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>tred suggest</title>',
    '<style>',
    'body{font-family:system-ui,sans-serif;margin:0;background:#f6f8fa;height:100vh;display:flex;flex-direction:column;}',
    '.header{padding:1em 2em;background:#fff;border-bottom:1px solid #d0d7de;box-shadow:0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,.05);}',
    '.header h1{margin:0;font-size:1.2em;color:#24292f;}',
    '.header p{margin:.5em 0 0;color:#57606a;font-size:.9em;}',
    '.split{display:flex;flex:1;overflow:hidden;}',
    '.pane{flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:1em;position:relative;}',
    '.pane.left{background:#fff;border-right:1px solid #d0d7de;}',
    '.pane.right{background:#f0fdf4;}',
    '.line{font-family:ui-monospace,monospace;font-size:0.9em;line-height:1.6;white-space:pre;}',
    '.num{display:inline-block;width:3em;color:#8c959f;text-align:right;margin-right:1em;user-select:none;}',
    '.tag{position:absolute;top:1em;right:2em;font-size:0.8em;color:#fff;padding:0.2em 0.6em;border-radius:1em;}',
    '.left .tag{background:#0969da;}',
    '.right .tag{background:#2da44e;}',
    '</style>',
    '</head><body>',
    '<div class="header"><h1>💡 tred suggest: ' + esc(file) + '</h1>',
    '<p>发现第 ' + line + ' 行有 ' + (score * 100).toFixed(1) + '% 的相似度。左侧为当前代码，右侧为想要应用的补丁目标。</p></div>',
    '<div class="split">',
    '  <div class="pane left"><div class="tag">CURRENT</div>' + currentHtml + '</div>',
    '  <div class="pane right"><div class="tag">TARGET</div>' + targetHtml + '</div>',
    '</div></body></html>'
  ].join('\n');
}

function healNewBlock(oldBlock: string[], newBlock: string[], actualBlock: string[]): string[] | null {
  if (oldBlock.length !== actualBlock.length) return null;
  const healed = [...newBlock];
  for (let i = 0; i < oldBlock.length; i++) {
    if (oldBlock[i] !== actualBlock[i]) {
      const idx = healed.indexOf(oldBlock[i]);
      if (idx !== -1) healed[idx] = actualBlock[i];
    }
  }
  return healed;
}

export function applyPatch(
  filePath: string,
  patchText: string,
  options: { fuzzy?: boolean, fuzzyThreshold?: number, minRareTokens?: number, autoHeal?: boolean, html?: boolean } = {},
): PatchResult {
  const original = readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8').replace(/\r\n/g, '\n');
  const lines = original.split('\n');
  const patchLines = patchText.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').split('\n');

  const hunks: { oldStart: number, oldBlock: string[], newBlock: string[] }[] = [];
  let currentHunk: { oldStart: number, oldBlock: string[], newBlock: string[] } | null = null;

  for (const l of patchLines) {
    if (l.startsWith('---') || l.startsWith('+++') || l.startsWith('diff') || l.startsWith('index') || l.startsWith('\\')) continue;
    const hunkMatch = l.match(/^@@ -(\d+)(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)(?:,\d+)? @@/);
    if (hunkMatch) {
      if (currentHunk) hunks.push(currentHunk);
      currentHunk = { oldStart: parseInt(hunkMatch[1], 10), oldBlock: [], newBlock: [] };
      continue;
    }
    if (!currentHunk) continue;
    const firstChar = l.charAt(0);
    if (firstChar === ' ') {
      const text = l.slice(1);
      currentHunk.oldBlock.push(text);
      currentHunk.newBlock.push(text);
    } else if (firstChar === '-') {
      currentHunk.oldBlock.push(l.slice(1));
    } else if (firstChar === '+') {
      currentHunk.newBlock.push(l.slice(1));
    } else if (l === '') {
      currentHunk.oldBlock.push('');
      currentHunk.newBlock.push('');
    }
  }
  if (currentHunk) hunks.push(currentHunk);
  if (hunks.length === 0) return { ok: false, message: '未找到有效的 Hunk (@@ ... @@)' };

  const globalFreq = new Map<string, number>();
  for (const line of lines) {
    for (const t of extractTokens(line)) globalFreq.set(t, (globalFreq.get(t) || 0) + 1);
  }

  function tokenWeight(token: string): number {
    return 1 / Math.log2((globalFreq.get(token) || 1) + 1);
  }

  function weightedTokenSet(line: string): Map<string, number> {
    const map = new Map<string, number>();
    for (const t of extractTokens(line)) if (!map.has(t)) map.set(t, tokenWeight(t));
    return map;
  }

  function weightedLineSimilarity(a: string, b: string): number {
    if (a === b) return 1;
    if (a.trim() === b.trim()) return 0.95;
    if (!a.trim() || !b.trim()) return 0;
    const charSim = getSimilarity(a, b);
    const ta = weightedTokenSet(a);
    const tb = weightedTokenSet(b);
    if (ta.size === 0 || tb.size === 0) return charSim;
    let intersection = 0, union = 0;
    for (const [t, w] of ta) { union += w; if (tb.has(t)) intersection += w; }
    for (const [t, w] of tb) if (!ta.has(t)) union += w;
    return charSim * 0.4 + (union > 0 ? intersection / union : 0) * 0.6;
  }

  const fuzzyEnabled = Boolean(options.fuzzy);
  const fuzzyThreshold = options.fuzzyThreshold ?? 0.9;
  const minRareTokens = options.minRareTokens ?? 2;
  const autoHeal = Boolean(options.autoHeal);
  const html = Boolean(options.html);

  const sortedHunks = [...hunks].sort((a, b) => b.oldStart - a.oldStart);
  let currentLines = [...lines];

  for (const hunk of sortedHunks) {
    const { oldStart, oldBlock } = hunk;
    let { newBlock } = hunk;
    if (oldBlock.length === 0) return { ok: false, message: 'Hunk 缺少上下文，无法定位' };

    const rareTokens = new Set<string>();
    for (const line of oldBlock) {
      for (const t of extractTokens(line)) if ((globalFreq.get(t) || 1) <= 6) rareTokens.add(t);
    }

    const approxIdx = Math.max(0, oldStart - 1);
    let foundIdx = -1;

    for (let i = 0; i <= currentLines.length - oldBlock.length; i++) {
      let match = true;
      for (let j = 0; j < oldBlock.length; j++) {
        if (currentLines[i + j] !== oldBlock[j]) { match = false; break; }
      }
      if (match) { foundIdx = i; break; }
    }

    if (foundIdx === -1) {
      const startSearch = Math.max(0, approxIdx - 50);
      const endSearch = Math.min(currentLines.length - oldBlock.length, approxIdx + 50);
      for (let i = startSearch; i <= endSearch; i++) {
        let match = true;
        for (let j = 0; j < oldBlock.length; j++) {
          if (currentLines[i + j] !== oldBlock[j]) { match = false; break; }
        }
        if (match) { foundIdx = i; break; }
      }
    }

    if (foundIdx === -1) {
      const startSearch = Math.max(0, approxIdx - 50);
      const endSearch = Math.min(currentLines.length - oldBlock.length, approxIdx + 50);
      for (let i = startSearch; i <= endSearch; i++) {
        let match = true;
        for (let j = 0; j < oldBlock.length; j++) {
          if (currentLines[i + j].trim() !== oldBlock[j].trim()) { match = false; break; }
        }
        if (match) { foundIdx = i; break; }
      }
    }

    if (foundIdx === -1 && fuzzyEnabled) {
      if (rareTokens.size < minRareTokens) {
        return { ok: false, message: 'Hunk @@ -' + oldStart + ' 只有 ' + rareTokens.size + ' 个稀有标识符，模糊匹配已拒绝。' };
      }
      const isOutOfBounds = approxIdx + 100 > currentLines.length;
      const startSearch = isOutOfBounds ? 0 : Math.max(0, approxIdx - 100);
      const endSearch = isOutOfBounds ? currentLines.length - oldBlock.length : Math.min(currentLines.length - oldBlock.length, approxIdx + 100);
      let best: { score: number, rareHits: number, idx: number } | null = null;

      for (let i = startSearch; i <= endSearch; i++) {
        const windowTokenSet = new Set<string>();
        for (let j = 0; j < oldBlock.length; j++) {
          const w = currentLines[i + j];
          if (w !== undefined) for (const t of extractTokens(w)) windowTokenSet.add(t);
        }
        let rareHits = 0;
        for (const t of rareTokens) if (windowTokenSet.has(t)) rareHits++;
        if (rareHits < minRareTokens) continue;

        let totalScore = 0, totalWeight = 0;
        for (let j = 0; j < oldBlock.length; j++) {
          const sim = weightedLineSimilarity(currentLines[i + j] || '', oldBlock[j]);
          let lineHasRare = false;
          for (const t of extractTokens(oldBlock[j])) if (rareTokens.has(t)) { lineHasRare = true; break; }
          totalScore += sim * (lineHasRare ? 2 : 1);
          totalWeight += (lineHasRare ? 2 : 1);
        }
        const finalScore = totalWeight > 0 ? totalScore / totalWeight : 0;
        if (!best || finalScore > best.score) best = { score: finalScore, rareHits, idx: i };
      }

      if (best) {
        if (autoHeal && best.score >= fuzzyThreshold - 0.15) {
          const actualBlock = currentLines.slice(best.idx, best.idx + oldBlock.length);
          const healedNew = healNewBlock(oldBlock, newBlock, actualBlock);
          if (healedNew) {
            console.log('  [tred patch] 🔧 Auto-Heal 成功，上下文已修正为当前文件内容 (原相似度: ' + (best.score * 100).toFixed(1) + '%)');
            newBlock = healedNew;
            foundIdx = best.idx;
          }
        }

        if (foundIdx === -1 && best.score >= fuzzyThreshold) {
          foundIdx = best.idx;
          console.log('  [tred patch] 模糊命中 (相似度 ' + (best.score * 100).toFixed(1) + '%, 行 ' + (foundIdx + 1) + ')');
        } else if (foundIdx === -1) {
          const ctxStart = Math.max(0, best.idx - 2);
          const ctxEnd = Math.min(currentLines.length, best.idx + oldBlock.length + 2);
          const currentCtx = currentLines.slice(ctxStart, ctxEnd);
          if (html) {
            const targetCtx = newBlock;
            const htmlStr = genHtmlReport(filePath, best.idx + 1, currentCtx, targetCtx, best.score);
            writeFileSync('tred-suggest.html', htmlStr);
            console.log('  [tred suggest] 可视化报告已生成: tred-suggest.html');
          }
          return {
            ok: false,
            message: '模糊匹配得分 ' + (best.score * 100).toFixed(1) + '% 未达阈值 ' + (fuzzyThreshold * 100).toFixed(0) + '%',
            suggestion: { line: best.idx + 1, score: best.score, context: currentCtx, reason: '是不是想改这里？' }
          };
        }
      }
    }

    if (foundIdx === -1) {
      return { ok: false, message: 'Hunk @@ -' + oldStart + ' 未找到匹配位置' + (fuzzyEnabled ? '（已尝试模糊匹配）' : '（可加 --fuzzy 尝试）') };
    }
    currentLines.splice(foundIdx, oldBlock.length, ...newBlock);
  }

  const newText = currentLines.join('\n');
  if (/\.[jt]sx?$/.test(filePath)) {
    const errors = checkSyntax(filePath, newText);
    if (errors.length > 0) return { ok: false, message: '应用 Patch 后语法报错：' + errors.join('; ') };
  }
  writeFileSync(filePath, newText, 'utf-8');
  return { ok: true };
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/render-github.ts
================================================================================
import type { VerifyIssue, VerifyReport, FixReport } from './types.js';

function escProp(s: string): string {
  return s
    .replace(/%/g, '%25')
    .replace(/\r/g, '%0D')
    .replace(/\n/g, '%0A')
    .replace(/:/g, '%3A')
    .replace(/,/g, '%2C');
}

function escMsg(s: string): string {
  return s
    .replace(/%/g, '%25')
    .replace(/\r/g, '%0D')
    .replace(/\n/g, '%0A');
}

function issueToAnnotation(issue: VerifyIssue): string {
  const level = issue.level === 'error' ? 'error' : 'warning';
  const props: string[] = [];
  if (issue.file) props.push('file=' + escProp(issue.file));
  if (issue.line) props.push('line=' + issue.line);
  if (issue.code) props.push('title=' + escProp(issue.code));
  return '::' + level + ' ' + props.join(',') + '::' + escMsg(issue.message);
}

export function renderGithubAnnotations(report: VerifyReport): string {
  if (report.issues.length === 0) {
    return '::notice title=tred::verify 通过（' + report.scannedFiles + ' 个文件）\n';
  }
  const lines: string[] = [];
  for (const issue of report.issues) {
    lines.push(issueToAnnotation(issue));
  }
  return lines.join('\n') + '\n';
}

export function renderFixGithubAnnotations(report: FixReport): string {
  const lines: string[] = [];
  for (const f of report.files) {
    if (f.error) {
      lines.push('::error file=' + escProp(f.file) + ',title=tred-fix::' + escMsg(f.error));
    } else if (f.applied) {
      lines.push('::notice file=' + escProp(f.file) + ',title=tred-fix::' + f.actions.length + ' 处已修复');
    }
  }
  if (lines.length === 0) {
    lines.push('::notice title=tred-fix::无需修复');
  }
  return lines.join('\n') + '\n';
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/render-html.ts

import type { VerifyIssue, VerifyReport, VerifyFileReport } from './types.js';

export interface HtmlOptions {
  version?: string;
  title?: string;
}

const LF = String.fromCharCode(10);

function escapeHtml(s: string): string {
  return s
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&#39;");
}

const CSS = "body { font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, \"Segoe UI\", system-ui, sans-serif; margin: 0; padding: 2em; background: #f6f8fa; color: #24292f; }\nh1 { font-size: 1.8em; margin-bottom: 0.3em; }\n.meta { color: #57606a; margin-bottom: 1.5em; font-size: 0.9em; }\n.stats { display: flex; gap: 1em; margin-bottom: 2em; flex-wrap: wrap; }\n.card { padding: 1em 1.5em; background: white; border: 1px solid #d0d7de; border-radius: 6px; min-width: 120px; }\n.card .num { font-size: 1.6em; font-weight: 600; }\n.card .label { color: #57606a; font-size: 0.85em; }\n.card.error { border-left: 4px solid #cf222e; }\n.card.warning { border-left: 4px solid #bf8700; }\n.card.ok { border-left: 4px solid #2da44e; }\n.card.info { border-left: 4px solid #0969da; }\n.status { padding: 0.8em 1.2em; border-radius: 6px; margin-bottom: 2em; font-weight: 600; }\n.status.ok { background: #dafbe1; color: #1a7f37; }\n.status.fail { background: #ffebe9; color: #cf222e; }\ntable { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; background: white; border-radius: 6px; overflow: hidden; border: 1px solid #d0d7de; margin-bottom: 2em; }\nth, td { padding: 0.6em 1em; text-align: left; border-bottom: 1px solid #eaeef2; }\nth { background: #f6f8fa; font-weight: 600; font-size: 0.9em; }\ntr:last-child td { border-bottom: none; }\n.badge { display: inline-block; padding: 0.1em 0.5em; border-radius: 3px; font-size: 0.8em; font-weight: 600; }\n.badge.error { background: #ffebe9; color: #cf222e; }\n.badge.warning { background: #fff8c5; color: #7d4e00; }\ndetails { background: white; border: 1px solid #d0d7de; border-radius: 6px; padding: 1em 1.5em; margin-bottom: 1em; }\nsummary { cursor: pointer; font-weight: 600; }\nsummary:hover { color: #0969da; }\n.anchor-item { padding: 0.3em 0; font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, monospace; font-size: 0.9em; }\n.anchor-item.orphan { color: #cf222e; }\n.empty { color: #57606a; font-style: italic; }\n#filter { width: 100%; padding: 0.6em 1em; border: 1px solid #d0d7de; border-radius: 6px; margin-bottom: 1em; font-size: 1em; box-sizing: border-box; }\nfooter { margin-top: 3em; color: #57606a; font-size: 0.85em; text-align: center; }\ncode { background: #f6f8fa; padding: 0.1em 0.4em; border-radius: 3px; font-family: ui-monospace, monospace; font-size: 0.9em; }";

function badge(level: 'error' | 'warning'): string {
  return '<span class="badge ' + level + '">' + level + '</span>';
}

function statCard(num: number, label: string, kind: string): string {
  return '<div class="card ' + kind + '"><div class="num">' + num + '</div><div class="label">' + label + '</div></div>';
}

function renderIssueRow(issue: VerifyIssue): string {
  const loc = issue.file ? escapeHtml(issue.file) + (issue.line ? ':' + issue.line : '') : '—';
  return '<tr><td>' + badge(issue.level) + '</td><td><code>' + escapeHtml(issue.code) + '</code></td><td>' + loc + '</td><td>' + escapeHtml(issue.message) + '</td></tr>';
}

function renderFileDetails(fr: VerifyFileReport): string {
  const orphan = fr.orphans.length > 0;
  const icon = orphan ? '❌' : '✅';
  const parts: string[] = [];
  parts.push('<details>');
  parts.push('<summary>' + icon + ' ' + escapeHtml(fr.file) + ' (' + fr.anchors.length + ' anchors, ' + fr.orphans.length + ' orphans)</summary>');
  parts.push('<div style="margin-top: 1em">');
  if (fr.anchors.length > 0) {
    parts.push('<div style="margin-bottom: 0.5em"><strong>Anchors:</strong></div>');
    for (const a of fr.anchors) {
      const fp = a.fp ? ' fp=' + escapeHtml(a.fp) : '';
      parts.push('<div class="anchor-item">' + escapeHtml(a.name) + ' L' + a.line + '-L' + a.endLine + fp + '</div>');
    }
  }
  if (fr.orphans.length > 0) {
    parts.push('<div style="margin: 0.5em 0"><strong>Orphans:</strong></div>');
    for (const o of fr.orphans) {
      parts.push('<div class="anchor-item orphan">⚠ ' + o.kind.toUpperCase() + ' ' + escapeHtml(o.name) + ' L' + o.line + '</div>');
    }
  }
  parts.push('</div>');
  parts.push('</details>');
  return parts.join(LF);
}

export function renderHtml(report: VerifyReport, opts?: HtmlOptions): string {
  const version = (opts && opts.version) || '0.0.0';
  const title = (opts && opts.title) || 'tred verify report';
  const errors = report.issues.filter((i) => i.level === 'error').length;
  const warnings = report.issues.filter((i) => i.level === 'warning').length;
  const ts = new Date().toISOString();

  const parts: string[] = [];
  parts.push('<!DOCTYPE html>');
  parts.push('<html lang="en">');
  parts.push('<head>');
  parts.push('<meta charset="utf-8">');
  parts.push('<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">');
  parts.push('<title>' + escapeHtml(title) + '</title>');
  parts.push('<style>');
  parts.push(CSS);
  parts.push('</style>');
  parts.push('</head>');
  parts.push('<body>');

  parts.push('<h1>🔍 ' + escapeHtml(title) + '</h1>');
  parts.push('<div class="meta">');
  parts.push('Dir: <code>' + escapeHtml(report.dir) + '</code> &middot; ');
  parts.push('tred v' + escapeHtml(version) + ' &middot; ');
  parts.push(ts);
  parts.push('</div>');

  parts.push('<div class="stats">');
  parts.push(statCard(report.scannedFiles, 'Scanned Files', 'info'));
  parts.push(statCard(report.files.length, 'With Anchors', 'info'));
  parts.push(statCard(errors, 'Errors', errors > 0 ? 'error' : 'ok'));
  parts.push(statCard(warnings, 'Warnings', warnings > 0 ? 'warning' : 'ok'));
  parts.push('</div>');

  if (report.ok) {
    parts.push('<div class="status ok">✅ All checks passed</div>');
  } else {
    parts.push('<div class="status fail">❌ ' + errors + ' error(s) found</div>');
  }

  if (report.issues.length > 0) {
    parts.push('<h2>Issues (' + report.issues.length + ')</h2>');
    parts.push('<input id="filter" type="text" placeholder="Filter by code / file / message...">');
    parts.push('<table id="issues-table">');
    parts.push('<thead><tr><th>Level</th><th>Code</th><th>Location</th><th>Message</th></tr></thead>');
    parts.push('<tbody>');
    for (const issue of report.issues) {
      parts.push(renderIssueRow(issue));
    }
    parts.push('</tbody>');
    parts.push('</table>');
  }

  if (report.files.length > 0) {
    parts.push('<h2>Files (' + report.files.length + ')</h2>');
    for (const fr of report.files) {
      parts.push(renderFileDetails(fr));
    }
  } else {
    parts.push('<div class="empty">No anchors found in scanned files.</div>');
  }

  parts.push('<footer>Generated by <strong>tred</strong> v' + escapeHtml(version) + '</footer>');

  parts.push('<script>');
  parts.push('(function() {');
  parts.push('  var f = document.getElementById("filter");');
  parts.push('  if (!f) return;');
  parts.push('  var table = document.getElementById("issues-table");');
  parts.push('  if (!table) return;');
  parts.push('  f.addEventListener("input", function() {');
  parts.push('    var q = f.value.toLowerCase();');
  parts.push('    var rows = table.querySelectorAll("tbody tr");');
  parts.push('    for (var i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {');
  parts.push('      var txt = rows[i].textContent.toLowerCase();');
  parts.push('      rows[i].style.display = (q === "" || txt.indexOf(q) >= 0) ? "" : "none";');
  parts.push('    }');
  parts.push('  });');
  parts.push('})();');
  parts.push('</script>');

  parts.push('</body>');
  parts.push('</html>');

  return parts.join(LF) + LF;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/render-markdown.ts
================================================================================
import type { FixReport, VerifyReport } from './types.js';

function cell(s: string): string {
  return s.replace(/\|/g, '\\|').replace(/\r?\n/g, ' ');
}

function codeInline(s: string): string {
  if (s.includes('`')) return '`` ' + s + ' ``';
  return '`' + s + '`';
}

function statusEmoji(level: 'error' | 'warning'): string {
  return level === 'error' ? '🔴' : '🟡';
}

export function renderVerifyMarkdown(r: VerifyReport): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('<!-- tred:verify -->');
  out.push('## 🔍 tred verify');
  out.push('');
  out.push('- **目录**: ' + codeInline(r.dir));
  out.push('- **扫描**: ' + r.scannedFiles + ' 个文件，' + r.files.length + ' 个含锚点');
  if (r.cacheStats && r.cacheStats.enabled) {
    out.push('- **缓存**: ' + r.cacheStats.hits + ' hits / ' + r.cacheStats.misses + ' misses / ' + r.cacheStats.removed + ' removed');
  }

  const errors = r.issues.filter(function (i) { return i.level === 'error'; }).length;
  const warnings = r.issues.filter(function (i) { return i.level === 'warning'; }).length;

  out.push('');
  if (r.issues.length === 0) {
    out.push('### ✅ 无问题');
  } else if (r.ok) {
    out.push('### ⚠️ 通过（' + warnings + ' 个 warning）');
  } else {
    out.push('### ❌ 未通过（' + errors + ' 个 error，' + warnings + ' 个 warning）');
  }

  if (r.issues.length > 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('#### 问题汇总');
    out.push('');
    out.push('| 级别 | 代码 | 位置 | 描述 |');
    out.push('|---|---|---|---|');
    for (const i of r.issues) {
      const loc = i.file
        ? codeInline(i.file) + (i.line ? ':' + i.line : '')
        : '—';
      out.push('| ' + statusEmoji(i.level) + ' ' + i.level + ' | ' + codeInline(i.code) + ' | ' + loc + ' | ' + cell(i.message) + ' |');
    }
  }

  if (r.files.length > 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('#### 文件明细');
    out.push('');
    for (const fr of r.files) {
      const orphan = fr.orphans.length > 0;
      const icon = orphan ? '❌' : '✅';
      out.push('<details><summary>' + icon + ' ' + codeInline(fr.file) + '</summary>');
      out.push('');
      if (fr.anchors.length > 0) {
        out.push('**健康锚点**');
        out.push('');
        for (const a of fr.anchors) {
          const fp = a.fp ? '`fp=' + a.fp + '`' : '_no fp_';
          out.push('- ' + codeInline(a.name) + ' L' + a.line + '-L' + a.endLine + ' ' + fp);
        }
      }
      if (fr.orphans.length > 0) {
        out.push('');
        out.push('**孤儿**');
        out.push('');
        for (const o of fr.orphans) {
          out.push('- ⚠ ' + o.kind.toUpperCase() + ' ' + codeInline(o.name) + ' L' + o.line);
        }
      }
      out.push('');
      out.push('</details>');
    }
  }

  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}

export function renderFixMarkdown(r: FixReport): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('<!-- tred:verify-fix -->');
  out.push('## 🛠 tred verify --fix' + (r.dryRun ? ' (dry-run)' : ' (--apply)'));
  out.push('');
  out.push('- **目录**: ' + codeInline(r.dir));
  out.push('- **扫描**: ' + r.scannedFiles + ' 个文件');
  out.push('- **模式**: ' + (r.dryRun ? 'dry-run（不写盘）' : 'apply（已写盘）'));

  if (r.totalActions === 0 && r.remainingOrphanStarts.length === 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('### ✅ 无需修复');
    return out.join('\n') + '\n';
  }

  if (r.totalActions > 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push(r.dryRun ? '### 待修复 (' + r.totalActions + ' 处)' : '### 修复结果 (' + r.totalActions + ' 处)');
    out.push('');
    for (const f of r.files) {
      const icon = f.error ? '❌' : (f.applied ? '✅' : '·');
      out.push('#### ' + icon + ' ' + codeInline(f.file));
      out.push('');
      for (const a of f.actions) {
        out.push('- L' + a.line + ' — ' + cell(a.description));
      }
      if (f.error) {
        out.push('');
        out.push('> ❌ **错误**: ' + cell(f.error));
      }
      out.push('');
    }
  }

  if (r.remainingOrphanStarts.length > 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('### ⚠ 需手动处理 (' + r.remainingOrphanStarts.length + ' 处孤儿 START)');
    out.push('');
    out.push('以下锚点有 START 但无 END，**未自动修复**（保守策略）。');
    out.push('建议用 `tred replace --auto-inject --selector ...` 重写，或手动补 `@TRED_END`。');
    out.push('');
    for (const o of r.remainingOrphanStarts) {
      out.push('- ' + codeInline(o.file) + ':' + o.line + ' — ' + codeInline(o.anchorName));
    }
  }

  if (r.dryRun && r.files.length > 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('<details><summary>查看 diff（' + r.files.length + ' 个文件）</summary>');
    out.push('');
    for (const f of r.files) {
      out.push('**' + codeInline(f.file) + '**');
      out.push('');
      out.push('````diff');
      out.push(f.diff.trimEnd());
      out.push('````');
      out.push('');
    }
    out.push('</details>');
    out.push('');
    out.push('> 💡 加 `--apply` 实际写盘');
  }

  out.push('');
  out.push(r.ok ? (r.dryRun ? '✅ dry-run 完成' : '✅ 修复完成') : '❌ 有文件修复失败');
  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}

// v0.17: diff-dir / annotate-dir markdown
function bq(s: string): string { return String.fromCharCode(96) + s + String.fromCharCode(96); }

export function renderDiffDirMarkdown(r: {
  ok: boolean;
  leftDir: string;
  rightDir: string;
  files: { file: string; leftExists: boolean; rightExists: boolean; anchors: { name: string; leftFound: boolean; rightFound: boolean; diff: string; message?: string }[] }[];
  stats: { compared: number; unchanged: number; changed: number; missing: number };
}): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('<!-- tred:diff-dir -->');
  out.push('## 🔀 tred diff --dir');
  out.push('');
  out.push('- **左侧**: ' + bq(r.leftDir));
  out.push('- **右侧**: ' + bq(r.rightDir));
  out.push('- **统计**: 比对 ' + r.stats.compared + ' / 未变 ' + r.stats.unchanged + ' / 变化 ' + r.stats.changed + ' / 缺失 ' + r.stats.missing);
  out.push('');
  out.push(r.ok ? '### ✅ 无差异' : '### ❌ 有差异');
  for (const f of r.files) {
    if (f.anchors.length === 0) continue;
    out.push('');
    out.push('### ' + bq(f.file));
    for (const a of f.anchors) {
      if (a.message) {
        out.push('- ⚠ ' + bq(a.name) + ' — ' + a.message);
      } else if (!a.diff) {
        out.push('- ✓ ' + bq(a.name) + ' — 无变化');
      } else {
        out.push('- ≠ ' + bq(a.name));
        out.push('');
        out.push('````diff');
        out.push(a.diff.trimEnd());
        out.push('````');
      }
    }
  }
  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}

export function renderAnnotateDirMarkdown(r: {
  ok: boolean;
  dir: string;
  apply: boolean;
  files: { file: string; ok: boolean; actions: { name: string; kind: string; nodeKind: string; line: number }[]; applied: boolean; syntaxErrors?: string[] }[];
  totalActions: number;
  annotatedFiles: number;
  failedFiles: number;
}): string {
  const out: string[] = [];
  out.push('<!-- tred:annotate-dir -->');
  out.push('## 🏷 tred annotate' + (r.apply ? ' (--apply)' : ' (dry-run)'));
  out.push('');
  out.push('- **目录**: ' + bq(r.dir));
  out.push('- **涉及文件**: ' + r.annotatedFiles);
  out.push('- **新增锚点**: ' + r.totalActions);
  if (r.failedFiles > 0) out.push('- **失败文件**: ' + r.failedFiles);
  if (r.files.length === 0) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('### ✅ 无需新增');
    return out.join('\n') + '\n';
  }
  out.push('');
  out.push('### ' + (r.ok ? '✅ 全部成功' : '❌ 有失败'));
  for (const f of r.files) {
    const icon = f.ok ? (f.applied ? '✅' : '·') : '❌';
    out.push('');
    out.push('<details><summary>' + icon + ' ' + bq(f.file) + ' (' + f.actions.length + ' 条)</summary>');
    out.push('');
    for (const a of f.actions) {
      out.push('- L' + a.line + ' ' + a.kind.toUpperCase() + ' ' + bq(a.name) + ' (' + a.nodeKind + ')');
    }
    if (!f.ok && f.syntaxErrors) {
      out.push('');
      for (const e of f.syntaxErrors) out.push('> ❌ ' + e);
    }
    out.push('');
    out.push('</details>');
  }
  if (!r.apply) {
    out.push('');
    out.push('> 💡 加 --apply 写盘');
  }
  return out.join('\n') + '\n';
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/render-sarif.ts
================================================================================
import type { VerifyIssue, VerifyReport } from './types.js';

export interface SarifOptions {
  version?: string;
  informationUri?: string;
}

function ruleIdOf(code: string): string {
  return 'TRED_' + code;
}

interface RuleMeta {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  shortDescription: string;
  fullDescription: string;
  defaultLevel: 'error' | 'warning';
}

const RULES: RuleMeta[] = [
  {
    id: ruleIdOf('ORPHAN_START'),
    name: 'OrphanStart',
    shortDescription: 'Anchor START without matching END',
    fullDescription: 'A @TRED_START comment has no matching @TRED_END within the same file.',
    defaultLevel: 'error',
  },
  {
    id: ruleIdOf('ORPHAN_END'),
    name: 'OrphanEnd',
    shortDescription: 'Anchor END without matching START',
    fullDescription: 'A @TRED_END comment has no matching @TRED_START within the same file.',
    defaultLevel: 'error',
  },
  {
    id: ruleIdOf('DUPLICATE_WITHIN'),
    name: 'DuplicateAnchorWithinFile',
    shortDescription: 'Duplicate anchor name within a file',
    fullDescription: 'The same anchor name appears more than once in a single file.',
    defaultLevel: 'error',
  },
  {
    id: ruleIdOf('DUPLICATE_ACROSS'),
    name: 'DuplicateAnchorAcrossFiles',
    shortDescription: 'Duplicate anchor name across files',
    fullDescription: 'The same anchor name appears in more than one file. This may cause unintended matches.',
    defaultLevel: 'warning',
  },
  {
    id: ruleIdOf('FP_MISMATCH'),
    name: 'FingerprintMismatch',
    shortDescription: 'Fingerprint mismatch for same anchor',
    fullDescription: 'The same anchor name has different fingerprints across files, indicating out-of-sync versions.',
    defaultLevel: 'warning',
  },
];

function toLevel(l: 'error' | 'warning'): 'error' | 'warning' {
  return l;
}

export function renderSarif(report: VerifyReport, opts?: SarifOptions): string {
  const version = (opts && opts.version) || '0.0.0';
  const uri = (opts && opts.informationUri) || 'https://github.com/chinartcn/tred';

  // 按 code 聚合 issues，保证每个 ruleId 只出现一次
  const rulesUsed = new Set<string>();
  for (const issue of report.issues) rulesUsed.add(issue.code);

  const rules = RULES.filter((r) => rulesUsed.has(r.id.replace(/^TRED_/, ''))).map((r) => ({
    id: r.id,
    name: r.name,
    shortDescription: { text: r.shortDescription },
    fullDescription: { text: r.fullDescription },
    defaultConfiguration: { level: r.defaultLevel },
    helpUri: uri + '#rules',
  }));

  const results = report.issues.map((issue) => {
    const loc: Record<string, unknown> = {
      physicalLocation: {
        artifactLocation: {
          uri: issue.file || '',
          uriBaseId: '%SRCROOT%',
        },
      },
    };
    if (issue.line) {
      const pl = loc.physicalLocation as Record<string, unknown>;
      pl.region = { startLine: issue.line, startColumn: 1 };
    }
    return {
      ruleId: ruleIdOf(issue.code),
      level: toLevel(issue.level),
      message: { text: issue.message },
      locations: [loc],
    };
  });

  const sarif = {
    $schema: 'https://json.schemastore.org/sarif-2.1.0.json',
    version: '2.1.0',
    runs: [
      {
        tool: {
          driver: {
            name: 'tred',
            version: version,
            informationUri: uri,
            rules: rules,
          },
        },
        originalUriBaseIds: {
          '%SRCROOT%': {
            uri: report.dir ? report.dir + '/' : './',
          },
        },
        results: results,
      },
    ],
  };

  return JSON.stringify(sarif, null, 2) + '\n';
}

import ts from 'typescript';
import type { ReplaceAssertions } from './types.js';

export function checkSyntax(fileName: string, source: string): string[] {
  const sf = ts.createSourceFile(fileName, source, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
  const diags = (sf as unknown as { parseDiagnostics?: ts.Diagnostic[] }).parseDiagnostics ?? [];
  return diags.map((d) => {
    const msg = ts.flattenDiagnosticMessageText(d.messageText, '\n');
    if (d.file && typeof d.start === 'number') {
      const { line, character } = d.file.getLineAndCharacterOfPosition(d.start);
      return `${d.file.fileName}:${line + 1}:${character + 1} - TS${d.code}: ${msg}`;
    }
    return `TS${d.code}: ${msg}`;
  });
}

export function checkAssertions(content: string, assertions: ReplaceAssertions): string[] {
  const errors: string[] = [];

  if (assertions.include) {
    for (const word of assertions.include) {
      if (!content.includes(word)) errors.push('断言失败：必须包含 "' + word + '"');
    }
  }
  if (assertions.exclude) {
    for (const word of assertions.exclude) {
      if (content.includes(word)) errors.push('断言失败：不能包含 "' + word + '"');
    }
  }
  if (assertions.regex) {
    for (const pattern of assertions.regex) {
      try {
        if (!new RegExp(pattern).test(content)) errors.push('断言失败：必须匹配 /' + pattern + '/');
      } catch {
        errors.push('断言失败：非法正则 "' + pattern + '"');
      }
    }
  }

  if (assertions.hasAst || assertions.noAst) {
    const findAll = (kind: string, name?: string) => {
      let count = 0;
      const checkNode = (node: ts.Node) => {
        if (kind === 'class' && ts.isClassDeclaration(node) && (!name || node.name?.text === name)) count++;
        else if (kind === 'function' && ts.isFunctionDeclaration(node) && (!name || node.name?.text === name)) count++;
        else if (kind === 'method' && ts.isMethodDeclaration(node) && (!name || (node.name && ts.isIdentifier(node.name) && node.name.text === name))) count++;
        ts.forEachChild(node, checkNode);
      };

      const tryParse = (code: string) => {
        const sf = ts.createSourceFile('check.ts', code, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);
        count = 0;
        checkNode(sf);
        return count;
      };

      if (tryParse(content) > 0) return count;

      // Fallback 1: 当作类内部片段
      const classWrap = 'class __Dummy { ' + content + ' }';
      if (tryParse(classWrap) > 0) return count;

      // Fallback 2: 当作函数内部片段
      const funcWrap = 'function __dummy() { ' + content + ' }';
      if (tryParse(funcWrap) > 0) return count;

      return 0;
    };

    if (assertions.hasAst) {
      for (const rule of assertions.hasAst) {
        const parts = rule.split(':');
        const kind = parts[0];
        const name = parts[1];
        if (findAll(kind, name) === 0) errors.push('AST 断言失败：必须包含 ' + kind + (name ? ' ' + name : ''));
      }
    }
    if (assertions.noAst) {
      for (const rule of assertions.noAst) {
        const parts = rule.split(':');
        const kind = parts[0];
        const name = parts[1];
        if (findAll(kind, name) > 0) errors.push('AST 断言失败：不能包含 ' + kind + (name ? ' ' + name : ''));
      }
    }
  }

  return errors;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/serve.ts

import { createServer } from 'node:http';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';

export interface ServeOptions {
  port: number;
}

export interface ServeHandle {
  url: string;
  port: number;
  close: () => void;
}

export function serveHtml(getHtml: () => string, opts: ServeOptions): Promise<ServeHandle> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const server = createServer((req, res) => {
      const url = req.url || '/';
      if (url === '/favicon.ico') {
        res.statusCode = 204;
        res.end();
        return;
      }
      try {
        const html = getHtml();
        res.statusCode = 200;
        res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8");
        res.setHeader("Cache-Control", "no-store, no-cache, must-revalidate");
        res.end(html);
      } catch (e) {
        res.statusCode = 500;
        res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8");
        res.end("tred serve error: " + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
      }
    });

    server.on('error', reject);
    server.listen(opts.port, () => {
      const addr = server.address();
      let actualPort = opts.port;
      if (addr && typeof addr === 'object') actualPort = addr.port;
      resolve({
        url: 'http://localhost:' + actualPort,
        port: actualPort,
        close: () => server.close(),
      });
    });
  });
}

export function tryOpenBrowser(url: string): boolean {
  const candidates: Array<{ cmd: string; args: string[] }> = [
    { cmd: "termux-open-url", args: [url] },
    { cmd: "termux-open", args: [url] },
    { cmd: "open", args: [url] },
    { cmd: "xdg-open", args: [url] },
    { cmd: "cmd", args: ["/c", "start", "", url] },
  ];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    try {
      const r = spawnSync(c.cmd, c.args, { stdio: "ignore", timeout: 2000 });
      if (r.status === 0) return true;
    } catch {
      // try next
    }
  }
  return false;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/snippet.ts
