import type { SnippetSelector } from './types.js';

export function findSnippetRange(
  source: string,
  sel: SnippetSelector,
): { start: number; end: number } | null {
  const lines = source.split('\n');
  const lineStarts = [];
  let pos = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    lineStarts.push(pos);
    pos += lines[i].length + 1;
  }

  let startLineIdx = -1;
  let endLineIdx = -1;

  // 1. 找起始行
  if (sel.startLine !== undefined) {
    startLineIdx = sel.startLine - 1;
  } else if (sel.startSnippet) {
    let count = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
      if (lines[i].includes(sel.startSnippet)) {
        count++;
        if (count === (sel.occurrence ?? 1)) {
          startLineIdx = i;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  if (startLineIdx < 0 || startLineIdx >= lines.length) return null;

  // 2. 找结束行（v2.5.0: cutToEnd 优先级最高）
  if (sel.cutToEnd) {
    endLineIdx = lines.length - 1;
  } else if (sel.endLine !== undefined) {
    endLineIdx = sel.endLine - 1;
  } else if (sel.endSnippet) {
    for (let i = startLineIdx; i < lines.length; i++) {
      if (lines[i].includes(sel.endSnippet)) {
        endLineIdx = i;
        break;
      }
    }
  } else {
    endLineIdx = startLineIdx;
  }

  if (endLineIdx < startLineIdx || endLineIdx >= lines.length) return null;

  const startPos = lineStarts[startLineIdx];
  const endPos = lineStarts[endLineIdx] + lines[endLineIdx].length;
  return { start: startPos, end: endPos };
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/split.ts
================================================================================
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { basename, resolve } from 'node:path';
import ts from 'typescript';
import { checkSyntax } from './safety.js';

function dedent(str: string): string {
  const lines = str.split('\n');
  const minIndent = lines
    .filter(l => l.trim().length > 0)
    .reduce((min, l) => Math.min(min, l.match(/^\s*/)![0].length), Infinity);
  return lines.map(l => l.slice(minIndent)).join('\n');
}

export function splitFile(filePath: string, targets: string[], newFileName: string, targetClass?: string) {
  const original = readFileSync(filePath, 'utf-8');
  const sf = ts.createSourceFile(filePath, original, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true);

  const extractedNodes: ts.Node[] = [];
  const removedRanges: { start: number, end: number }[] = [];
  const foundNames = new Set<string>();
  let classDecl: ts.ClassDeclaration | undefined;

  if (targetClass) {
    classDecl = sf.statements.find((s): s is ts.ClassDeclaration => ts.isClassDeclaration(s) && s.name?.text === targetClass);
    if (!classDecl) return { ok: false, message: '找不到目标类: ' + targetClass };

    for (const member of classDecl.members) {
      // 1. 标准方法: renderNode() {}
      if (ts.isMethodDeclaration(member) && ts.isIdentifier(member.name) && targets.includes(member.name.text)) {
        extractedNodes.push(member);
        removedRanges.push({ start: member.getFullStart(), end: member.getEnd() });
        foundNames.add(member.name.text);
      }
      // 2. 箭头函数属性: renderNode = () => {}
      else if (ts.isPropertyDeclaration(member) && ts.isIdentifier(member.name) && targets.includes(member.name.text)) {
        extractedNodes.push(member);
        removedRanges.push({ start: member.getStart(sf), end: member.getEnd() });
        foundNames.add(member.name.text);
      }
    }

    const missing = targets.filter(t => !foundNames.has(t));
    if (missing.length > 0) {
      const available = classDecl.members
        .filter(m => ts.isMethodDeclaration(m) || ts.isPropertyDeclaration(m))
        .map(m => (m.name && ts.isIdentifier(m.name) ? m.name.text : ''))
        .filter(Boolean).join(', ');
      return { ok: false, message: '在类 ' + targetClass + ' 中找不到: ' + missing.join(', ') + '。该类实际拥有的成员: ' + available };
    }
  } else {
    for (const stmt of sf.statements) {
      if (ts.isFunctionDeclaration(stmt) && stmt.name && targets.includes(stmt.name.text)) {
        extractedNodes.push(stmt);
        removedRanges.push({ start: stmt.getFullStart(), end: stmt.getEnd() });
        foundNames.add(stmt.name.text);
      }
    }
  }

  if (extractedNodes.length === 0) {
    return { ok: false, message: '未找到任何指定的节点' };
  }

  // ── 构建新文件 ──
  const newFileLines: string[] = [];
  if (targetClass) {
    newFileLines.push('import type { ' + targetClass + ' } from \'./' + basename(filePath).replace(/\.ts$/, '.js') + '\';');
    newFileLines.push('');
  }

  for (const node of extractedNodes) {
    let text = original.slice(node.getFullStart(), node.getEnd()).trim();
    if (targetClass) {
      if (ts.isMethodDeclaration(node)) {
        const name = (node.name as ts.Identifier).text;
        const params = node.parameters.map(p => p.getText(sf)).join(', ');
        const typeParams = node.typeParameters ? '<' + node.typeParameters.map(t => t.getText(sf)).join(', ') + '>' : '';
        const returnType = node.type ? ': ' + node.type.getText(sf) : '';
        let bodyStr = '{}';
        if (node.body && ts.isBlock(node.body)) {
          const inner = node.body.statements.map(st => st.getText(sf)).join('\n');
          const dedented = dedent(inner).trim();
          bodyStr = '{\n  ' + dedented.replace(/\n/g, '\n  ') + '\n}';
        }
        // 剥离原本方法声明部分的文本，用标准 function 替换
        const fullText = original.slice(node.getFullStart(), node.getEnd()).trim();
        const bodyStartIdx = fullText.lastIndexOf('{');
        const headStr = bodyStartIdx !== -1 ? fullText.slice(0, bodyStartIdx).trim() : '';
        // 如果 headStr 包含原来方法的声明，我们拆掉它
        const prefix = headStr.replace(/^(?:public|private|protected|static|async|\s)*/g, '').replace(name + '[^{]*$/', '').trim();
        // 重新构建，保留注释
        let comments = '';
        const commentMatch = fullText.match(/^(\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\/|\/\/[^\n]*\n)+/);
        if (commentMatch) comments = commentMatch[0];
        text = comments + 'export function ' + name + typeParams + '(this: ' + targetClass + (params ? ', ' + params : '') + ')' + returnType + ' ' + bodyStr;
      } else if (ts.isPropertyDeclaration(node)) {
        const name = (node.name as ts.Identifier).text;
        let comments = '';
        const fullText = original.slice(node.getFullStart(), node.getEnd()).trim();
        const commentMatch = fullText.match(/^(\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\/|\/\/[^\n]*\n)+/);
        if (commentMatch) comments = commentMatch[0];

        if (node.initializer && ts.isArrowFunction(node.initializer)) {
          const arrow = node.initializer;
          const params = arrow.parameters.map(p => p.getText(sf)).join(', ');
          const returnType = arrow.type ? ': ' + arrow.type.getText(sf) : '';
          let bodyStr = arrow.body.getText(sf);
          if (ts.isBlock(arrow.body)) {
            const inner = arrow.body.statements.map(st => st.getText(sf)).join('\n');
            const dedented = dedent(inner).trim();
            bodyStr = '{\n  ' + dedented.replace(/\n/g, '\n  ') + '\n}';
          }
          text = comments + 'export const ' + name + ' = function(this: ' + targetClass + (params ? ', ' + params : '') + ')' + returnType + ' ' + bodyStr + ';';
        } else {
          text = comments + 'export const ' + name + ' = ' + node.initializer?.getText(sf) + ';';
        }
      }
    } else {
      if (!text.startsWith('export ')) text = 'export ' + text;
    }
    newFileLines.push(text);
    newFileLines.push('');
  }

  const newContent = newFileLines.join('\n');
  const fullNewPath = resolve(filePath, '..', newFileName);

  const newSyntaxErrors = checkSyntax(fullNewPath, newContent);
  if (newSyntaxErrors.length > 0) {
    return { ok: false, message: '新文件语法错误: ' + newSyntaxErrors.join('; ') };
  }
  writeFileSync(fullNewPath, newContent, 'utf-8');

  // ── 处理原文件：移除代码，增加 import 与 Mixin ──
  removedRanges.sort((a, b) => b.start - a.start);
  let origText = original;
  for (const r of removedRanges) {
    origText = origText.slice(0, r.start) + origText.slice(r.end);
  }

  const newBaseName = basename(newFileName).replace(/\.ts$/, '.js');
  const imports = Array.from(foundNames);
  if (targetClass) {
    const importStmt = 'import { ' + imports.join(', ') + ' } from \'./' + newBaseName + '\';';
    const mixinStmt = '\nObject.assign(' + targetClass + '.prototype, { ' + imports.join(', ') + ' });\n';
    origText = importStmt + '\n' + origText + mixinStmt;
  } else {
    const hasExport = extractedNodes.some(n => {
      if (!ts.canHaveModifiers(n)) return false;
      const mods = ts.getModifiers(n);
      return mods ? mods.some(m => m.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.ExportKeyword) : false;
    });
    const importStmt = 'import { ' + imports.join(', ') + ' } from \'./' + newBaseName + '\';';
    const reExport = hasExport ? '\nexport { ' + imports.join(', ') + ' };\n' : '';
    origText = importStmt + '\n' + origText + reExport;
  }

  origText = origText.replace(/\n\s*\n\s*\n/g, '\n\n');

  const origSyntaxErrors = checkSyntax(filePath, origText);
  if (origSyntaxErrors.length > 0) {
    writeFileSync(fullNewPath, '', 'utf-8');
    return { ok: false, message: '原文件移除代码后语法错误: ' + origSyntaxErrors.join('; ') };
  }
  writeFileSync(filePath, origText, 'utf-8');
  return { ok: true, message: '已拆分 ' + extractedNodes.length + ' 个成员到 ' + newFileName };
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/state.ts
================================================================================
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join, resolve } from 'node:path';

export interface AnchorState {
  approxLine: number;
  astSignature: string;
  fingerprint: string;
  updatedAt: string;
}

export interface TredState {
  version: number;
  tracked: Record<string, Record<string, AnchorState>>;
}

export function getStatePath(): string {
  return join(process.cwd(), '.tred', 'state.json');
}

export function loadState(): TredState {
  const p = getStatePath();
  if (!existsSync(p)) {
    return { version: 1, tracked: {} };
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(readFileSync(p, 'utf-8'));
  } catch {
    return { version: 1, tracked: {} };
  }
}

export function saveState(state: TredState): void {
  const p = getStatePath();
  mkdirSync(dirname(p), { recursive: true });
  writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify(state, null, 2) + '\n', 'utf-8');
}

export function updateAnchorState(file: string, anchorName: string, approxLine: number, astSignature: string, fingerprint: string): void {
  const state = loadState();
  if (!state.tracked[file]) state.tracked[file] = {};
  state.tracked[file][anchorName] = {
    approxLine,
    astSignature,
    fingerprint,
    updatedAt: new Date().toISOString()
  };
  saveState(state);
}

export function getAnchorState(file: string, anchorName: string): AnchorState | null {
  const state = loadState();
  return state.tracked[file]?.[anchorName] || null;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/strip.ts
================================================================================
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

export function stripAnchors(source: string): string {
  const lines = source.split('\n');
  const kept = lines.filter(line => {
    return !/^\s*\/\/\s*@TRED_(START|END):/.test(line);
  });
  return kept.join('\n');
}

export function stripDir(dir: string, apply: boolean): number {
  let changedCount = 0;

  function walk(d: string): void {
    const entries = readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true });
    for (const entry of entries) {
      const fullPath = join(d, entry.name);
      if (entry.isDirectory()) {
        if (entry.name === 'node_modules' || entry.name === '.git' || entry.name === 'dist') continue;
        walk(fullPath);
      } else if (entry.isFile() && /\.(ts|tsx|js|jsx|mjs|cjs)$/.test(entry.name)) {
        const raw = readFileSync(fullPath, 'utf-8');
        const stripped = stripAnchors(raw);
        if (stripped !== raw) {
          changedCount++;
          if (apply) writeFileSync(fullPath, stripped, 'utf-8');
        }
      }
    }
  }

  walk(dir);
  return changedCount;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/types.ts
================================================================================
export interface AstSelector {
  kind: 'class' | 'function' | 'arrow-function' | 'method' | 'statement' | 'variable' | 'property';
  className?: string;
  functionName?: string;
  methodName?: string;
  variableName?: string;
  /** property 的路径，如 "TECHS.铁矿机.cost" */
  path?: string;
  /** 如果有多个匹配，指定第几个（1-based），默认 1 */
  occurrence?: number;
  /** v2.8.0: 开启向量模糊匹配 */
  fuzzy?: boolean;
  /** v3.1.0: 模糊匹配阈值，低于此分数直接报错（0~1） */
  fuzzyThreshold?: number;
  /** v3.1.0: 多级上下文，支持 ['class:Worker', 'method:onmessage'] */
  context?: string | string[];
}

export interface SnippetSelector {
  /** 按行范围 */
  startLine?: number;
  endLine?: number;
  /** 按文本片段（包含该片段的行开始/结束） */
  startSnippet?: string;
  endSnippet?: string;
  /** 有多处匹配时，取第几个，默认 1 */
  occurrence?: number;
  /** v2.5.0: 强制从 start 切到文件末尾（救急残留代码） */
  cutToEnd?: boolean;
}

export type Formatter = (source: string, file: string) => string | Promise<string>;

// v2.5.0: 文件级切分
export interface FileSelector {
  kind: 'file';
  scope: 'head' | 'tail' | 'all';
  line?: number;
  snippet?: string;
}

export interface ReplaceAssertions {
  /** 新内容必须包含这些文本 */
  include?: string[];
  /** 新内容必须不包含这些文本 */
  exclude?: string[];
  /** 新内容必须匹配这些正则（字符串形式） */
  regex?: string[];
  /** 替换后代码必须包含特定的 AST 节点，如 "method:run" */
  hasAst?: string[];
  /** 替换后代码不能包含特定的 AST 节点，如 "variable:oldVar" */
  noAst?: string[];
}

export interface ReplaceOptions {
  /** 智能断言：写盘前对即将替换的代码进行特征校验 */
  assertions?: ReplaceAssertions;
  /** 模糊匹配：忽略锚点名大小写，允许冒号前后无空格 */
  fuzzy?: boolean;
  file: string;
  anchor: string;
  content: string;
  fingerprint: string;
  autoInjectAnchor?: boolean;
  astSelector?: AstSelector;
  /** v2.0.0: 通用选择器，供多语言 Parser 使用 */
  selector?: any;
  snippetSelector?: SnippetSelector;
  dryRun?: boolean;
  format?: Formatter;
}

export type ReplaceStatus = 'replaced' | 'skipped' | 'injected' | 'failed';

export interface FailureContext {
  oldCode: string;

}

}

}

================================================================================
FILE: src/verify.ts
================================================================================
import { existsSync, readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync, type Dirent } from 'node:fs';
import { join, relative, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { isCacheHit, loadCache, saveCache, statFile } from './cache.js';
import { getChangedFiles, isGitRepo } from './git.js';
import { loadState } from './state.js';
import { matchesAnyGlob } from './glob.js';
import type {
  VerifyAnchorEntry,
  VerifyDuplicateAcross,
  VerifyDuplicateWithin,
  VerifyCacheEntry,
  VerifyCacheStats,
  VerifyFileReport,
  VerifyFingerprintMismatch,
  VerifyIssue,
  VerifyOrphan,
  VerifyReport,
} from './types.js';

const START_RE = /^\s*\/\/\s*@TRED_START:\s*(.+?)(?:\s*\|\s*fp=([a-f0-9]{8}))?\s*$/;
const END_RE = /^\s*\/\/\s*@TRED_END:\s*(.+?)\s*$/;

const DEFAULT_EXTS = ['.ts', '.tsx', '.js', '.jsx', '.mjs', '.cjs'];
const DEFAULT_IGNORE = new Set([
  'node_modules', '.git', 'dist', 'build', '.next', '.tred-tmp', '.turbo', '.tred-cache',
]);

export interface ScanResult {
  anchors: VerifyAnchorEntry[];
  orphans: VerifyOrphan[];
}

/**
 * 线性扫描单个文件的所有锚点。
 * - 配对采用 FIFO：第一个 START 配下一个同名 END（支持嵌套不同名锚点）
 * - 有 START 无 END → 孤儿 START；有 END 无 START → 孤儿 END
 */
export function scanFileAnchors(source: string): ScanResult {
  const lines = source.split('\n');
  const anchors: VerifyAnchorEntry[] = [];
  const orphans: VerifyOrphan[] = [];
  const pending = new Map<string, Array<{ line: number; fp: string | null }>>();

  for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) {
    const lineNo = i + 1;
    const sm = lines[i].match(START_RE);
    if (sm) {
      const name = sm[1].trim();
      const fp = sm[2] ?? null;
      const list = pending.get(name) ?? [];
      list.push({ line: lineNo, fp });
      pending.set(name, list);
      continue;
    }
    const em = lines[i].match(END_RE);
    if (em) {
      const name = em[1].trim();
      const list = pending.get(name);
      if (list && list.length > 0) {
        const head = list.shift()!;
        anchors.push({ name, line: head.line, endLine: lineNo, fp: head.fp });
        if (list.length === 0) pending.delete(name);
      } else {
        orphans.push({ name, line: lineNo, kind: 'end' });
      }
    }
  }

  for (const [name, list] of pending) {
    for (const item of list) {
      orphans.push({ name, line: item.line, kind: 'start' });
    }
  }

  anchors.sort((a, b) => a.line - b.line);
  orphans.sort((a, b) => a.line - b.line);
  return { anchors, orphans };
}

export interface VerifyOptions {
  dir: string;
  strict?: boolean;
  extensions?: string[];
  ignore?: Set<string>;
  /** 启用增量缓存（.tred-cache/verify.json） */
  cache?: boolean;
  /** 缓存目录，默认 .tred-cache（相对 cwd） */
  cacheDir?: string;
  /** v0.17: 只处理 git 改动文件（HEAD / main / <sha>） */
  since?: string;
  /** v0.18: 从文件读取改动列表（每行一个相对路径） */
  sinceFile?: string;
  /** v0.17: glob 排除列表，如 ['*.test.ts'] */
  exclude?: string[];
}

export function verifyDir(opts: VerifyOptions): VerifyReport {
  const exts = opts.extensions ?? DEFAULT_EXTS;
  const ignore = opts.ignore ?? DEFAULT_IGNORE;
  const files: VerifyFileReport[] = [];
  let scannedFiles = 0;

  const useCache = Boolean(opts.cache);
  const cacheDir = opts.cacheDir ?? '.tred-cache';
  const oldCache = useCache ? loadCache(cacheDir) : null;
  const newEntries: Record<string, VerifyCacheEntry> = {};
  const seenFiles = new Set<string>();
  const cacheStats: VerifyCacheStats = { enabled: useCache, hits: 0, misses: 0, removed: 0 };

  // v0.17: --since / --exclude 过滤
  let sinceSet: Set<string> | null = null;
  if (opts.since || opts.sinceFile) {
    sinceSet = new Set<string>();
    const cwd = process.cwd();
    const absDir = resolve(opts.dir);
    function addItem(item: string): void {
      if (!item) return;
      sinceSet!.add(item);
      // 尝试各种基准目录拼接，任一命中即可
      const candidates = [
        resolve(cwd, item),
        resolve(absDir, item),
        resolve(absDir, '..', item),
      ];
      for (const abs of candidates) {
        sinceSet!.add(abs);
        const relFromDir = relative(absDir, abs);
        if (relFromDir && relFromDir.indexOf('..') !== 0) sinceSet!.add(relFromDir);
      }
    }
    if (opts.since) {
      if (!isGitRepo()) {
        console.error('[tred] --since 需要 git 仓库');
      } else {
        const changed = getChangedFiles(opts.since, cwd);
        for (const item of changed) addItem(item);
        if (sinceSet.size === 0) {
          console.error('[tred] --since ' + opts.since + ': 无改动文件');
        }
      }
    }
    if (opts.sinceFile) {
      try {
        const raw = readFileSync(opts.sinceFile, 'utf-8');
        const items = raw.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        for (const item of items) addItem(item);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('[tred] --since-file 读取失败: ' + (e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e)));
        // 全部读取失败且没有 since 来源 → 还原为 null（等价于"没传过滤"）
        if (sinceSet && sinceSet.size === 0) sinceSet = null;
      }
    }
  }
  const excludeGlobs: string[] = (opts.exclude) || [];

  function walk(d: string): void {
    let entries: Dirent[];
    try {
      entries = readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true });
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    for (const entry of entries) {
      if (entry.isDirectory()) {
        if (ignore.has(entry.name)) continue;
        walk(join(d, entry.name));
      } else if (entry.isFile()) {
        const file = join(d, entry.name);
        if (!exts.some(function (e) { return file.endsWith(e); })) continue;
        const rel = relative(opts.dir, file) || entry.name;
        if (sinceSet) {
          const absFile = resolve(process.cwd(), file);
          if (!sinceSet.has(rel) && !sinceSet.has(file) && !sinceSet.has(absFile)) return;
        }
        if (matchesAnyGlob(rel, excludeGlobs)) return;
        seenFiles.add(rel);

        if (useCache) {
          const sig = statFile(file);
          if (sig) {
            const cached = oldCache?.entries[rel];
            if (isCacheHit(cached, sig)) {
              cacheStats.hits++;
              scannedFiles++;
              newEntries[rel] = { mtimeMs: sig.mtimeMs, size: sig.size, file: cached!.file };
              if (cached!.file) files.push(cached!.file);
              continue;
            }
            cacheStats.misses++;
            let source: string;
            try {
              source = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
            } catch {
              continue;
            }
            scannedFiles++;
            const scan = scanFileAnchors(source);
            const hasContent = scan.anchors.length > 0 || scan.orphans.length > 0;
            const fileReport: VerifyFileReport | null = hasContent
              ? { file: rel, anchors: scan.anchors, orphans: scan.orphans }
              : null;
            newEntries[rel] = { mtimeMs: sig.mtimeMs, size: sig.size, file: fileReport };
            if (fileReport) files.push(fileReport);
            continue;
          }
        }

        let source: string;
        try {
          source = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
        } catch {
          continue;
        }
        scannedFiles++;
        const scan = scanFileAnchors(source);
        if (scan.anchors.length === 0 && scan.orphans.length === 0) continue;
        files.push({
          file: rel,
          anchors: scan.anchors,
          orphans: scan.orphans,
        });
      }
    }
  }
  const isSingleFile = existsSync(opts.dir) && statSync(opts.dir).isFile();
  if (isSingleFile) {
    const rel = relative(process.cwd(), opts.dir) || opts.dir;
    const source = readFileSync(opts.dir, 'utf-8');
    scannedFiles++;
    const scan = scanFileAnchors(source);
    if (scan.anchors.length > 0 || scan.orphans.length > 0) {
      files.push({ file: rel, anchors: scan.anchors, orphans: scan.orphans });
    }
  } else {
    walk(opts.dir);
  }

  if (useCache) {
    if (oldCache) {
      for (const rel of Object.keys(oldCache.entries)) {
        if (!seenFiles.has(rel)) cacheStats.removed++;
      }
    }
    saveCache(cacheDir, { version: 1, entries: newEntries });
  }

  // 同文件内重复
  const duplicatesWithin: VerifyDuplicateWithin[] = [];
  for (const fr of files) {
    const byName = new Map<string, number[]>();
    for (const a of fr.anchors) {
      const arr = byName.get(a.name) ?? [];
      arr.push(a.line);
      byName.set(a.name, arr);
    }
    for (const [name, lines] of byName) {
      if (lines.length > 1) duplicatesWithin.push({ file: fr.file, name, lines });
    }
  }

  // 跨文件同名
  const globalByName = new Map<string, Array<{ file: string; line: number; fp: string | null }>>();
  for (const fr of files) {
    for (const a of fr.anchors) {
      const arr = globalByName.get(a.name) ?? [];
      arr.push({ file: fr.file, line: a.line, fp: a.fp });
      globalByName.set(a.name, arr);
    }
  }
  const duplicatesAcross: VerifyDuplicateAcross[] = [];
  for (const [name, entries] of globalByName) {
    const uniqFiles = new Set(entries.map(function (e) { return e.file; }));
    if (uniqFiles.size > 1) duplicatesAcross.push({ name, entries });
  }

  // 跨文件指纹不一致（非 null fp 出现 >=2 个不同值）
  const fingerprintMismatches: VerifyFingerprintMismatch[] = [];
  for (const [name, entries] of globalByName) {
    const fps = new Set<string>();
    for (const e of entries) if (e.fp) fps.add(e.fp);
    if (fps.size >= 2) fingerprintMismatches.push({ name, entries });
  }

  // 汇总 issues
  const issues: VerifyIssue[] = [];
  for (const fr of files) {
    for (const o of fr.orphans) {
      issues.push({
        level: 'error',
        code: o.kind === 'start' ? 'ORPHAN_START' : 'ORPHAN_END',
        file: fr.file,
        line: o.line,
        message:
          o.kind === 'start'
            ? '锚点 "' + o.name + '" 有 START 无 END'
            : '锚点 "' + o.name + '" 有 END 无 START',
      });
    }
  }
  for (const d of duplicatesWithin) {
    issues.push({
      level: 'error',
      code: 'DUPLICATE_WITHIN',
      file: d.file,
      message: '锚点 "' + d.name + '" 在同文件出现 ' + d.lines.length + ' 次（行 ' + d.lines.join(', ') + '）',
    });
  }
  for (const d of duplicatesAcross) {
    const files = Array.from(new Set(d.entries.map(function (e) { return e.file; })));
    issues.push({
      level: 'warning',
      code: 'DUPLICATE_ACROSS',
      message: '锚点 "' + d.name + '" 跨 ' + files.length + ' 个文件出现：' + files.join(', '),
    });
  }
  for (const m of fingerprintMismatches) {
    issues.push({
      level: 'warning',
      code: 'FP_MISMATCH',
      message: '锚点 "' + m.name + '" 跨文件指纹不一致',
    });
  }

  // v2.2.0: 检查 .tred/state.json 中的外部锚点
  if (opts.dir === '.' || opts.dir === process.cwd()) {
    const state = loadState();
    for (const relFile of Object.keys(state.tracked)) {
      const absPath = resolve(opts.dir, relFile);
      if (!existsSync(absPath)) {
        issues.push({
          level: 'error',
          code: 'STATE_FILE_MISSING',
          file: relFile,
          message: '记录在 state.json 中的文件已丢失',
        });
        continue;
      }
      for (const [anchorName, anchorState] of Object.entries(state.tracked[relFile])) {
        issues.push({
          level: 'warning',
          code: 'STATE_ANCHOR',
          file: relFile,
          line: anchorState.approxLine,
          message: '外部锚点 [' + anchorName + '] (AST: ' + anchorState.astSignature + ')',
        });
      }
    }
  }

  const hasError = issues.some(function (i) { return i.level === 'error'; });
  const hasWarning = issues.some(function (i) { return i.level === 'warning'; });
  const ok = opts.strict ? !hasError && !hasWarning : !hasError;

  const report: VerifyReport = {
    ok,
    dir: opts.dir,
    scannedFiles,
    files,
    issues,
    duplicatesWithin,
    duplicatesAcross,
    fingerprintMismatches,
  };
  if (useCache) report.cacheStats = cacheStats;
  return report;
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/watch.ts
================================================================================
import { readdirSync, watch as fsWatch } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

const WATCH_IGNORE = new Set([
  'node_modules', '.git', 'dist', 'build', '.next', '.tred-tmp', '.turbo', '.tred-cache',
]);

export interface WatchHandle {
  close: () => void;
}

export function watchDir(dir: string, onChange: () => void, debounceMs?: number): WatchHandle {
  const delay = debounceMs ?? 200;
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
  let closed = false;

  const trigger = (): void => {
    if (closed) return;
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    timer = setTimeout(() => {
      timer = null;
      if (!closed) onChange();
    }, delay);
  };

  const cleanups: (() => void)[] = [];

  function attachOne(d: string): void {
    try {
      const w = fsWatch(d, trigger);
      cleanups.push(() => { try { w.close(); } catch { /* */ } });
    } catch { /* skip */ }
  }

  function walk(d: string): void {
    try {
      attachOne(d);
      const entries = readdirSync(d, { withFileTypes: true });
      for (const e of entries) {
        if (e.isDirectory() && !WATCH_IGNORE.has(e.name)) {
          walk(join(d, e.name));
        }
      }
    } catch { /* skip */ }
  }

  let usedRecursive = false;
  try {
    const w = fsWatch(dir, { recursive: true }, trigger);
    cleanups.push(() => { try { w.close(); } catch { /* */ } });
    usedRecursive = true;
  } catch {
    walk(dir);
  }

  return {
    close: () => {
      closed = true;
      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
      for (const fn of cleanups) {
        try { fn(); } catch { /* */ }
      }
    },
  };
}


================================================================================
FILE: test/annotate-dir.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { annotateDir } from '../src/index.js';

function setup(files: Record<string, string>): string {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-ad-'));
  for (const rel of Object.keys(files)) {
    const full = join(dir, rel);
    mkdirSync(join(full, '..'), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(full, files[rel]);
  }
  return dir;
}

describe('annotateDir', function () {
  it('多文件：class + method + function 全覆盖', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': 'export class Foo {\n  bar() {}\n}\nexport function h() {}\n',
      'b.ts': 'export class Baz {}\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true, methods: true, functions: true });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r.annotatedFiles, 2);
      assert.equal(r.totalActions, 8);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('dry-run 不写盘', function () {
    const dir = setup({ 'a.ts': 'export class Foo {}\n' });
    try {
      const before = readFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'utf-8');
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true, apply: false });
      assert.equal(r.apply, false);
      assert.equal(readFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'utf-8'), before);
      assert.equal(r.files[0].applied, false);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('--apply 落盘', function () {
    const dir = setup({ 'a.ts': 'export class Foo {}\n' });
    try {
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true, apply: true });
      assert.equal(r.apply, true);
      assert.equal(r.files[0].applied, true);
      const after = readFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'utf-8');
      assert.match(after, /@TRED_START: Foo/);
      assert.match(after, /@TRED_END: Foo/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('空目录 -> ok，0 文件', function () {
    const dir = setup({});
    try {
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 0);
      assert.equal(r.annotatedFiles, 0);
      assert.equal(r.totalActions, 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('忽略 node_modules / dist', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': 'export class Foo {}\n',
      'node_modules/x.ts': 'export class Lib {}\n',
      'dist/y.ts': 'export class Built {}\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true });
      assert.equal(r.annotatedFiles, 1);
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 1);
      assert.match(r.files[0].file, /a\.ts$/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('幂等：二次跑 0 新增', function () {
    const dir = setup({ 'a.ts': 'export class Foo {}\n' });
    try {
      annotateDir({ dir, classes: true, apply: true });
      const r2 = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true, apply: true });
      assert.equal(r2.totalActions, 0);
      assert.equal(r2.annotatedFiles, 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('语法错的文件标 failed，不影响其他文件', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'good.ts': 'export class Foo {}\n',
      'bad.ts': 'export class Bar {}\nconst x = ;\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true, apply: false });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
      assert.equal(r.failedFiles, 1);
      const good = r.files.find((f) => f.file.endsWith('good.ts'));
      assert.ok(good);
      assert.equal(good!.ok, true);
      const bad = r.files.find((f) => f.file.endsWith('bad.ts'));
      assert.ok(bad);
      assert.equal(bad!.ok, false);
      assert.ok(bad!.syntaxErrors && bad!.syntaxErrors.length > 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('没有匹配节点时 files 为空', function () {
    const dir = setup({ 'a.ts': 'export const x = 1;\n' });
    try {
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true, methods: true, functions: true });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('嵌套子目录递归扫描', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': 'export class Foo {}\n',
      'sub/b.ts': 'export class Bar {}\n',
      'sub/deep/c.ts': 'export class Baz {}\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true });
      assert.equal(r.annotatedFiles, 3);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/annotate-order.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { annotateDir } from '../src/index.js';

function setup(files: Record<string, string>): string {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-ao-'));
  for (const rel of Object.keys(files)) {
    const full = join(dir, rel);
    mkdirSync(join(full, '..'), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(full, files[rel]);
  }
  return dir;
}

describe('annotateDir order', function () {
  it('source（默认）：按路径字母排', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'z.ts': 'export class Zed {}\n',
      'a.ts': 'export class Alpha {}\n',
      'm.ts': 'export class Mike {}\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true });
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 3);
      assert.ok(r.files[0].file.endsWith('a.ts'));
      assert.ok(r.files[1].file.endsWith('m.ts'));
      assert.ok(r.files[2].file.endsWith('z.ts'));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('name：按首锚点名排', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'z.ts': 'export class Charlie {}\n',
      'a.ts': 'export class Alpha {}\n',
      'm.ts': 'export class Bravo {}\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true, order: 'name' });
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 3);
      // 按 class 名字母排：Alpha, Bravo, Charlie
      assert.ok(r.files[0].file.endsWith('a.ts'));
      assert.ok(r.files[1].file.endsWith('m.ts'));
      assert.ok(r.files[2].file.endsWith('z.ts'));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('kind：按 nodeKind 排', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'f.ts': 'export function helper() {}\n',
      'c.ts': 'export class Alpha {}\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true, functions: true, order: 'kind' });
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 2);
      // class < function
      assert.ok(r.files[0].file.endsWith('c.ts'));
      assert.ok(r.files[1].file.endsWith('f.ts'));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('空目录 → 排序无副作用', function () {
    const dir = setup({});
    try {
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true, order: 'name' });
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/annotate.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { annotateFile, annotateFileContent } from '../src/index.js';

describe('annotateFileContent', function () {
  it('classes: 顶层 class 加 START/END', function () {
    const src = 'export class Foo {\n  bar() {}\n}\n';
    const r = annotateFileContent(src, 'x.ts', { classes: true });
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 2);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /@TRED_START: Foo/);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /@TRED_END: Foo/);
  });

  it('methods: 用 ClassName#methodName', function () {
    const src = 'export class Foo {\n  bar() {}\n  baz() {}\n}\n';
    const r = annotateFileContent(src, 'x.ts', { methods: true });
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 4);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /@TRED_START: Foo#bar/);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /@TRED_START: Foo#baz/);
  });

  it('functions: 顶层 function 用函数名', function () {
    const src = 'export function helper() { return 1; }\n';
    const r = annotateFileContent(src, 'x.ts', { functions: true });
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 2);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /@TRED_START: helper/);
  });

  it('全部三类一起加：class + method + function', function () {
    const src = [
      'export class Foo {',
      '  bar() {}',
      '}',
      'export function helper() {}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = annotateFileContent(src, 'x.ts', { classes: true, methods: true, functions: true });
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 6);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /@TRED_START: Foo\n/);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /@TRED_START: Foo#bar/);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /@TRED_START: helper/);
  });

  it('已存在锚点不重复加', function () {
    const src = [
      '// @TRED_START: Foo | fp=11111111',
      'export class Foo {',
      '  bar() {}',
      '}',
      '// @TRED_END: Foo',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = annotateFileContent(src, 'x.ts', { classes: true });
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 0);
    assert.equal(r.nextSource, src);
  });

  it('嵌套 namespace 内 class 缩进保持', function () {
    const src = [
      'namespace NS {',
      '  export class Foo {}',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = annotateFileContent(src, 'x.ts', { classes: true });
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /\n  \/\/ @TRED_START: Foo\n  export class Foo/);
  });

  it('无匹配节点 → actions 空', function () {
    const src = 'export const x = 1;\n';
    const r = annotateFileContent(src, 'x.ts', { classes: true, methods: true, functions: true });
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 0);
    assert.equal(r.nextSource, src);
  });

  it('class 内嵌套 class：内外均加锚点且不重叠', function () {
    const src = [
      'export class Outer {',
      '  inner() {',
      '    return 1;',
      '  }',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = annotateFileContent(src, 'x.ts', { classes: true, methods: true });
    // 2 (class Outer) + 2 (method inner) = 4
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 4);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /@TRED_START: Outer\n/);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /@TRED_START: Outer#inner/);
  });
});

describe('annotateFile', function () {
  it('dry-run 不写盘，applied=false', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-an-'));
    const file = join(dir, 'x.ts');
    writeFileSync(file, 'export class Foo {}\n');
    try {
      const r = annotateFile({ file, classes: true });
      assert.equal(r.applied, false);
      assert.equal(r.actions.length, 2);
      assert.equal(readFileSync(file, 'utf-8'), 'export class Foo {}\n');
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('apply 写盘，action 已落地', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-an-'));
    const file = join(dir, 'x.ts');
    writeFileSync(file, 'export class Foo {}\n');
    try {
      const r = annotateFile({ file, classes: true, apply: true });
      assert.equal(r.applied, true);
      const after = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
      assert.match(after, /@TRED_START: Foo/);
      assert.match(after, /@TRED_END: Foo/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('文件不存在 → ok=false', function () {
    const r = annotateFile({ file: '/no/such/file.ts', classes: true });
    assert.equal(r.ok, false);
    assert.match((r.syntaxErrors || []).join(' '), /读取失败/);
  });

  it('annotate 结果可直接 verify 校验通过', async function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-an-'));
    const file = join(dir, 'x.ts');
    writeFileSync(file, [
      'export class Foo {',
      '  bar() {}',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n'));
    try {
      annotateFile({ file, classes: true, methods: true, apply: true });
      const { verifyDir } = await import('../src/index.js');
      const v = verifyDir({ dir });
      assert.equal(v.ok, true, JSON.stringify(v.issues));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/ast-outline.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdtempSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { buildOutline, makeFingerprint, replace } from '../src/index.js';

const SRC = [
  'export class GraphEngine {',
  '  loadUnlocked(): void {}',
  '  save(): void {}',
  '}',
  '',
  'export function helper(): void {}',
  '',
].join('\n');

describe('buildOutline', function () {
  it('提取 class + method + function，含 selector', function () {
    const o = buildOutline('t.ts', SRC);
    assert.equal(o.length, 2);
    assert.equal(o[0].kind, 'class');
    assert.equal(o[0].name, 'GraphEngine');
    assert.equal(o[0].line, 1);
    assert.equal(o[0].children?.length, 2);
    const m0 = o[0].children![0];
    assert.equal(m0.kind, 'method');
    assert.equal(m0.name, 'loadUnlocked');
    assert.equal(m0.line, 2);
    assert.deepEqual(m0.selector, { kind: 'method', className: 'GraphEngine', methodName: 'loadUnlocked' });
    assert.equal(o[1].kind, 'function');
    assert.equal(o[1].name, 'helper');
  });

  it('无 class/function 返回空数组', function () {
    const o = buildOutline('t.ts', 'const x = 1;\n');
    assert.equal(o.length, 0);
  });

  it('selector 可直接喂给 replace autoInjectAnchor', async function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-outline-'));
    const file = join(dir, 't.ts');
    try {
      writeFileSync(file, SRC);
      const o = buildOutline(file, readFileSync(file, 'utf-8'));
      const methodSel = o[0].children![0].selector!;
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'X', source: 'outline-test', version: '1' });
      const r = await replace({
        file: file, anchor: 'X',
        content: 'loadUnlocked(): void {\n  console.log(1);\n}',
        fingerprint: fp, autoInjectAnchor: true, astSelector: methodSel,
      });
      assert.equal(r.status, 'injected');
      assert.match(readFileSync(file, 'utf-8'), /console\.log\(1\)/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/baseline.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync, existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { buildBaseline, filterIssuesByBaseline, loadBaseline, saveBaseline, verifyDir } from '../src/index.js';

describe('baseline save/load', function () {
  it('save 后 load 可读回', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-bl-'));
    try {
      const fp = join(dir, 'b.json');
      saveBaseline(fp, { version: 1, createdAt: '2026-01-01T00:00:00Z', dir: '.', issues: [] });
      const b = loadBaseline(fp);
      assert.ok(b);
      assert.equal(b!.version, 1);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
  it('文件缺失 → null', function () {
    assert.equal(loadBaseline('/no/such/baseline.json'), null);
  });
  it('损坏 JSON → null', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-bl-'));
    try {
      const fp = join(dir, 'bad.json');
      writeFileSync(fp, '{bad');
      assert.equal(loadBaseline(fp), null);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
  it('版本不符 → null', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-bl-'));
    try {
      const fp = join(dir, 'v2.json');
      writeFileSync(fp, JSON.stringify({ version: 2, issues: [] }));
      assert.equal(loadBaseline(fp), null);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});

describe('buildBaseline + filterIssuesByBaseline', function () {
  function setup(): { dir: string; report: ReturnType<typeof verifyDir> } {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-bl2-'));
    writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), '// @TRED_START: X\nbody\n');
    const report = verifyDir({ dir });
    return { dir, report };
  }

  it('buildBaseline 把 issues 拷贝过去', function () {
    const { dir, report } = setup();
    try {
      assert.ok(report.issues.length > 0);
      const bl = buildBaseline(report);
      assert.equal(bl.issues.length, report.issues.length);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('filterIssuesByBaseline 全部命中 → ok=true', function () {
    const { dir, report } = setup();
    try {
      const bl = buildBaseline(report);
      const filtered = filterIssuesByBaseline(report, bl, false);
      assert.equal(filtered.issues.length, 0);
      assert.equal(filtered.ok, true);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('新增 issue 未命中基线 → ok=false', function () {
    const { dir, report } = setup();
    try {
      const bl = buildBaseline(report);
      // 加一个新 issue
      const r2 = verifyDir({ dir });
      const augmented = Object.assign({}, r2, {
        issues: r2.issues.concat([{ level: 'error', code: 'NEW', message: '新问题', file: 'b.ts', line: 1 }]),
        ok: false,
      });
      const filtered = filterIssuesByBaseline(augmented, bl, false);
      assert.equal(filtered.issues.length, 1);
      assert.equal(filtered.ok, false);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('strict 模式下 warning 也阻塞', function () {
    const { dir, report } = setup();
    try {
      const bl = buildBaseline(report);
      const augmented = Object.assign({}, report, {
        issues: [{ level: 'warning', code: 'W', message: 'warn', file: 'c.ts', line: 1 }],
        ok: true,
      });
      const filteredNonStrict = filterIssuesByBaseline(augmented, bl, false);
      assert.equal(filteredNonStrict.ok, true);
      const filteredStrict = filterIssuesByBaseline(augmented, bl, true);
      assert.equal(filteredStrict.ok, false);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/cache.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { getCachePath, isCacheHit, loadCache, saveCache, statFile, verifyDir } from '../src/index.js';

describe('cache 原子读写', function () {
  function setup(): { dir: string; cacheDir: string } {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-cache-'));
    const cacheDir = join(dir, '.tred-cache');
    return { dir, cacheDir };
  }

  it('saveCache 后 loadCache 可读回', function () {
    const { cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      saveCache(cacheDir, { version: 1, entries: {} });
      const c = loadCache(cacheDir);
      assert.ok(c);
      assert.equal(c!.version, 1);
    } finally { rmSync(cacheDir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('loadCache 缺失文件 → null', function () {
    const { cacheDir } = setup();
    assert.equal(loadCache(cacheDir), null);
  });

  it('loadCache 版本不符 → null', function () {
    const { cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      const p = getCachePath(cacheDir);
      mkdirSync(cacheDir, { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(p, JSON.stringify({ version: 999, entries: {} }));
      assert.equal(loadCache(cacheDir), null);
    } finally { rmSync(cacheDir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('loadCache 损坏 JSON → null', function () {
    const { cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      const p = getCachePath(cacheDir);
      mkdirSync(cacheDir, { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(p, '{bad json');
      assert.equal(loadCache(cacheDir), null);
    } finally { rmSync(cacheDir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('statFile 不存在 → null', function () {
    assert.equal(statFile('/definitely/not/exist.ts'), null);
  });

  it('isCacheHit 双条件都匹配才 true', function () {
    const sig = { mtimeMs: 100, size: 50 };
    assert.equal(isCacheHit(undefined, sig), false);
    assert.equal(isCacheHit({ mtimeMs: 100, size: 50, file: null }, sig), true);
    assert.equal(isCacheHit({ mtimeMs: 101, size: 50, file: null }, sig), false);
    assert.equal(isCacheHit({ mtimeMs: 100, size: 51, file: null }, sig), false);
  });
});

describe('verifyDir 缓存集成', function () {
  function setup(): { dir: string; cacheDir: string } {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-vc-'));
    const cacheDir = join(dir, '.tred-cache');
    mkdirSync(join(dir, 'src'), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(join(dir, 'src/a.ts'), '// @TRED_START: A | fp=11111111\n// @TRED_END: A\n');
    writeFileSync(join(dir, 'src/b.ts'), 'export const x = 1;\n');
    return { dir, cacheDir };
  }

  it('首次 cache 全 miss，二次全 hit', function () {
    const { dir, cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      const r1 = verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      assert.ok(r1.cacheStats);
      assert.equal(r1.cacheStats!.hits, 0);
      assert.equal(r1.cacheStats!.misses, 2);
      assert.equal(r1.files.length, 1);

      const r2 = verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      assert.equal(r2.cacheStats!.hits, 2);
      assert.equal(r2.cacheStats!.misses, 0);
      assert.equal(r2.files.length, 1);
      assert.deepEqual(r2.files[0].anchors, r1.files[0].anchors);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('改文件 → 该文件 miss，其余 hit', function () {
    const { dir, cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'src/a.ts'), '// @TRED_START: A | fp=22222222\n// @TRED_END: A\n');
      const r2 = verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      assert.equal(r2.cacheStats!.hits, 1);
      assert.equal(r2.cacheStats!.misses, 1);
      assert.equal(r2.files[0].anchors[0].fp, "22222222");
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('删文件 → removed 计数', function () {
    const { dir, cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      rmSync(join(dir, 'src/b.ts'));
      const r2 = verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      assert.equal(r2.cacheStats!.removed, 1);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('未开 cache：cacheStats 为 undefined，且不写缓存文件', function () {
    const { dir, cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      const r = verifyDir({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.cacheStats, undefined);
      assert.throws(() => readFileSync(getCachePath(cacheDir), 'utf-8'));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('缓存文件损坏 → 全 miss 后重建', function () {
    const { dir, cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      writeFileSync(getCachePath(cacheDir), '{bad');
      const r2 = verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      assert.equal(r2.cacheStats!.hits, 0);
      assert.equal(r2.cacheStats!.misses, 2);
      const c = loadCache(cacheDir);
      assert.ok(c);
      assert.equal(Object.keys(c!.entries).length, 2);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('.tred-cache 本身不会被扫进结果', function () {
    const { dir, cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      const r2 = verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      assert.equal(r2.scannedFiles, 2);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/cli-worker.ts
================================================================================
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { _setStdinOverride, cliMain } from '../src/cli.js';

interface Case {
  id: string;
  args: string[];
  stdin?: string;
}

interface Result {
  id: string;
  status: number;
  stdout: string;
  stderr: string;
}

const casesFile = process.argv[2];
if (!casesFile) {
  process.stderr.write('usage: cli-worker.ts <cases.json>\n');
  process.exit(1);
}

const cases = JSON.parse(readFileSync(casesFile, "utf-8")) as Case[];

const origOut = process.stdout.write.bind(process.stdout);
const origErr = process.stderr.write.bind(process.stderr);
const origExit = process.exit;

const results: Result[] = [];

for (const c of cases) {
  const stdoutBuf: string[] = [];
  const stderrBuf: string[] = [];

  process.stdout.write = ((chunk: any) => { stdoutBuf.push(String(chunk)); return true; }) as any;
  process.stderr.write = ((chunk: any) => { stderrBuf.push(String(chunk)); return true; }) as any;
  (process as any).exit = (code?: number) => { throw { __tredExit: code ?? 0 }; };

  _setStdinOverride(c.stdin);

  let exitCode = 0;
  try {
    await cliMain(c.args);
  } catch (e: any) {
    if (e && typeof e === 'object' && '__tredExit' in e) {
      exitCode = e.__tredExit;
    } else {
      stderrBuf.push('UNEXPECTED: ' + (e instanceof Error ? e.stack : String(e)));
      exitCode = 1;
    }
  } finally {
    process.stdout.write = origOut;
    process.stderr.write = origErr;
    (process as any).exit = origExit;
    _setStdinOverride(undefined);
  }

  results.push({
    id: c.id,
    status: exitCode,
    stdout: stdoutBuf.join(''),
    stderr: stderrBuf.join(''),
  });
}

origOut(JSON.stringify(results, null, 2) + '\n');


================================================================================
FILE: test/cli.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it, before, after } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join, resolve } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const ROOT = resolve(fileURLToPath(new URL('..', import.meta.url)));
const NODE = process.execPath;
const WORKER = resolve(ROOT, 'test/cli-worker.ts');

const ORIGINAL = [
  'export class GraphEngine {',
  '  loadUnlocked(): void {',
  "    console.log('old');",
  '  }',
  '}',
  '',
].join('\n');

const BODY = [
  'loadUnlocked(): void {',
  "  console.log('new');",
  '}',
  '',
].join('\n');

const SEL = '{"kind":"method","className":"GraphEngine","methodName":"loadUnlocked"}';

interface Case { id: string; args: string[]; stdin?: string; }
interface Result { id: string; status: number; stdout: string; stderr: string; }

describe('cli (single spawn)', function () {
  let dir: string;
  let results: Record<string, Result> = {};
  const filePaths: Record<string, string> = {};

  before(function () {
    dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-cli-batch-'));
    filePaths.inject = join(dir, 'inject.ts');
    filePaths.dryrun = join(dir, 'dryrun.ts');
    filePaths.stdin = join(dir, 'stdin.ts');
    filePaths.syntax = join(dir, 'syntax.ts');
    filePaths.json = join(dir, 'json.ts');
    for (const p of Object.values(filePaths)) writeFileSync(p, ORIGINAL);

    const bodyFile = join(dir, 'body.ts');
    const badFile = join(dir, 'bad.ts');
    writeFileSync(bodyFile, BODY);
    writeFileSync(badFile, [
      'loadUnlocked(): void {',
      "  console.log('oops')",
      '',
    ].join('\n'));

    function baseArgs(target: string, extra: string[]): string[] {
      return [
        'replace',
        '--file', target,
        '--anchor', 'GraphEngine#loadUnlocked',
        '--content-file', bodyFile,
        '--fp-source', 'cli-test',
        '--fp-version', '1',
        '--auto-inject',
        '--selector', SEL,
      ].concat(extra);
    }

    const cases: Case[] = [
      { id: 'help', args: ['--help'] },
      { id: 'version', args: ['--version'] },
      { id: 'inject1', args: baseArgs(filePaths.inject, []) },
      { id: 'inject2', args: baseArgs(filePaths.inject, []) },
      { id: 'dryrun', args: baseArgs(filePaths.dryrun, ['--dry-run']) },
      {
        id: 'stdin',
        args: [
          'replace',
          '--file', filePaths.stdin,
          '--anchor', 'GraphEngine#loadUnlocked',
          '--content-stdin',
          '--fp', 'abcdef12',
          '--auto-inject',
          '--selector', SEL,
        ],
        stdin: "loadUnlocked(): void {\n  console.log('stdin');\n}\n",
      },
      {
        id: 'syntax',
        args: [
          'replace',
          '--file', filePaths.syntax,
          '--anchor', 'GraphEngine#loadUnlocked',
          '--content-file', badFile,
          '--fp-source', 's', '--fp-version', '1',
          '--auto-inject',
          '--selector', SEL,
        ],
      },
      { id: 'missing-file', args: ['replace', '--anchor', 'X', '--content', 'y', '--fp', '00000000'] },
      { id: 'json', args: baseArgs(filePaths.json, ['--json']) },
      { id: 'bad-fp', args: ['replace', '--file', filePaths.json, '--anchor', 'X', '--content', 'y', '--fp', 'xyz'] },
      { id: 'fp-conflict', args: ['replace', '--file', filePaths.json, '--anchor', 'X', '--content', 'y', '--fp', '00000000', '--fp-source', 's', '--fp-version', '1'] },
    ];

    const casesFile = join(dir, '_cases.json');
    writeFileSync(casesFile, JSON.stringify(cases));

    const start = Date.now();
    const r = spawnSync(NODE, ['--import', 'tsx', WORKER, casesFile], { encoding: 'utf-8' });
    const elapsed = Date.now() - start;
    if (r.status !== 0) {
      throw new Error('cli-worker failed (status=' + r.status + '): ' + r.stderr);
    }
    const parsed = JSON.parse(r.stdout) as Result[];
    for (const res of parsed) results[res.id] = res;
    console.log('[bench] ' + cases.length + ' cases via 1 spawn in ' + elapsed + 'ms');
  });

  after(function () {
    try { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); } catch { /* */ }
  });

  it('--help 退出 0 且包含用法', function () {
    assert.equal(results.help.status, 0);
    assert.match(results.help.stdout, /用法/);
    assert.match(results.help.stdout, /--content-file/);
  });

  it('--version 与 package.json 一致', function () {
    const pkg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(resolve(ROOT, 'package.json'), 'utf-8')) as { version: string };
    assert.equal(results.version.status, 0);
    assert.equal(results.version.stdout.trim(), 'tred v' + pkg.version);
  });

  it('inject 首次成功，二次幂等 skipped', function () {
    assert.equal(results.inject1.status, 0);
    assert.match(results.inject1.stdout, /INJECTED/);
    assert.equal(results.inject2.status, 0);
    assert.match(results.inject2.stdout, /SKIPPED/);
    const after = readFileSync(filePaths.inject, 'utf-8');
    assert.match(after, /@TRED_START/);
    assert.match(after, /console\.log\('new'\)/);
  });

  it('--dry-run 不写盘且输出 diff', function () {
    assert.equal(results.dryrun.status, 0);
    assert.match(results.dryrun.stdout, /--- /);
    assert.equal(readFileSync(filePaths.dryrun, 'utf-8'), ORIGINAL);
  });

  it('--content-stdin 从管道读内容', function () {
    assert.equal(results.stdin.status, 0);
    assert.match(readFileSync(filePaths.stdin, 'utf-8'), /console\.log\('stdin'\)/);
  });

  it('语法错误退出码 1，文件不变', function () {
    assert.equal(results.syntax.status, 1);
    assert.match(results.syntax.stdout, /FAILED/);
    assert.equal(readFileSync(filePaths.syntax, 'utf-8'), ORIGINAL);
  });

  it('缺 --file 退出码 1', function () {
    assert.equal(results['missing-file'].status, 1);
    assert.match(results['missing-file'].stderr, /--file/);
  });

  it('--json 输出可解析且状态正确', function () {
    assert.equal(results.json.status, 0);
    const parsed = JSON.parse(results.json.stdout);
    assert.equal(parsed.status, 'injected');
    assert.equal(parsed.anchor, 'GraphEngine#loadUnlocked');
  });

  it('--fp 非法格式退出码 1', function () {
    assert.equal(results['bad-fp'].status, 1);
    assert.match(results['bad-fp'].stderr, /8 位/);
  });

  it('--fp 与 --fp-source/--fp-version 互斥', function () {
    assert.equal(results['fp-conflict'].status, 1);
    assert.match(results['fp-conflict'].stderr, /不能同时使用/);
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/diff-anchor.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { diffAnchor } from '../src/index.js';

function setup(contents: { a?: string; b?: string }): { dir: string; a: string; b: string } {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-da-'));
  const a = join(dir, 'a.ts');
  const b = join(dir, 'b.ts');
  if (contents.a !== undefined) writeFileSync(a, contents.a);
  if (contents.b !== undefined) writeFileSync(b, contents.b);
  return { dir, a, b };
}

describe('diffAnchor', function () {
  it('两侧找到且内容相同 → diff 空，ok=true', function () {
    const src = [
      'export class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: X | fp=11111111',
      '  foo() { return 1; }',
      '  // @TRED_END: X',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const { dir, a, b } = setup({ a: src, b: src });
    try {
      const r = diffAnchor({ anchor: 'X', left: a, right: b });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r.diff, '');
      assert.equal(r.left.found, true);
      assert.equal(r.right.found, true);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('内容不同 → diff 有 +/- 行', function () {
    const a = [
      '// @TRED_START: X | fp=11111111',
      'foo() { return 1; }',
      '// @TRED_END: X',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const b = [
      '// @TRED_START: X | fp=22222222',
      'foo() { return 2; }',
      '// @TRED_END: X',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const { dir, a: pa, b: pb } = setup({ a: a, b: b });
    try {
      const r = diffAnchor({ anchor: 'X', left: pa, right: pb });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.match(r.diff, /^--- /m);
      assert.match(r.diff, /\+foo\(\) \{ return 2; \}/);
      assert.match(r.diff, /-foo\(\) \{ return 1; \}/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('左侧缺锚点 → ok=false', function () {
    const b = [
      '// @TRED_START: X | fp=11111111',
      'foo() {}',
      '// @TRED_END: X',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const { dir, a, b: pb } = setup({ a: 'export const x = 1;\n', b: b });
    try {
      const r = diffAnchor({ anchor: 'X', left: a, right: pb });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
      assert.match(r.message || '', /左侧/);
      assert.equal(r.left.found, false);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('右侧缺锚点 → ok=false', function () {
    const a = [
      '// @TRED_START: X | fp=11111111',
      'foo() {}',
      '// @TRED_END: X',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const { dir, a: pa, b } = setup({ a: a, b: 'export const y = 2;\n' });
    try {
      const r = diffAnchor({ anchor: 'X', left: pa, right: b });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
      assert.match(r.message || '', /右侧/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('两侧都缺锚点 → ok=false，message 说明双侧', function () {
    const { dir, a, b } = setup({ a: 'x\n', b: 'y\n' });
    try {
      const r = diffAnchor({ anchor: 'X', left: a, right: b });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
      assert.match(r.message || '', /两侧/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('文件不存在 → 该侧 found=false', function () {
    const { dir, a } = setup({ a: '// @TRED_START: X\nfoo\n// @TRED_END: X\n' });
    try {
      const r = diffAnchor({ anchor: 'X', left: a, right: '/no/such/file.ts' });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
      assert.equal(r.right.found, false);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('只对比锚点内部，两侧 START/END 位置偏移不影响', function () {
    const a = [
      'export class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: X | fp=11111111',
      '  foo() {}',
      '  // @TRED_END: X',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const b = [
      '// 完全不同的文件头',
      'const x = 1;',
      'export class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: X | fp=11111111',
      '  foo() {}',
      '  // @TRED_END: X',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const { dir, a: pa, b: pb } = setup({ a: a, b: b });
    try {
      const r = diffAnchor({ anchor: 'X', left: pa, right: pb });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r.diff, '', '锚点内内容相同，忽略外部差异');
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/diff-dir.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { diffDir } from '../src/index.js';

const FILE_A = [
  'export class A {',
  '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=11111111',
  '  x(): void { return 1; }',
  '  // @TRED_END: A#x',
  '}',
  '',
].join('\n');

const FILE_A_CHANGED = [
  'export class A {',
  '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=22222222',
  '  x(): void { return 2; }',
  '  // @TRED_END: A#x',
  '}',
  '',
].join('\n');

function setup(left: Record<string, string>, right: Record<string, string>): { leftDir: string; rightDir: string; dir: string } {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-dd-'));
  const leftDir = join(dir, 'left');
  const rightDir = join(dir, 'right');
  for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(left)) {
    const full = join(leftDir, rel);
    mkdirSync(join(full, '..'), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(full, content);
  }
  for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(right)) {
    const full = join(rightDir, rel);
    mkdirSync(join(full, '..'), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(full, content);
  }
  return { leftDir, rightDir, dir };
}

describe('diffDir', function () {
  it('两侧相同 → ok，0 changed', function () {
    const { leftDir, rightDir, dir } = setup({ 'a.ts': FILE_A }, { 'a.ts': FILE_A });
    try {
      const r = diffDir({ leftDir, rightDir });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r.stats.compared, 1);
      assert.equal(r.stats.changed, 0);
      assert.equal(r.stats.unchanged, 1);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('内容变化 → changed > 0，diff 非空', function () {
    const { leftDir, rightDir, dir } = setup({ 'a.ts': FILE_A }, { 'a.ts': FILE_A_CHANGED });
    try {
      const r = diffDir({ leftDir, rightDir });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
      assert.equal(r.stats.changed, 1);
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 1);
      assert.match(r.files[0].anchors[0].diff, /return 2/);
      assert.match(r.files[0].anchors[0].diff, /return 1/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('右侧文件缺失 → missing', function () {
    const { leftDir, rightDir, dir } = setup({ 'a.ts': FILE_A }, {});
    try {
      const r = diffDir({ leftDir, rightDir });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
      assert.equal(r.stats.missing, 1);
      assert.match(r.files[0].anchors[0].message || '', /右侧文件缺失/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('anchorFilter 只对比匹配的锚点', function () {
    const FILE_2 = [
      'export class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=11111111',
      '  x(): void {}',
      '  // @TRED_END: A#x',
      '  // @TRED_START: A#y | fp=11111111',
      '  y(): void {}',
      '  // @TRED_END: A#y',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const { leftDir, rightDir, dir } = setup({ 'a.ts': FILE_2 }, { 'a.ts': FILE_2 });
    try {
      const r = diffDir({ leftDir, rightDir, anchorFilter: 'A#x' });
      assert.equal(r.stats.compared, 1);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('忽略 node_modules', function () {
    const { leftDir, rightDir, dir } = setup(
      { 'a.ts': FILE_A, 'node_modules/x.ts': FILE_A },
      { 'a.ts': FILE_A },
    );
    try {
      const r = diffDir({ leftDir, rightDir });
      assert.equal(r.stats.compared, 1);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/diff-lines.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { getDiffLines } from '../src/index.js';

const HAS_GIT = spawnSync('git', ['--version'], { encoding: 'utf-8' }).status === 0;

function gitIn(dir: string, args: string[]): void {
  const r = spawnSync('git', args, { cwd: dir, encoding: 'utf-8' });
  if (r.status !== 0) throw new Error('git ' + args.join(' ') + ': ' + r.stderr);
}

describe('getDiffLines', { skip: !HAS_GIT ? 'git not installed' : false }, function () {
  it('单文件单行修改：返回该行号', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-dl-'));
    try {
      gitIn(dir, ['init', '-b', 'main']);
      gitIn(dir, ['config', 'user.email', 't@t.com']);
      gitIn(dir, ['config', 'user.name', 'T']);
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'line1\nline2\nline3\n');
      gitIn(dir, ['add', '.']);
      gitIn(dir, ['commit', '-m', 'init']);
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'line1\nMODIFIED\nline3\n');
      gitIn(dir, ['add', '.']);
      gitIn(dir, ['commit', '-m', 'mod']);

      const map = getDiffLines('HEAD~1', dir);
      const lines = map.get('a.ts');
      assert.ok(lines);
      assert.ok(lines!.has(2));
      assert.equal(lines!.has(1), false);
      assert.equal(lines!.has(3), false);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('多文件改动：各自返回行号', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-dl-'));
    try {
      gitIn(dir, ['init', '-b', 'main']);
      gitIn(dir, ['config', 'user.email', 't@t.com']);
      gitIn(dir, ['config', 'user.name', 'T']);
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'a1\na2\n');
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'b.ts'), 'b1\nb2\n');
      gitIn(dir, ['add', '.']);
      gitIn(dir, ['commit', '-m', 'init']);
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'a1\nMOD\na3\n');
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'b.ts'), 'b1\nB_MOD\n');
      gitIn(dir, ['add', '.']);
      gitIn(dir, ['commit', '-m', 'mods']);

      const map = getDiffLines('HEAD~1', dir);
      assert.ok(map.get('a.ts'));
      assert.ok(map.get('b.ts'));
      assert.ok(map.get('a.ts')!.has(2));
      assert.ok(map.get('b.ts')!.has(2));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('无改动：空 Map', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-dl-'));
    try {
      gitIn(dir, ['init', '-b', 'main']);
      gitIn(dir, ['config', 'user.email', 't@t.com']);
      gitIn(dir, ['config', 'user.name', 'T']);
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'x\n');
      gitIn(dir, ['add', '.']);
      gitIn(dir, ['commit', '-m', 'init']);

      const map = getDiffLines('HEAD', dir);
      assert.equal(map.size, 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('非法 ref：返回空 Map（不抛错）', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-dl-'));
    try {
      gitIn(dir, ['init', '-b', 'main']);
      gitIn(dir, ['config', 'user.email', 't@t.com']);
      gitIn(dir, ['config', 'user.name', 'T']);
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'x\n');
      gitIn(dir, ['add', '.']);
      gitIn(dir, ['commit', '-m', 'init']);
      const map = getDiffLines('no-such-ref-999', dir);
      assert.equal(map.size, 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/doctor.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { doctor } from '../src/index.js';

describe('doctor', function () {
  it('空目录：基础字段存在，ok=true（无 error）', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-doc-'));
    try {
      const r = doctor({ dir });
      assert.equal(typeof r.ok, 'boolean');
      assert.equal(typeof r.tredVersion, 'string');
      assert.equal(typeof r.packageVersion, 'string');
      assert.equal(typeof r.nodeVersion, 'string');
      assert.equal(typeof r.platform, 'string');
      assert.ok(Array.isArray(r.suggestions));
      assert.ok(r.suggestions.length > 0);
      assert.equal(r.anchors.errors, 0);
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('有 error 级锚点：ok=false，suggestions 提到问题', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-doc-'));
    try {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'broken.ts'), '// @TRED_START: X\nbody\n');
      const r = doctor({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
      assert.ok(r.anchors.errors > 0);
      const hasSuggestion = r.suggestions.some((s) => s.indexOf('error') >= 0);
      assert.equal(hasSuggestion, true);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('版本文件一致', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-doc-'));
    try {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'package.json'), JSON.stringify({ version: '9.9.9' }));
      writeFileSync(join(dir, '.tred-version'), '9.9.9\n');
      const r = doctor({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.packageVersion, '9.9.9');
      assert.equal(r.tredVersion, '9.9.9');
      const mismatch = r.suggestions.some((s) => s.indexOf('版本不一致') >= 0);
      assert.equal(mismatch, false);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('版本不一致：suggestion 报告', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-doc-'));
    try {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'package.json'), JSON.stringify({ version: '1.0.0' }));
      writeFileSync(join(dir, '.tred-version'), '2.0.0\n');
      const r = doctor({ dir });
      const mismatch = r.suggestions.some((s) => s.indexOf('版本不一致') >= 0);
      assert.equal(mismatch, true);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('缓存存在时报告 entries', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-doc-'));
    try {
      mkdirSync(join(dir, '.tred-cache'), { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(
        join(dir, '.tred-cache', 'verify.json'),
        JSON.stringify({ version: 1, entries: { 'a.ts': { mtimeMs: 1, size: 10, file: null } } }),
      );
      const r = doctor({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.cache.exists, true);
      assert.equal(r.cache.entries, 1);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('JSON 可序列化', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-doc-'));
    try {
      const r = doctor({ dir });
      const j = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(r));
      assert.equal(typeof j.nodeVersion, 'string');
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/fix-cache.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { fixDir, verifyDir } from '../src/index.js';

describe('fixDir --cache', function () {
  function setup(): { dir: string; cacheDir: string } {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-fc-'));
    const cacheDir = join(dir, '.tred-cache');
    mkdirSync(join(dir, 'src'), { recursive: true });
    writeFileSync(
      join(dir, 'src/a.ts'),
      'export class A {\n  // @TRED_END: ghost\n  x() {}\n}\n',
    );
    writeFileSync(
      join(dir, 'src/b.ts'),
      'export const x = 1;\n',
    );
    return { dir, cacheDir };
  }

  it('未开 cache：cacheStats 为 undefined，且不写缓存文件', function () {
    const { dir, cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      fixDir({ dir, apply: false });
      assert.throws(() => readFileSync(join(cacheDir, 'verify.json'), 'utf-8'));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('fix --cache --apply：修复后缓存反映新状态，verify 全 hit', function () {
    const { dir, cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      // 1. 首次 fix（无缓存）：全 miss，修掉 ghost
      const f1 = fixDir({ dir, apply: true, cache: true, cacheDir });
      assert.equal(f1.totalActions, 1);
      assert.equal(f1.files[0].applied, true);

      // 2. 紧接 verify：应该全 hit（fix 主动更新了缓存）
      const v = verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      assert.equal(v.cacheStats!.hits, v.scannedFiles);
      assert.equal(v.cacheStats!.misses, 0);
      assert.equal(v.ok, true);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('fix --cache 无修改：不更新缓存，verify 保持 hit', function () {
    const { dir, cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      // 先把 ghost 修掉并缓存
      fixDir({ dir, apply: true, cache: true, cacheDir });

      // 第二次 fix：无改动
      const f2 = fixDir({ dir, apply: true, cache: true, cacheDir });
      assert.equal(f2.totalActions, 0);

      const v = verifyDir({ dir, cache: true, cacheDir });
      assert.equal(v.cacheStats!.misses, 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('fix --cache --apply 未传 cache-dir：写入默认 .tred-cache', function () {
    const { dir } = setup();
    const cwd = process.cwd();
    try {
      process.chdir(dir);
      fixDir({ dir: ".", apply: true, cache: true });
      const cachePath = join(dir, '.tred-cache/verify.json');
      const raw = readFileSync(cachePath, 'utf-8');
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      assert.equal(parsed.version, 1);
    } finally {
      process.chdir(cwd);
      rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });

  it('fix --cache dry-run（不 apply）：不写缓存（避免与实际状态不一致）', function () {
    const { dir, cacheDir } = setup();
    try {
      const r = fixDir({ dir, apply: false, cache: true, cacheDir });
      assert.equal(r.dryRun, true);
