      // fix 未写盘，缓存条目应保留原始状态（若首次 verifyDir 内已写入）
      // 验证文件仍未被改
      const after = readFileSync(join(dir, 'src/a.ts'), 'utf-8');
      assert.match(after, /@TRED_END: ghost/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/fix.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { checkSyntax, fixDir, fixFileContent, verifyDir } from '../src/index.js';

describe('fixFileContent', function () {
  it('删孤儿 END，保留健康锚点', function () {
    const src = [
      'class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=11111111',
      '  x() {}',
      '  // @TRED_END: A#x',
      '  // @TRED_END: A#ghost',
      '  y() {}',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src);
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 1);
    assert.equal(r.actions[0].kind, 'delete-orphan-end');
    assert.equal(r.actions[0].anchorName, 'A#ghost');
    assert.equal(r.actions[0].line, 5);
    assert.doesNotMatch(r.nextSource, /A#ghost/);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /A#x/);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /x\(\) \{\}/);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /y\(\) \{\}/);
  });

  it('无孤儿 END → no-op', function () {
    const src = [
      'class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=11111111',
      '  x() {}',
      '  // @TRED_END: A#x',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src);
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 0);
    assert.equal(r.nextSource, src);
  });

  it('多处孤儿 END 一次全删', function () {
    const src = [
      '// @TRED_END: ghost1',
      'const a = 1;',
      '// @TRED_END: ghost2',
      'const b = 2;',
      '// @TRED_END: ghost3',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src);
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 3);
    assert.doesNotMatch(r.nextSource, /ghost/);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /const a = 1;/);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /const b = 2;/);
  });

  it('孤儿 START 只报告，不修复', function () {
    const src = [
      'class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=11111111',
      '  x() {}',
      '  // @TRED_START: A#y | fp=22222222',
      '  y() {}',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src);
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 0);
    assert.equal(r.nextSource, src);
    assert.ok(r.remainingOrphanStarts.some(function (o) { return o.anchorName === 'A#x'; }));
    assert.ok(r.remainingOrphanStarts.some(function (o) { return o.anchorName === 'A#y'; }));
  });

  it('孤儿 END 在文件末尾（无尾换行）', function () {
    const src = 'const a = 1;\n// @TRED_END: ghost';
    const r = fixFileContent(src);
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 1);
    assert.equal(r.nextSource, 'const a = 1;');
  });
});

describe('fixDir', function () {
  function setup(files: Record<string, string>): string {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-fix-'));
    for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
      const full = join(dir, rel);
      mkdirSync(join(full, '..'), { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(full, content);
    }
    return dir;
  }

  it('dry-run 不写盘，applied=false，有 diff', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': 'class A {\n  // @TRED_END: ghost\n  x() {}\n}\n',
    });
    try {
      const before = readFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'utf-8');
      const r = fixDir({ dir: dir });
      assert.equal(r.dryRun, true);
      assert.equal(r.totalActions, 1);
      assert.equal(r.files[0].applied, false);
      assert.match(r.files[0].diff, /@TRED_END: ghost/);
      assert.equal(readFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'utf-8'), before);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('--apply 写盘，fix 后 verify 通过', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': 'class A {\n  // @TRED_END: ghost\n  x() {}\n}\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = fixDir({ dir: dir, apply: true });
      assert.equal(r.dryRun, false);
      assert.equal(r.totalActions, 1);
      assert.equal(r.files[0].applied, true);
      const after = readFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'utf-8');
      assert.doesNotMatch(after, /@TRED_END: ghost/);
      const v = verifyDir({ dir: dir });
      assert.equal(v.ok, true);
      assert.equal(v.issues.length, 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('多文件混合修复', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': 'class A {\n  // @TRED_END: g1\n  x() {}\n}\n',
      'b.ts': 'class B {\n  // @TRED_END: g2\n  y() {}\n}\n',
      'c.ts': 'class C {\n  // @TRED_START: C#z | fp=33333333\n  z() {}\n}\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = fixDir({ dir: dir, apply: true });
      assert.equal(r.totalActions, 2);
      assert.equal(r.remainingOrphanStarts.length, 1);
      assert.equal(r.remainingOrphanStarts[0].file, 'c.ts');
      const v = verifyDir({ dir: dir });
      assert.equal(v.duplicatesWithin.length, 0);
      // c.ts 孤儿 START 还在 → 有 error
      assert.equal(v.ok, false);
      assert.ok(v.issues.some(function (i) { return i.code === 'ORPHAN_START'; }));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('健康项目 → no-op', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': 'class A {\n  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=11111111\n  x() {}\n  // @TRED_END: A#x\n}\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = fixDir({ dir: dir, apply: true });
      assert.equal(r.totalActions, 0);
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 0);
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('修复后文件语法合法', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': [
        'export class A {',
        '  // @TRED_END: ghost',
        '  x(): number { return 1; }',
        '}',
        '',
      ].join('\n'),
    });
    try {
      const r = fixDir({ dir: dir, apply: true });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r.files[0].applied, true);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});

describe('fixOrphanStart', function () {
  function setupFile(content: string): { dir: string; file: string } {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-fixos-'));
    const file = join(dir, 'a.ts');
    writeFileSync(file, content);
    return { dir, file };
  }

  it('START 后紧跟 method → 补 END 到 method } 之后', function () {
    const src = [
      'export class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=11111111',
      '  x(): void {}',
      '  y(): void {}',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src, { fixOrphanStart: true });
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 1);
    assert.equal(r.actions[0].kind, 'add-orphan-end');
    assert.equal(r.actions[0].anchorName, 'A#x');
    const lines = r.nextSource.split("\n");
    const endIdx = lines.findIndex((l) => l.includes("@TRED_END: A#x"));
    assert.ok(endIdx > 0, 'END 行应存在');
    assert.ok(lines[endIdx - 1].includes('}'), 'END 应紧跟在 method } 之后');
    assert.ok(lines[endIdx].trim().startsWith('// @TRED_END'), 'END 行缩进正确');
  });

  it('START 后紧跟顶层 statement → 补 END 到 statement 后', function () {
    const src = [
      '// @TRED_START: X',
      'const a = 1;',
      'const b = 2;',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src, { fixOrphanStart: true });
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 1);
    const lines = r.nextSource.split("\n");
    const endIdx = lines.findIndex((l) => l.includes("@TRED_END: X"));
    assert.equal(lines[endIdx - 1].trim(), 'const a = 1;');
  });

  it('START 单独在顶层 EOF → 补到文件末尾', function () {
    const src = [
      'const a = 1;',
      '// @TRED_START: X',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src, { fixOrphanStart: true });
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 1);
    assert.match(r.nextSource, /@TRED_END: X\n$/);
  });

  it('START 在 class body 内无后续成员 → 不修（保守）', function () {
    const src = [
      'export class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: X | fp=11111111',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src, { fixOrphanStart: true });
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 0);
    assert.equal(r.nextSource, src);
    assert.equal(r.remainingOrphanStarts.length, 1);
  });

  it('跨锚点：target 范围内出现另一 START → 不修', function () {
    const src = [
      '// @TRED_START: A',
      'if (true) {',
      '  // @TRED_START: B',
      '  foo();',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src, { fixOrphanStart: true });
    const aAdded = r.actions.filter((x) => x.anchorName === "A" && x.kind === "add-orphan-end");
    assert.equal(aAdded.length, 0);
    assert.ok(r.remainingOrphanStarts.some((o) => o.anchorName === "A"));
  });

  it('不跨锚点：A 后紧跟语句、B 在之后 → A 仍可修', function () {
    const src = [
      '// @TRED_START: A',
      'const a = 1;',
      '// @TRED_START: B',
      'function foo() {}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src, { fixOrphanStart: true });
    const aAdded = r.actions.filter((x) => x.anchorName === "A" && x.kind === "add-orphan-end");
    assert.equal(aAdded.length, 1, "A 应被补 END（B 不在 A 到 target 范围内）");
  });

  it('默认不开时不补 START', function () {
    const src = [
      '// @TRED_START: X',
      'const a = 1;',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src);
    assert.equal(r.actions.length, 0);
    assert.equal(r.nextSource, src);
    assert.equal(r.remainingOrphanStarts.length, 1);
  });

  it('fixDir --apply + fixOrphanStart → verify 通过', function () {
    const src = [
      'export class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=11111111',
      '  x(): void {}',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const { dir } = setupFile(src);
    try {
      const r = fixDir({ dir, apply: true, fixOrphanStart: true });
      assert.equal(r.totalActions, 1);
      assert.equal(r.remainingOrphanStarts.length, 0);
      const v = verifyDir({ dir });
      assert.equal(v.ok, true);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('补后语法合法（checkSyntax 通过）', function () {
    const src = [
      'export class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=11111111',
      '  x(): number { return 1; }',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = fixFileContent(src, { fixOrphanStart: true });
    const errors = checkSyntax("a.ts", r.nextSource);
    assert.equal(errors.length, 0);
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/git.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync, unlinkSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import {
  isGitRepo, getCurrentBranch, getHeadSha, isDirty,
  getStagedFiles, getUnstagedFiles, getUntrackedFiles,
  getChangedFiles, gitShow,
} from '../src/index.js';

const HAS_GIT = spawnSync('git', ['--version'], { encoding: 'utf-8' }).status === 0;

function gitIn(dir: string, args: string[]): void {
  const r = spawnSync('git', args, { cwd: dir, encoding: 'utf-8' });
  if (r.status !== 0) throw new Error('git ' + args.join(' ') + ' failed: ' + r.stderr);
}

function setupRepo(): string {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-git-'));
  gitIn(dir, ['init', '-b', 'main']);
  gitIn(dir, ['config', 'user.email', 'test@test.com']);
  gitIn(dir, ['config', 'user.name', 'Test']);
  writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'export const a = 1;\n');
  gitIn(dir, ['add', 'a.ts']);
  gitIn(dir, ['commit', '-m', 'init']);
  return dir;
}

describe('git APIs', { skip: !HAS_GIT ? 'git not installed' : false }, function () {
  it('isGitRepo: 仓库外 false，仓库内 true', function () {
    assert.equal(isGitRepo(os.tmpdir()), false);
    const dir = setupRepo();
    try { assert.equal(isGitRepo(dir), true); }
    finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('getCurrentBranch / getHeadSha', function () {
    const dir = setupRepo();
    try {
      assert.equal(getCurrentBranch(dir), 'main');
      assert.ok(getHeadSha(dir));
      assert.equal((getHeadSha(dir) as string).length, 40);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('isDirty：clean → false，modify → true', function () {
    const dir = setupRepo();
    try {
      assert.equal(isDirty(dir), false);
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'export const a = 2;\n');
      assert.equal(isDirty(dir), true);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('getUntrackedFiles / getStagedFiles / getUnstagedFiles', function () {
    const dir = setupRepo();
    try {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'b.ts'), 'x\n');
      assert.deepEqual(getUntrackedFiles(dir), ['b.ts']);
      gitIn(dir, ['add', 'b.ts']);
      assert.deepEqual(getStagedFiles(dir), ['b.ts']);
      assert.deepEqual(getUnstagedFiles(dir), []);
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'b.ts'), 'y\n');
      assert.deepEqual(getUnstagedFiles(dir), ['b.ts']);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('getChangedFiles("HEAD")：未提交改动 + untracked', function () {
    const dir = setupRepo();
    try {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'export const a = 9;\n');
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'new.ts'), 'x\n');
      const files = getChangedFiles('HEAD', dir);
      assert.ok(files.indexOf('a.ts') >= 0);
      assert.ok(files.indexOf('new.ts') >= 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('getChangedFiles("HEAD~0") 等价 HEAD', function () {
    const dir = setupRepo();
    try {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'export const a = 9;\n');
      const files = getChangedFiles('HEAD', dir);
      assert.ok(files.indexOf('a.ts') >= 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('gitShow：取出 HEAD 版本内容', function () {
    const dir = setupRepo();
    try {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), 'modified\n');
      const headContent = gitShow('HEAD', 'a.ts', dir);
      assert.equal(headContent, 'export const a = 1;\n');
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('gitShow：不存在路径返回 null', function () {
    const dir = setupRepo();
    try {
      assert.equal(gitShow('HEAD', 'nope.ts', dir), null);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});

================================================================================
FILE: test/init.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { init } from '../src/index.js';

describe('init', function () {
  it('空目录 → 生成 .tred/config.json', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-init-'));
    try {
      const r = init({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.ok(r.created.indexOf('.tred/config.json') >= 0 || r.created.some((c) => c.indexOf('config.json') >= 0));
      assert.ok(existsSync(join(dir, '.tred/config.json')));
      const cfg = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, '.tred/config.json'), 'utf-8'));
      assert.equal(cfg.dir, 'src');
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('二次调用 → config 被跳过', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-init-'));
    try {
      init({ dir });
      const r2 = init({ dir });
      assert.equal(r2.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r2.created.length, 0);
      assert.ok(r2.skipped.some((s) => s.indexOf('config.json') >= 0));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('--with-hook 生成 pre-commit', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-init-'));
    try {
      mkdirSync(join(dir, '.git/hooks'), { recursive: true });
      const r = init({ dir, withHook: true });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.ok(r.hookPath);
      assert.ok(existsSync(join(dir, '.git/hooks/pre-commit')));
      const hook = readFileSync(join(dir, '.git/hooks/pre-commit'), 'utf-8');
      assert.match(hook, /tred verify/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('--with-hook 但目录不存在 → 自动创建 .git/hooks', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-init-'));
    try {
      const r = init({ dir, withHook: true });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.ok(existsSync(join(dir, '.git/hooks/pre-commit')));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/render-github.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { renderGithubAnnotations, renderFixGithubAnnotations } from '../src/index.js';
import type { VerifyReport, FixReport } from '../src/index.js';

function emptyVerify(): VerifyReport {
  return {
    ok: true, dir: 'src', scannedFiles: 5, files: [],
    issues: [], duplicatesWithin: [], duplicatesAcross: [], fingerprintMismatches: [],
  };
}

describe('renderGithubAnnotations', function () {
  it('无 issue：输出 notice', function () {
    const out = renderGithubAnnotations(emptyVerify());
    assert.match(out, /^::notice title=tred::/);
    assert.match(out, /verify 通过/);
  });

  it('error 输出 ::error 前缀', function () {
    const r = emptyVerify();
    r.ok = false;
    r.issues = [{ level: 'error', code: 'ORPHAN_START', file: 'a.ts', line: 5, message: '有 START 无 END' }];
    const out = renderGithubAnnotations(r);
    assert.match(out, /^::error file=a\.ts,line=5,title=ORPHAN_START::有 START 无 END/m);
  });

  it('warning 输出 ::warning 前缀', function () {
    const r = emptyVerify();
    r.issues = [{ level: 'warning', code: 'DUP', file: 'b.ts', line: 3, message: '重复锚点' }];
    const out = renderGithubAnnotations(r);
    assert.match(out, /^::warning file=b\.ts,line=3,title=DUP::重复锚点/m);
  });

  it('% 转义为 %25', function () {
    const r = emptyVerify();
    r.issues = [{ level: 'error', code: 'X', file: 'a.ts', line: 1, message: '50% 的代码' }];
    const out = renderGithubAnnotations(r);
    assert.match(out, /50%25 的代码/);
    assert.doesNotMatch(out, /50% 的代码/);
  });

  it('文件路径的 : 和 , 转义', function () {
    const r = emptyVerify();
    r.issues = [{ level: 'error', code: 'X', file: 'a:b,c.ts', line: 1, message: 'x' }];
    const out = renderGithubAnnotations(r);
    assert.match(out, /file=a%3Ab%2Cc\.ts/);
  });

  it('message 里的换行转义为 %0A', function () {
    const r = emptyVerify();
    r.issues = [{ level: 'error', code: 'X', file: 'a.ts', line: 1, message: 'a\nb' }];
    const out = renderGithubAnnotations(r);
    assert.match(out, /a%0Ab/);
  });
});

describe('renderFixGithubAnnotations', function () {
  function emptyFix(): FixReport {
    return { ok: true, dryRun: true, dir: 'src', scannedFiles: 2, files: [], totalActions: 0, remainingOrphanStarts: [] };
  }

  it('无修复：notice', function () {
    const out = renderFixGithubAnnotations(emptyFix());
    assert.match(out, /^::notice title=tred-fix::/);
  });

  it('apply 成功：notice + 文件', function () {
    const r = emptyFix();
    r.dryRun = false;
    r.files = [{ file: 'a.ts', actions: [], diff: '', applied: true }];
    const out = renderFixGithubAnnotations(r);
    assert.match(out, /^::notice file=a\.ts,title=tred-fix::/m);
  });

  it('有 error：::error', function () {
    const r = emptyFix();
    r.files = [{ file: 'bad.ts', actions: [], diff: '', applied: false, error: '语法校验失败' }];
    const out = renderFixGithubAnnotations(r);
    assert.match(out, /^::error file=bad\.ts,title=tred-fix::语法校验失败/m);
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/render-html.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { renderHtml } from '../src/index.js';
import type { VerifyReport } from '../src/index.js';

function emptyReport(): VerifyReport {
  return {
    ok: true, dir: 'src', scannedFiles: 3, files: [],
    issues: [], duplicatesWithin: [], duplicatesAcross: [], fingerprintMismatches: [],
  };
}

describe('renderHtml', function () {
  it('空 report：合法 HTML 结构', function () {
    const html = renderHtml(emptyReport(), { version: '1.1.0' });
    assert.match(html, /^<!DOCTYPE html>/);
    assert.match(html, /<html lang="en">/);
    assert.match(html, /<title>tred verify report<\/title>/);
    assert.match(html, /tred v1\.1\.0/);
    assert.match(html, /✅ All checks passed/);
  });

  it('有 error：显示红色状态', function () {
    const r = emptyReport();
    r.ok = false;
    r.issues = [{ level: 'error', code: 'ORPHAN_START', file: 'a.ts', line: 5, message: '有 START 无 END' }];
    const html = renderHtml(r);
    assert.match(html, /❌ 1 error/);
    assert.match(html, /ORPHAN_START/);
    assert.match(html, /a\.ts:5/);
  });

  it('XSS 防护：message 含 <script> 被转义', function () {
    const r = emptyReport();
    r.issues = [{ level: 'error', code: 'X', file: 'a.ts', line: 1, message: '<script>alert(1)</script>' }];
    const html = renderHtml(r);
    assert.doesNotMatch(html, /<script>alert/);
    assert.match(html, /&lt;script&gt;alert/);
  });

  it('file 路径转义', function () {
    const r = emptyReport();
    r.files = [{
      file: 'a<b>.ts',
      anchors: [{ name: 'X', line: 1, endLine: 2, fp: null }],
      orphans: [],
    }];
    const html = renderHtml(r);
    assert.match(html, /a&lt;b&gt;\.ts/);
    assert.doesNotMatch(html, /a<b>\.ts/);
  });

  it('含 filter 脚本', function () {
    const r = emptyReport();
    r.issues = [{ level: 'error', code: 'X', file: 'a.ts', line: 1, message: 'x' }];
    const html = renderHtml(r);
    assert.match(html, /id="filter"/);
    assert.match(html, /getElementById\("filter"\)/);
  });

  it('无锚点：显示 empty 提示', function () {
    const html = renderHtml(emptyReport());
    assert.match(html, /No anchors found/);
  });

  it('含 details 折叠 + stats 卡片', function () {
    const r = emptyReport();
    r.files = [{
      file: 'a.ts',
      anchors: [{ name: 'A#x', line: 2, endLine: 4, fp: 'abcdef12' }],
      orphans: [],
    }];
    const html = renderHtml(r);
    assert.match(html, /<details>/);
    assert.match(html, /Scanned Files/);
    assert.match(html, /A#x/);
    assert.match(html, /fp=abcdef12/);
  });
});

FILE: test/render-markdown.test.ts

import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { renderFixMarkdown, renderVerifyMarkdown } from '../src/index.js';
import type { FixReport, VerifyReport } from '../src/index.js';

function emptyVerifyReport(): VerifyReport {
  return {
    ok: true, dir: 'src', scannedFiles: 3, files: [],
    issues: [], duplicatesWithin: [], duplicatesAcross: [], fingerprintMismatches: [],
  };
}

describe('renderVerifyMarkdown', function () {
  it('空报告：含标题、✅ 无问题', function () {
    const md = renderVerifyMarkdown(emptyVerifyReport());
    assert.match(md, /^<!-- tred:verify -->/);
    assert.match(md, /## 🔍 tred verify/);
    assert.match(md, /### ✅ 无问题/);
  });

  it('有 error：❌ 未通过 + 表格行', function () {
    const r = emptyVerifyReport();
    r.ok = false;
    r.files = [{
      file: 'a.ts',
      anchors: [{ name: 'A#x', line: 2, endLine: 4, fp: 'a1b2c3d4' }],
      orphans: [{ name: 'A#ghost', line: 5, kind: 'end' }],
    }];
    r.issues = [{
      level: 'error', code: 'ORPHAN_END', file: 'a.ts', line: 5,
      message: '锚点 "A#ghost" 有 END 无 START',
    }];
    const md = renderVerifyMarkdown(r);
    assert.match(md, /### ❌ 未通过（1 个 error，0 个 warning）/);
    assert.match(md, /ORPHAN_END/);
    assert.match(md, /<details><summary>❌/);
  });

  it('| 转义', function () {
    const r = emptyVerifyReport();
    r.ok = false;
    r.issues = [{ level: 'warning', code: 'X', message: 'a | b' }];
    const md = renderVerifyMarkdown(r);
    assert.match(md, /a \\\| b/);
  });
});

describe('renderFixMarkdown', function () {
  function emptyFixReport(): FixReport {
    return {
      ok: true, dryRun: true, dir: 'src', scannedFiles: 2,
      files: [], totalActions: 0, remainingOrphanStarts: [],
    };
  }

  it('dry-run 无修复：✅ 无需修复', function () {
    const md = renderFixMarkdown(emptyFixReport());
    assert.match(md, /^<!-- tred:verify-fix -->/);
    assert.match(md, /\(dry-run\)/);
    assert.match(md, /### ✅ 无需修复/);
  });

  it('apply 有修复', function () {
    const r = emptyFixReport();
    r.dryRun = false;
    r.totalActions = 1;
    r.files = [{
      file: 'a.ts',
      actions: [{ kind: 'delete-orphan-end', line: 5, anchorName: 'ghost', description: '删除孤儿 END: "ghost" @ L5' }],
      diff: '', applied: true,
    }];
    const md = renderFixMarkdown(r);
    assert.match(md, /\(--apply\)/);
    assert.match(md, /### 修复结果 \(1 处\)/);
    assert.match(md, /- L5 — 删除孤儿 END/);
  });

  it('remainingOrphanStarts 警告', function () {
    const r = emptyFixReport();
    r.totalActions = 1;
    r.files = [{
      file: 'a.ts',
      actions: [{ kind: 'delete-orphan-end', line: 5, anchorName: 'g', description: '删 g' }],
      diff: '', applied: true,
    }];
    r.remainingOrphanStarts = [{ file: 'b.ts', line: 3, anchorName: 'Bar#y', kind: 'start' }];
    const md = renderFixMarkdown(r);
    assert.match(md, /### ⚠ 需手动处理 \(1 处孤儿 START\)/);
    assert.match(md, /tred replace --auto-inject --selector/);
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/render-sarif.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { renderSarif } from '../src/index.js';
import type { VerifyReport } from '../src/index.js';

function emptyReport(): VerifyReport {
  return {
    ok: true, dir: 'src', scannedFiles: 3, files: [],
    issues: [], duplicatesWithin: [], duplicatesAcross: [], fingerprintMismatches: [],
  };
}

describe('renderSarif', function () {
  it('空 report：合法 SARIF 2.1.0，results=[]', function () {
    const sarif = JSON.parse(renderSarif(emptyReport()));
    assert.equal(sarif.version, '2.1.0');
    assert.equal(sarif.$schema, 'https://json.schemastore.org/sarif-2.1.0.json');
    assert.equal(sarif.runs.length, 1);
    assert.equal(sarif.runs[0].results.length, 0);
    assert.equal(sarif.runs[0].tool.driver.name, 'tred');
  });

  it('有 issue：results 含 ruleId + level + locations', function () {
    const r = emptyReport();
    r.ok = false;
    r.issues = [{ level: 'error', code: 'ORPHAN_START', file: 'a.ts', line: 5, message: '有 START 无 END' }];
    const sarif = JSON.parse(renderSarif(r, { version: '1.1.0' }));
    const run = sarif.runs[0];
    assert.equal(run.tool.driver.version, '1.1.0');
    assert.equal(run.results.length, 1);
    const res = run.results[0];
    assert.equal(res.ruleId, 'TRED_ORPHAN_START');
    assert.equal(res.level, 'error');
    assert.equal(res.message.text, '有 START 无 END');
    assert.equal(res.locations[0].physicalLocation.artifactLocation.uri, 'a.ts');
    assert.equal(res.locations[0].physicalLocation.region.startLine, 5);
  });

  it('rules 只包含用到的 code', function () {
    const r = emptyReport();
    r.issues = [
      { level: 'error', code: 'ORPHAN_START', file: 'a.ts', line: 1, message: 'x' },
      { level: 'warning', code: 'DUPLICATE_ACROSS', message: 'y' },
    ];
    const sarif = JSON.parse(renderSarif(r));
    const ruleIds = sarif.runs[0].tool.driver.rules.map((x: { id: string }) => x.id);
    assert.ok(ruleIds.indexOf('TRED_ORPHAN_START') >= 0);
    assert.ok(ruleIds.indexOf('TRED_DUPLICATE_ACROSS') >= 0);
    assert.equal(ruleIds.indexOf('TRED_FP_MISMATCH'), -1);
  });

  it('无 file/line 的 issue 不崩', function () {
    const r = emptyReport();
    r.issues = [{ level: 'warning', code: 'DUPLICATE_ACROSS', message: 'no location' }];
    const sarif = JSON.parse(renderSarif(r));
    assert.equal(sarif.runs[0].results[0].locations[0].physicalLocation.artifactLocation.uri, '');
  });

  it('JSON 可序列化 + informationUri 可配', function () {
    const sarif = JSON.parse(renderSarif(emptyReport(), { informationUri: 'https://example.com/tred' }));
    assert.equal(sarif.runs[0].tool.driver.informationUri, 'https://example.com/tred');
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/replace-many.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdtempSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { makeFingerprint, replaceMany } from '../src/index.js';

const CLASS_SRC = [
  'export class A {',
  '  foo(): void {}',
  '}',
  '',
].join('\n');

describe('replaceMany', function () {
  let dir: string;
  let f1: string;
  let f2: string;

  function setup(): void {
    dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-batch-'));
    f1 = join(dir, 'a.ts');
    f2 = join(dir, 'b.ts');
    writeFileSync(f1, CLASS_SRC);
    writeFileSync(f2, CLASS_SRC);
  }

  function cleanup(): void { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }

  const sel = { kind: 'method' as const, className: 'A', methodName: 'foo' };

  it('全部成功 → committed，两个文件都更新', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const fp1 = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#foo', source: 'b', version: '1' });
      const fp2 = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#foo', source: 'b', version: '2' });
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#foo', content: 'foo(): void { console.log(1); }', fingerprint: fp1, autoInjectAnchor: true, astSelector: sel },
        { file: f2, anchor: 'A#foo', content: 'foo(): void { console.log(2); }', fingerprint: fp2, autoInjectAnchor: true, astSelector: sel },
      ]);
      assert.equal(r.status, 'committed');
      assert.equal(r.committedFiles.length, 2);
      assert.match(readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8'), /console\.log\(1\)/);
      assert.match(readFileSync(f2, 'utf-8'), /console\.log\(2\)/);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });

  it('中途语法错 → rolled-back，所有文件不变', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const before1 = readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8');
      const before2 = readFileSync(f2, 'utf-8');
      const fp1 = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#foo', source: 'b', version: '1' });
      const fp2 = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#foo', source: 'b', version: '2' });
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#foo', content: 'foo(): void { console.log(1); }', fingerprint: fp1, autoInjectAnchor: true, astSelector: sel },
        { file: f2, anchor: 'A#foo', content: 'foo(): void { console.log(2)', fingerprint: fp2, autoInjectAnchor: true, astSelector: sel },
      ]);
      assert.equal(r.status, 'rolled-back');
      assert.equal(readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8'), before1);
      assert.equal(readFileSync(f2, 'utf-8'), before2);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });

  it('同一文件多个锚点 → 串行计算', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const fp1 = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'X1', source: 'b', version: '1' });
      const fp2 = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'X2', source: 'b', version: '1' });
      writeFileSync(f1, [
        'export class A {',
        '  // @TRED_START: X1 | fp=00000000',
        '  foo(): void {}',
        '  // @TRED_END: X1',
        '  // @TRED_START: X2 | fp=00000000',
        '  bar(): void {}',
        '  // @TRED_END: X2',
        '}',
        '',
      ].join('\n'));
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'X1', content: 'foo(): void { console.log(1); }', fingerprint: fp1 },
        { file: f1, anchor: 'X2', content: 'bar(): void { console.log(2); }', fingerprint: fp2 },
      ]);
      assert.equal(r.status, 'committed');
      const after = readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8');
      assert.match(after, /console\.log\(1\)/);
      assert.match(after, /console\.log\(2\)/);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });

  it('空数组 → committed', async function () {
    const r = await replaceMany([]);
    assert.equal(r.status, 'committed');
  });

  it('文件不存在 → rolled-back', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'X', content: 'y', fingerprint: '00000000' },
        { file: join(dir, 'missing.ts'), anchor: 'X', content: 'y', fingerprint: '00000000' },
      ]);
      assert.equal(r.status, 'rolled-back');
      assert.match(r.results[1].message || '', /文件不存在/);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });

  it('dryRun → 不写盘，status = dry-run', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const before = readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8');
      const fp1 = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#foo', source: 'b', version: '1' });
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#foo', content: 'foo(): void { console.log(1); }', fingerprint: fp1, autoInjectAnchor: true, astSelector: sel, dryRun: true },
      ]);
      assert.equal(r.status, 'dry-run');
      assert.equal(readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8'), before);
      assert.ok(r.results[0].diff && r.results[0].diff.includes('+'));
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });
});


describe('replaceMany continueOnError', function () {
  let dir: string;
  let f1: string;
  let f2: string;

  function setup(): void {
    dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-partial-'));
    f1 = join(dir, 'a.ts');
    f2 = join(dir, 'b.ts');
    const src = [
      'export class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=00000000',
      '  x(): void {}',
      '  // @TRED_END: A#x',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n');
    writeFileSync(f1, src);
    writeFileSync(f2, src);
  }

  function cleanup(): void { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }

  const GOOD = 'x(): void { console.log(1); }';
  const BAD = 'x(): void { console.log(1)';  // 缺 }

  it('单文件失败，其余文件正常提交 → partial', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'p', version: '1' });
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: GOOD, fingerprint: fp },
        { file: f2, anchor: 'A#x', content: BAD, fingerprint: fp },
      ], { continueOnError: true });
      assert.equal(r.status, 'partial');
      assert.equal(r.committedFiles.length, 1);
      assert.equal(r.committedFiles[0], f1);
      assert.match(readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8'), /console\.log\(1\)/);
      // f2 未写
      assert.match(readFileSync(f2, 'utf-8'), /fp=00000000/);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });

  it('全部成功 → committed', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'p', version: '1' });
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: GOOD, fingerprint: fp },
        { file: f2, anchor: 'A#x', content: GOOD, fingerprint: fp },
      ], { continueOnError: true });
      assert.equal(r.status, 'committed');
      assert.equal(r.committedFiles.length, 2);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });

  it('全部失败 → rolled-back', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'p', version: '1' });
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: BAD, fingerprint: fp },
        { file: f2, anchor: 'A#x', content: BAD, fingerprint: fp },
      ], { continueOnError: true });
      assert.equal(r.status, 'rolled-back');
      assert.equal(r.committedFiles.length, 0);
      // 两文件原样
      assert.match(readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8'), /fp=00000000/);
      assert.match(readFileSync(f2, 'utf-8'), /fp=00000000/);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });

  it('同文件多 op 第 2 个失败 → 该文件 skip，第 1 op 改 failed', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      // 给 f1 塞两个锚点
      writeFileSync(f1, [
        'export class A {',
        '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=00000000',
        '  x(): void {}',
        '  // @TRED_END: A#x',
        '  // @TRED_START: A#y | fp=00000000',
        '  y(): void {}',
        '  // @TRED_END: A#y',
        '}',
        '',
      ].join('\n'));
      const fp1 = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'p', version: '1' });
      const fp2 = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#y', source: 'p', version: '1' });
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: 'x(): void { console.log(1); }', fingerprint: fp1 },
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#y', content: BAD, fingerprint: fp2 },
      ], { continueOnError: true });
      assert.equal(r.status, 'rolled-back');  // 只有这一个文件且失败
      // 第 1 个 op 应被改 failed（因为文件最终没写）
      assert.equal(r.results[0].status, 'failed');
      assert.equal(r.results[1].status, 'failed');
      // 文件原样
      assert.match(readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8'), /fp=00000000/);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });

  it('提交阶段失败不影响其他文件 → partial', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'p', version: '1' });
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: GOOD, fingerprint: fp },
        { file: f2, anchor: 'A#x', content: GOOD, fingerprint: fp },
      ], {
        continueOnError: true,
        _writer: function (file, content) {
          if (file === f2) throw new Error('disk full on f2');
          writeFileSync(file, content, 'utf-8');
        },
      });
      assert.equal(r.status, 'partial');
      assert.equal(r.committedFiles.length, 1);
      assert.equal(r.committedFiles[0], f1);
      // f1 写了，f2 没写
      assert.match(readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8'), /console\.log\(1\)/);
      assert.match(readFileSync(f2, 'utf-8'), /fp=00000000/);
      // f2 的 op 在 results 里标 failed
      const f2Result = r.results.find((x) => x.file === f2);
      assert.equal(f2Result?.status, 'failed');
      assert.match(f2Result?.message || '', /提交失败/);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });

  it('missing 文件不阻塞其他文件 → partial', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'p', version: '1' });
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: GOOD, fingerprint: fp },
        { file: join(dir, 'missing.ts'), anchor: 'A#x', content: GOOD, fingerprint: fp },
      ], { continueOnError: true });
      assert.equal(r.status, 'partial');
      assert.equal(r.committedFiles.length, 1);
      assert.match(readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8'), /console\.log\(1\)/);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });

  it('continueOnError + dryRun → dry-run，不写盘', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'p', version: '1' });
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: GOOD, fingerprint: fp, dryRun: true },
        { file: f2, anchor: 'A#x', content: BAD, fingerprint: fp, dryRun: true },
      ], { continueOnError: true });
      assert.equal(r.status, 'dry-run');
      assert.match(readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8'), /fp=00000000/);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });

  it('未传 continueOnError → 保持原有全事务回滚', async function () {
    setup();
    try {
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'p', version: '1' });
      const r = await replaceMany([
        { file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: GOOD, fingerprint: fp },
        { file: f2, anchor: 'A#x', content: BAD, fingerprint: fp },
      ]);
      assert.equal(r.status, 'rolled-back');
      assert.equal(r.committedFiles.length, 0);
      assert.match(readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8'), /fp=00000000/);
    } finally { cleanup(); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/rollback-log.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { makeFingerprint, replaceMany, writeRollbackLog } from '../src/index.js';
import type { RollbackLog } from '../src/index.js';

function makeSetup() {
  const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-rl-'));
  const f1 = join(dir, 'a.ts');
  const f2 = join(dir, 'b.ts');
  const src = [
    'export class A {',
    '  // @TRED_START: A#x | fp=00000000',
    '  x(): void {}',
    '  // @TRED_END: A#x',
    '}',
    '',
  ].join('\n');
  writeFileSync(f1, src);
  writeFileSync(f2, src);
  return { dir, f1, f2 };
}

describe('writeRollbackLog 纯函数', function () {
  it('写出合法 JSON，结构完整', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-rlw-'));
    const logPath = join(dir, 'nested/rl.json');
    try {
      const log: RollbackLog = {
        timestamp: '2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z',
        status: 'rolled-back',
        error: 'disk full',
        commitPhase: {
          attempted: ['a.ts', 'b.ts'],
          committed: ['a.ts'],
          failed: 'b.ts',
          rollbackErrors: [{ file: 'a.ts', message: 'permission denied' }],
        },
        ops: [],
      };
      writeRollbackLog(log, logPath);
      const parsed = JSON.parse(readFileSync(logPath, 'utf-8'));
      assert.equal(parsed.status, 'rolled-back');
      assert.equal(parsed.error, 'disk full');
      assert.deepEqual(parsed.commitPhase.attempted, ['a.ts', 'b.ts']);
      assert.deepEqual(parsed.commitPhase.committed, ['a.ts']);
      assert.equal(parsed.commitPhase.failed, 'b.ts');
      assert.equal(parsed.commitPhase.rollbackErrors.length, 1);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});

describe('replaceMany + rollbackLog 集成', function () {
  it('提交成功：不写日志（即使传了路径）', async function () {
    const { dir, f1 } = makeSetup();
    const logPath = join(dir, "rollback.json");
    try {
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'rl', version: '1' });
      const r = await replaceMany(
        [{ file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: 'x(): void { console.log(1); }', fingerprint: fp }],
        { rollbackLog: logPath },
      );
      assert.equal(r.status, 'committed');
      assert.throws(
        () => readFileSync(logPath, "utf-8"),
        /ENOENT/,
        "提交成功不应写 rollback log",
      );
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('提交失败：写日志，含 attempted/committed/failed', async function () {
    const { dir, f1, f2 } = makeSetup();
    const logPath = join(dir, "rollback.json");
    try {
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'rl', version: '1' });
      // 第一个文件写成功，第二个抛错
      const r = await replaceMany(
        [
          { file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: 'x(): void { console.log(1); }', fingerprint: fp },
          { file: f2, anchor: 'A#x', content: 'x(): void { console.log(2); }', fingerprint: fp },
        ],
        {
          rollbackLog: logPath,
          _writer: function (file, content) {
            if (file === f2) throw new Error('boom on f2');
            writeFileSync(file, content, "utf-8");
          },
        },
      );
      assert.equal(r.status, 'rolled-back');
      assert.match(r.error || '', /boom on f2/);
      const log: RollbackLog = JSON.parse(readFileSync(logPath, 'utf-8'));
      assert.equal(log.status, 'rolled-back');
      assert.match(log.error, /boom on f2/);
      assert.equal(log.commitPhase.attempted.length, 2);
      assert.equal(log.commitPhase.committed.length, 1);
      assert.equal(log.commitPhase.committed[0], f1);
      assert.equal(log.commitPhase.failed, f2);
      assert.equal(log.commitPhase.rollbackErrors.length, 0);
      assert.equal(log.ops.length, 2);
      // f1 文件内容已被回滚到原状
      const after = readFileSync(f1, 'utf-8');
      assert.doesNotMatch(after, /console\.log\(1\)/);
      assert.match(after, /fp=00000000/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('回滚本身失败：记录 rollbackErrors', async function () {
    const { dir, f1, f2 } = makeSetup();
    const logPath = join(dir, "rollback.json");
    let calls = 0;
    try {
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'rl', version: '1' });
      const r = await replaceMany(
        [
          { file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: 'x(): void { console.log(1); }', fingerprint: fp },
          { file: f2, anchor: 'A#x', content: 'x(): void { console.log(2); }', fingerprint: fp },
        ],
        {
          rollbackLog: logPath,
          _writer: function (file, content) {
            calls++;
            // 前 1 次成功（f1 写入），第 2 次触发 f2 错误，第 3 次（回滚 f1）也失败
            if (calls === 1) { writeFileSync(file, content, "utf-8"); return; }
            if (calls === 2) throw new Error('commit failed');
            throw new Error('rollback failed');
          },
        },
      );
      assert.equal(r.status, 'rolled-back');
      const log: RollbackLog = JSON.parse(readFileSync(logPath, 'utf-8'));
      assert.equal(log.commitPhase.rollbackErrors.length, 1);
      assert.equal(log.commitPhase.rollbackErrors[0].file, f1);
      assert.match(log.commitPhase.rollbackErrors[0].message, /rollback failed/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('写日志本身失败：原错误仍保留，附加日志错误', async function () {
    const { dir, f1, f2 } = makeSetup();
    const logPath = join(dir, "no-such-dir/rollback.json");
    try {
      // 先创建 no-such-dir 再 chmod 不可写？跨平台不稳，改用路径是目录来触发
      const fp = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'A#x', source: 'rl', version: '1' });
      // 让 logPath 指向一个目录（atomicWrite 的 mkdir 会因目标已是目录而失败？不一定）
      // 更稳的方案：让 writer 在第二个文件失败，logPath 指向一个文件（非目录）但被占用
      // 简化：writer 失败导致 rollback log 写入尝试用 atomicWrite，正常应成功
      // 这里只验证：即使 rollbackLog 路径非法，也不抛异常，原错误仍返回
      const r = await replaceMany(
        [
          { file: f1, anchor: 'A#x', content: 'x(): void { console.log(1); }', fingerprint: fp },
          { file: f2, anchor: 'A#x', content: 'x(): void { console.log(2); }', fingerprint: fp },
        ],
        {
          rollbackLog: "/dev/null/not/a/file.json",
          _writer: function (file, content) {
            if (file === f2) throw new Error('boom');
            writeFileSync(file, content, "utf-8");
          },
        },
      );
      assert.equal(r.status, 'rolled-back');
      // 原错误仍是主导，日志错误附加在后
      assert.match(r.error || '', /boom/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/serve.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { serveHtml } from '../src/index.js';

describe('serveHtml', function () {
  it('返回当前 HTML', async function () {
    let html = '<h1>v1</h1>';
    const handle = await serveHtml(() => html, { port: 0 });
    try {
      const r = await fetch(handle.url);
      assert.equal(r.status, 200);
      assert.match(r.headers.get('content-type') || '', /text\/html/);
      const body = await r.text();
      assert.equal(body, '<h1>v1</h1>');
    } finally {
      handle.close();
    }
  });

  it('getHtml 动态更新：二次请求返回新内容', async function () {
    let html = '<h1>first</h1>';
    const handle = await serveHtml(() => html, { port: 0 });
    try {
      const r1 = await fetch(handle.url);
      assert.equal(await r1.text(), '<h1>first</h1>');

      html = '<h1>second</h1>';
      const r2 = await fetch(handle.url);
      assert.equal(await r2.text(), '<h1>second</h1>');
    } finally {
      handle.close();
    }
  });

  it('favicon.ico 返回 204', async function () {
    const handle = await serveHtml(() => '<html></html>', { port: 0 });
    try {
      const r = await fetch(handle.url + '/favicon.ico');
      assert.equal(r.status, 204);
    } finally {
      handle.close();
    }
  });

  it('getHtml 抛异常时返回 500', async function () {
    const handle = await serveHtml(
      () => { throw new Error('boom'); },
      { port: 0 },
    );
    try {
      const r = await fetch(handle.url);
      assert.equal(r.status, 500);
      assert.match(await r.text(), /tred serve error: boom/);
    } finally {
      handle.close();
    }
  });

  it('port:0 自动分配且 url 含实际端口', async function () {
    const handle = await serveHtml(() => 'ok', { port: 0 });
    try {
      assert.ok(handle.port > 0);
      assert.match(handle.url, /^http:\/\/localhost:\d+$/);
    } finally {
      handle.close();
    }
  });

  it('port 已被占用时 reject EADDRINUSE', async function () {
    const h1 = await serveHtml(() => 'first', { port: 0 });
    try {
      await assert.rejects(
        serveHtml(() => 'second', { port: h1.port }),
        (err: unknown) => {
          const e = err as { code?: string; message?: string };
          return e.code === 'EADDRINUSE' || e.code === 'EACCES' || /EADDR/i.test(String(e.message || ''));
        },
      );
    } finally {
      h1.close();
    }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/since-file.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { verifyDir, annotateDir } from '../src/index.js';

describe('verifyDir --sinceFile', function () {
  it('只扫 sinceFile 列出的文件', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-sf-'));
    try {
      mkdirSync(join(dir, 'src'), { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'src/a.ts'), '// @TRED_START: A | fp=11111111\n// @TRED_END: A\n');
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'src/b.ts'), '// @TRED_START: B | fp=22222222\n// @TRED_END: B\n');
      const listPath = join(dir, '_list.txt');
      writeFileSync(listPath, 'src/a.ts\n');
      const r = verifyDir({ dir, sinceFile: listPath } as any);
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 1);
      assert.match(r.files[0].file, /a\.ts$/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('sinceFile 不存在：不崩溃，全量扫', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-sf-'));
    try {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), '// @TRED_START: A | fp=11111111\n// @TRED_END: A\n');
      const r = verifyDir({ dir, sinceFile: '/no/such/list.txt' } as any);
      // 应该 fallback 到全量（sinceSet 为空）
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 1);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('空文件列表：处理 0 文件', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-sf-'));
    try {
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'a.ts'), '// @TRED_START: A | fp=11111111\n// @TRED_END: A\n');
      const listPath = join(dir, '_empty.txt');
      writeFileSync(listPath, '');
      const r = verifyDir({ dir, sinceFile: listPath } as any);
      // 空 set 会过滤掉所有文件
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 0);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});

describe('annotateDir --sinceFile', function () {
  it('只处理 sinceFile 列出的文件', function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-sf-a-'));
    try {
      mkdirSync(join(dir, 'src'), { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'src/a.ts'), 'export class Foo {}\n');
      writeFileSync(join(dir, 'src/b.ts'), 'export class Bar {}\n');
      const listPath = join(dir, '_list.txt');
      writeFileSync(listPath, 'src/a.ts\n');
      const r = annotateDir({ dir, classes: true, sinceFile: listPath } as any);
      assert.equal(r.annotatedFiles, 1);
      assert.match(r.files[0].file, /a\.ts$/);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});


================================================================================
FILE: test/tred.test.ts
================================================================================
import { describe, it, beforeEach, after } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';

import { makeFingerprint, replace, normalizeContent } from '../src/index.js';

const ORIGINAL = [
  'export class GraphEngine {',
  '  loadUnlocked(): void {',
  "    console.log('old');",
  '  }',
  '}',
  '',
].join('\n');

const METHOD_NEW = [
  'loadUnlocked(): void {',
  "  console.log('new');",
  '}',
].join('\n');

// 故意写成"绝对缩进"（body 4 空格、} 2 空格），验证 #6 归一化
const METHOD_ABS_INDENT = [
  'loadUnlocked(): void {',
  "    console.log('new');",
  '  }',
].join('\n');

describe('tred', function () {
  let dir: string;
  let file: string;

  beforeEach(function () {
    dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-'));
    file = join(dir, 'main.ts');
    writeFileSync(file, ORIGINAL);
  });

  after(function () {
    try {
      rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  });

  const selector = {
    kind: 'method' as const,
    className: 'GraphEngine',
    methodName: 'loadUnlocked',
  };

  it('AST 首插：无锚点时注入 START/END + fp', async function () {
    const fingerprint = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'GraphEngine#load', source: 'unit-test', version: '1' });
    const r = await replace({
      file,
      anchor: 'GraphEngine#load',
      content: METHOD_NEW,
      fingerprint,
      autoInjectAnchor: true,
      astSelector: selector,
    });
    assert.equal(r.status, 'injected');
    const src = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
    assert.match(src, /@TRED_START: GraphEngine#load/);
    assert.ok(src.includes('fp=' + fingerprint));
    assert.match(src, /@TRED_END: GraphEngine#load/);
    assert.match(src, /console\.log\('new'\)/);
    assert.doesNotMatch(src, /console\.log\('old'\)/);
  });

  it('AST 首插：START / END / 方法行缩进一致，不翻倍', async function () {
    const fingerprint = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'GraphEngine#load', source: 'indent-test', version: '1' });
    await replace({
      file,
      anchor: 'GraphEngine#load',
      content: METHOD_NEW,
      fingerprint,
      autoInjectAnchor: true,
      astSelector: selector,
    });
    const src = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
    const lines = src.split('\n');
    const lead = function (s: string) {
      const m = s.match(/^[\t ]*/);
      return m ? m[0] : '';
    };
    const startLine = lines.find(function (l) { return l.includes('@TRED_START'); });
    const endLine = lines.find(function (l) { return l.includes('@TRED_END'); });
    const methodLine = lines.find(function (l) { return l.includes('loadUnlocked(): void'); });
    assert.ok(startLine && endLine && methodLine);
    assert.equal(lead(startLine), '  ');
    assert.equal(lead(endLine), '  ');
    assert.equal(lead(methodLine), '  ');
  });

  it('锚点替换：已有锚点时直接替换内部内容', async function () {
    writeFileSync(file, [
      'export class GraphEngine {',
      '  // @TRED_START: GraphEngine#load | fp=00000000',
      '  loadUnlocked(): void {',
      "    console.log('old');",
      '  }',
      '  // @TRED_END: GraphEngine#load',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n'));
    const fingerprint = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'GraphEngine#load', source: 'unit-test', version: '2' });
    const r = await replace({
      file,
      anchor: 'GraphEngine#load',
      content: METHOD_NEW,
      fingerprint,
    });
    assert.equal(r.status, 'replaced');
    const src = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
    assert.ok(src.includes('fp=' + fingerprint));
    assert.match(src, /console\.log\('new'\)/);
    assert.doesNotMatch(src, /console\.log\('old'\)/);
  });

  it('幂等：指纹一致时跳过，不重复注入', async function () {
    const fingerprint = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'GraphEngine#load', source: 'unit-test', version: '3' });
    const opts = {
      file,
      anchor: 'GraphEngine#load',
      content: METHOD_NEW,
      fingerprint,
      autoInjectAnchor: true,
      astSelector: selector,
    };
    const first = await replace(opts);
    assert.equal(first.status, 'injected');
    const afterFirst = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
    const second = await replace(opts);
    assert.equal(second.status, 'skipped');
    assert.equal(readFileSync(file, 'utf-8'), afterFirst);
  });

  it('语法错误：parseDiagnostics 非空则拒绝写入，原文件保持不变', async function () {
    const fingerprint = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'GraphEngine#load', source: 'unit-test', version: '4' });
    const before = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
    const r = await replace({
      file,
      anchor: 'GraphEngine#load',
      content: [
        'loadUnlocked(): void {',
        "  console.log('boom')",
      ].join('\n'),
      fingerprint,
      autoInjectAnchor: true,
      astSelector: selector,
    });
    assert.equal(r.status, 'failed');
    assert.ok(r.syntaxErrors && r.syntaxErrors.length > 0);
    assert.equal(readFileSync(file, 'utf-8'), before);
  });

  // ────────────── v0.2 新增 ──────────────

  it('#6 缩进归一化：绝对缩进 content 自动转相对缩进', async function () {
    const fingerprint = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'GraphEngine#load', source: 'normalize', version: '1' });
    await replace({
      file,
      anchor: 'GraphEngine#load',
      content: METHOD_ABS_INDENT,
      fingerprint,
      autoInjectAnchor: true,
      astSelector: selector,
    });
    const src = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
    // 类内方法体缩进 = 2 (class) + 2 (body) = 4 空格；6 空格才是归一化失败的翻倍
    assert.match(src, /^ {4}console\.log\('new'\);$/m);
    assert.doesNotMatch(src, /^ {6}console\.log\('new'\);$/m);
  });

  it('#3 重复锚点：同名 START 出现两次时拒绝处理', async function () {
    writeFileSync(file, [
      'export class A {',
      '  // @TRED_START: X | fp=11111111',
      '  foo() {}',
      '  // @TRED_END: X',
      '}',
      'export class B {',
      '  // @TRED_START: X | fp=22222222',
      '  bar() {}',
      '  // @TRED_END: X',
      '}',
      '',
    ].join('\n'));
    const before = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
    const r = await replace({
      file,
      anchor: 'X',
      content: 'baz() {}',
      fingerprint: 'deadbeef',
    });
    assert.equal(r.status, 'failed');
    assert.match(r.message || '', /出现 2 次/);
    assert.equal(readFileSync(file, 'utf-8'), before);
  });

  it('#2 dry-run：不写盘且返回 diff', async function () {
    const fingerprint = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'GraphEngine#load', source: 'dry', version: '1' });
    const before = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
    const r = await replace({
      file,
      anchor: 'GraphEngine#load',
      content: METHOD_NEW,
      fingerprint,
      autoInjectAnchor: true,
      astSelector: selector,
      dryRun: true,
    });
    assert.equal(r.status, 'injected');
    assert.equal(readFileSync(file, 'utf-8'), before);
    assert.ok(r.diff && r.diff.includes('@TRED_START'));
    assert.ok(r.diff.includes('+') && r.diff.includes('-'));
  });

  it('#4 format：formatter 在语法门禁前执行', async function () {
    const fingerprint = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'GraphEngine#load', source: 'fmt', version: '1' });
    const r = await replace({
      file,
      anchor: 'GraphEngine#load',
      content: METHOD_NEW,
      fingerprint,
      autoInjectAnchor: true,
      astSelector: selector,
      format: function (s) {
        return s.replace("console.log('new')", "console.log('formatted')");
      },
    });
    assert.equal(r.status, 'injected');
    const src = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
    assert.match(src, /console\.log\('formatted'\)/);
    assert.doesNotMatch(src, /console\.log\('new'\)/);
  });
});

describe('normalizeContent', function () {
  it('绝对缩进 → 相对首行', function () {
    const input = [
      'foo() {',
      "    console.log('x');",
      '  }',
    ].join('\n');
    const expected = [
      'foo() {',
      "  console.log('x');",
      '}',
    ].join('\n');
    assert.equal(normalizeContent(input), expected);
  });

  it('已经相对缩进 → 原样返回', function () {
    const input = [
      'foo() {',
      "  console.log('x');",
      '}',
    ].join('\n');
    assert.equal(normalizeContent(input), input);
  });
});


describe('buildBlock trailing newline', function () {
  it('content 末尾多余换行：END 前不产生空行', async function () {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-trim-'));
    const file = join(dir, 'main.ts');
    writeFileSync(
      file,
      [
        'export class GraphEngine {',
        '  loadUnlocked(): void {',
        "    console.log('old');",
        '  }',
        '}',
        '',
      ].join('\n'),
    );
    try {
      const fingerprint = makeFingerprint({ anchor: 'GraphEngine#load', source: 'trim', version: '1' });
      await replace({
        file,
        anchor: 'GraphEngine#load',
        content: [
          'loadUnlocked(): void {',
          "  console.log('new');",
          '}',
          '',
          '',
        ].join('\n'),
        fingerprint,
        autoInjectAnchor: true,
        astSelector: { kind: 'method', className: 'GraphEngine', methodName: 'loadUnlocked' },
      });
      const src = readFileSync(file, 'utf-8');
      assert.doesNotMatch(src, /\n[ \t]*\n[ \t]*\/\/ @TRED_END/);
      assert.match(src, /\n  \/\/ @TRED_END: GraphEngine#load\n/);
    } finally {
      rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
  });
});

import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
import { strict as assert } from 'node:assert';
import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, rmSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { scanFileAnchors, verifyDir } from '../src/index.js';

describe('scanFileAnchors', function () {
  it('配对 + fp 提取', function () {
    const src = [
      '// @TRED_START: A | fp=12345678',
      'body',
      '// @TRED_END: A',
      '// @TRED_START: B',
      'body',
      '// @TRED_END: B',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = scanFileAnchors(src);
    assert.equal(r.anchors.length, 2);
    assert.equal(r.orphans.length, 0);
    assert.equal(r.anchors[0].name, 'A');
    assert.equal(r.anchors[0].fp, '12345678');
    assert.equal(r.anchors[0].line, 1);
    assert.equal(r.anchors[0].endLine, 3);
    assert.equal(r.anchors[1].fp, null);
  });

  it('孤儿 START', function () {
    const r = scanFileAnchors('// @TRED_START: X\nbody\n');
    assert.equal(r.anchors.length, 0);
    assert.equal(r.orphans.length, 1);
    assert.equal(r.orphans[0].kind, 'start');
    assert.equal(r.orphans[0].line, 1);
  });

  it('孤儿 END', function () {
    const r = scanFileAnchors('// @TRED_END: X\n');
    assert.equal(r.anchors.length, 0);
    assert.equal(r.orphans.length, 1);
    assert.equal(r.orphans[0].kind, 'end');
  });

  it('同名多块 FIFO 配对', function () {
    const src = [
      '// @TRED_START: X | fp=11111111',
      '// @TRED_END: X',
      '// @TRED_START: X | fp=22222222',
      '// @TRED_END: X',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = scanFileAnchors(src);
    assert.equal(r.anchors.length, 2);
    assert.equal(r.anchors[0].fp, '11111111');
    assert.equal(r.anchors[1].fp, '22222222');
  });

  it('嵌套不同名锚点', function () {
    const src = [
      '// @TRED_START: OUTER',
      '// @TRED_START: INNER',
      '// @TRED_END: INNER',
      '// @TRED_END: OUTER',
    ].join('\n');
    const r = scanFileAnchors(src);
    assert.equal(r.anchors.length, 2);
    assert.equal(r.orphans.length, 0);
  });
});

describe('verifyDir', function () {
  function setup(files: Record<string, string>): string {
    const dir = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'tred-verify-'));
    for (const [rel, content] of Object.entries(files)) {
      const full = join(dir, rel);
      mkdirSync(join(full, '..'), { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(full, content);
    }
    return dir;
  }

  it('健康项目 ok=true', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': '// @TRED_START: X | fp=a1b2c3d4\n// @TRED_END: X\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = verifyDir({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r.issues.length, 0);
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 1);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('孤儿 START → error，ok=false', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': '// @TRED_START: X\nbody\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = verifyDir({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
      assert.ok(r.issues.some(function (i) { return i.code === 'ORPHAN_START'; }));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('同文件重复 → error', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': [
        '// @TRED_START: X | fp=11111111',
        '// @TRED_END: X',
        '// @TRED_START: X | fp=22222222',
        '// @TRED_END: X',
      ].join('\n'),
    });
    try {
      const r = verifyDir({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
      assert.equal(r.duplicatesWithin.length, 1);
      assert.ok(r.issues.some(function (i) { return i.code === 'DUPLICATE_WITHIN'; }));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('跨文件重复 → warning，非 strict 下 ok=true', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': '// @TRED_START: X | fp=aaaaaaaa\n// @TRED_END: X\n',
      'b.ts': '// @TRED_START: X | fp=aaaaaaaa\n// @TRED_END: X\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = verifyDir({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r.duplicatesAcross.length, 1);
      assert.ok(r.issues.some(function (i) { return i.code === 'DUPLICATE_ACROSS' && i.level === 'warning'; }));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('strict 模式下跨文件重复 → ok=false', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': '// @TRED_START: X | fp=aaaaaaaa\n// @TRED_END: X\n',
      'b.ts': '// @TRED_START: X | fp=aaaaaaaa\n// @TRED_END: X\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = verifyDir({ dir, strict: true });
      assert.equal(r.ok, false);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('跨文件指纹不一致 → warning + fingerprintMismatches', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': '// @TRED_START: X | fp=aaaaaaaa\n// @TRED_END: X\n',
      'b.ts': '// @TRED_START: X | fp=bbbbbbbb\n// @TRED_END: X\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = verifyDir({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.fingerprintMismatches.length, 1);
      assert.ok(r.issues.some(function (i) { return i.code === 'FP_MISMATCH'; }));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('忽略 node_modules / .git / dist', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'node_modules/skip.ts': '// @TRED_START: X\n',
      'dist/skip.ts': '// @TRED_END: Y\n',
      'src/ok.ts': '// @TRED_START: OK | fp=a1b2c3d4\n// @TRED_END: OK\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = verifyDir({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.ok, true);
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 1);
      assert.equal(r.files[0].file, join('src', 'ok.ts'));
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('不扫无锚点文件（scannedFiles 计数，files 只含含锚点）', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'empty.ts': 'export const x = 1;\n',
      'anchored.ts': '// @TRED_START: A | fp=11111111\n// @TRED_END: A\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = verifyDir({ dir });
      assert.equal(r.scannedFiles, 2);
      assert.equal(r.files.length, 1);
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });

  it('report 可 JSON 序列化', function () {
    const dir = setup({
      'a.ts': '// @TRED_START: X | fp=aaaaaaaa\n// @TRED_END: X\n',
    });
    try {
      const r = verifyDir({ dir });
      const j = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(r));
      assert.equal(j.ok, true);
      assert.equal(typeof j.scannedFiles, 'number');
    } finally { rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true }); }
  });
});
